# B2 Edge (Tenancy) Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (- [ ]) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Put the B2 tenancy kernel (built by `2026-11-09-b2-core.md`) on every edge: billing rows carry and are
scoped by `tenant_id` (Citus-ready, with the ledger design's B2 additions); the principal crosses gRPC hops and Kafka
messages; tenants, members and invitations get REST, GraphQL and `identity.v1` RPCs in both topologies, with
last-owner protection and an invitation mail that finally has a link (F34); fakes, e2e journeys and docs follow.

**Architecture:** Four edge lanes in git worktrees, around the six core lanes. Lane B (`b2/billing`: Tasks 1-2,
sole owner of the billing migration set) ∥ Lane P (`b2/propagation`: Tasks 3-5 in `libs/transport`,
`libs/contracts`, `libs/graphql`) ∥ Lane I (`b2/identity-edge`: Tasks 6-12, serial) → Lane J (`b2/journeys`: Tasks
13-14, after every other B2 lane). Billing threads the tenant explicitly (`tenant_id` in `billing.v1` requests,
copied from `req.user.tenantId`, the same trust model as today's `user_id`) and cross-checks it against the CLS
principal once B2-23 propagates it. Identity's tenancy API is three ports (`TenantsPort`, `MembersPort`,
`InvitationsPort`) with local and gRPC adapters over one new `identity.v1.TenantsService`. RLS is not enforced
(B3); every query already carries its distribution column (sharding design §2.1).

**Tech Stack:** NestJS 12, Fastify 5, Drizzle ORM 0.45.4 + drizzle-kit 0.31.11, nestjs-cls 7.0.1, `@nestjs/cqrs`,
Apollo/`@nestjs/graphql`, gRPC (ts-proto, buf), Kafka (kafkajs), BullMQ mail queue (`@platform/mailer`), Stripe
22.6.2, zod 4, Vitest 5, Testcontainers Citus (`startTestPostgres()` from `@platform/testing/postgres`).

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-12-domains-tenancy-rls-program-design.md` (§4, §5.1, §5.2, §7, §9, §13,
§16); `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-09-sharding-citus-pgbouncer-design.md` (§2.1, §2.3, §8);
`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-09-stripe-ledger-immudb-design.md` (§1.2 S-2/S-4/S-6, §8.1, D5). Contract:
`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-09-b2-core.md` "Interfaces (B2 contract)" I-1..I-12. Inventory:
`docs/superpowers/plans/program-work/inventory.md` (B2-6, B2-12, B2-13, B2-14, B2-16, B2-17, B2-23..B2-29,
AUDIT-F23, F27, F29, F34). Conventions: `docs/superpowers/plans/program-work/conventions.md`.

## Executive summary

- **14 tasks, four lanes, one signed commit each.** Lane B `b2/billing` (1-2, sole owner of the billing migration
  set) ∥ Lane P `b2/propagation` (3-5) ∥ Lane I `b2/identity-edge` (6-12, serial) → Lane J `b2/journeys` (13-14).
- **Task 1 (B2-6):** billing migration `0001_tenant_columns` (hand-edited): `payments` PK `(tenant_id, id)`, uniques
  `(tenant_id, stripe_checkout_session_id)` and `(tenant_id, user_id, idempotency_key)`, index `(tenant_id, user_id,
id)`, `request_hash`; `stripe_events` PK `(tenant_id, id)` with nullable `user_id`; statuses `refunded`,
  `partially_refunded`, `disputed`; pre-B2 rows move to `PLATFORM_TENANT_ID`. Every query carries `tenant_id`;
  `billing.v1` requests gain `tenant_id`; checkout signs `{ paymentId, userId, tenantId }` into session and
  PaymentIntent metadata; the webhook resolves the tenant from signed metadata (`stripeOwnerOf`). Next billing
  index `0002` (SH-6).
- **Task 2 (B2-26):** checkout and listing require a principal acting in the request tenant (`TENANT_MISMATCH`);
  `all=true` = the whole active tenant with `billing:read-all`, re-granted to admins and recomputed service-side;
  the webhook runs as the signed principal; `billing.payment-succeeded.v1` is tenant-scoped; replays compare the
  request hash (ledger S-2); F29 proto comment.
- **Task 3 (B2-23):** `x-tenant-id`/`x-staff` metadata built only from the CLS principal (never request headers; an
  override for another user drops tenant and roles); `GrpcContextInterceptor` rebuilds the principal (fail closed:
  `INVALID_PRINCIPAL`); `principalPermissions()`; `RbacModule` in Grpc modules and gRPC-only services (I-5).
- **Task 4 (B2-24, F27):** envelope `tenantId` (nil = platform) and `actorId`; `defineEventContract(…, {
tenantScoped })`; producer defaults from CLS; consumers restore `{ userId: actorId, tenantId }`; Kafka doc drift.
- **Task 5 (B2-25):** a spec pins that DataLoader batches see the request principal (fix included if it fails).
- **Tasks 6-11 (B2-12, 13, 14, 16, 17; F34):** `identity.v1.TenantsService` (9 RPCs, additive); three ports with
  local and gRPC adapters; REST `/v1/tenants`, `/v1/tenant`, `/v1/tenant/members`, `/v1/tenant/leave`,
  `/v1/tenant/invitations`, `/v1/invitations/accept`; GraphQL `myTenants`, `tenantMembers`, `me { activeTenant }`,
  seven mutations. Every handler cross-checks the principal (`requireTenantActor`, `requireSelf`). Owner rank and a
  per-tenant `pg_advisory_xact_lock` (`LAST_OWNER`; actor roles re-read after the lock, from the membership row).
  Invitations: hashed 32-byte token, 7 days, single use, email match; `tenant-invitation` mail on the shared queue,
  job deleted once sent or failed; accept link from optional `MAIL_APP_URL` (https in production; fixes F34).
- **Tasks 12-14 (B2-27..29, F23, F29):** gateway fakes with the real rules (staff acts with token roles); journeys
  in both topologies; docs plus a scripts spec pinning the error catalogue.
- **Dependencies:** workspace and catalog only (`@platform/auth` in two service apps; `@platform/mailer` in identity
  and identity-service; `@domain/identity-contracts` in notifications-service; `@nestjs/bullmq` dev in
  identity-service); the controller runs `bun install`.
- **Must precede B2:** G Tasks 1-4, C wave 1, S0, E Tasks 1-2 (as for core), plus ledger L-7 and L-8a before Lane B.
- **Merge order:** Lane P after core `b2/context`; Lane B Task 1 after core `b2/tokens`, Task 2 after Lane P; Lane I
  after core `b2/flows` and `b2/context`, Task 11 after Lane P; Lane J last. SH-6 after `b2/billing`.
- **Review 2026-11-10:** 26 defects fixed in place (5 security, the rest executability and consistency); see "Plan
  review (2026-11-10)" at the end.

I-5 obligations this plan fulfils: B2-23 adds `RbacModule.forFeature(<X>_RBAC)` to every Grpc module and
`RbacModule.forRoot()` to the gRPC-only services (Task 3); B2-26 re-grants `BillingPermission.ReadAll` to `admin` in
the commit that scopes listing to the tenant (Task 2); B2-13 owns the per-tenant owner rules (Task 8). I-7: B2-13
implements `updateRoles`, `delete`, `countOwnersLocked` and `listMembers` in core's memberships files (Task 8).

## Global Constraints

- **Node.** Prefix every command (tooling, tests, `git commit`) with
  `export NVM_DIR="$HOME/.nvm"; . "$NVM_DIR/nvm.sh" >/dev/null; nvm use 24.4.0 >/dev/null;` and run it from the
  worktree root. Under Node 20 the pre-commit hook cannot load `lint-staged.config.ts`.
- **Upstream state.** Every path, import and signature here is written against `feat/domains-tenancy` after: G
  Tasks 1-4 (G-11: `src/api/index.ts` entries, the placement rule, `api-surface.spec.ts` `API_VALUES`, the
  gateway imports a domain only via `@domain/<x>/api`, `grpcCall` operations are
  `` `${<X>_GRPC_PACKAGE.package}.${<SERVICE>_NAME}/<Method>` ``); C wave 1 (credential endpoints carry
  `@RateLimit(IDENTITY_RATE_LIMITS.CREDENTIALS)` with the identifier in `email`, credential handlers throw); S0
  (`startTestPostgres()` on `citusdata/citus:14.2.0`, platform migration `0001`); E Tasks 1-2 (`bun run db:check`,
  `scripts/libs-vocabulary.spec.ts`); ledger wave 0 L-7 (Stripe S-1/S-3/S-5/S-8/S-10: `boundSession`,
  `retrieveCheckoutSession`, the amount-mismatch and API-version counters) and L-8a (`domains/billing/src/domain/
ledger/ledger.types.ts`); and the core lanes each task names under **Wave**. Line numbers are hints from
  `9600510`; match every edit on the quoted text.
- **Worktrees and merges.** One git worktree per lane under the session scratchpad (never inside the repo), cut from
  `feat/domains-tenancy` when the lane's wave opens. Branches: `b2/billing` (Lane B), `b2/propagation` (Lane P),
  `b2/identity-edge` (Lane I), `b2/journeys` (Lane J). The controller previews each merge with
  `git merge-tree --write-tree feat/domains-tenancy <branch>`, merges one lane at a time with
  `git merge --no-ff -S`, subject `chore(repo): merge <branch> — <summary>`, then runs `bun run check`,
  `bun run test` and `bun run db:check`. Never push, never open a PR, never merge to `master`.
- **Commits.** One GPG-signed commit per task (`git commit -S`), never `--no-verify`. **No attribution lines** (no
  `Co-Authored-By`, no trailer); the program decision of 2026-11-09 overrides `conventions.md` §1.2/§4.2. Header
  `<type>(<scope>[,<scope>]): <subject>`, at most 100 characters, subject starting lower-case, no trailing period;
  body lines at most 100 characters. Types: `build chore ci docs feat fix perf refactor revert style test`. Scopes
  used here: `billing`, `billing-contracts`, `identity`, `identity-contracts`, `notifications`, `transport`,
  `contracts`, `graphql`, `auth`, `mailer`, `config`, `monolith`, `gateway`, `identity-service`, `billing-service`,
  `notifications-service`, `docs`, `repo`. `b2`, `tenancy`, `rbac`, `kafka`, `grpc`, `scripts` are NOT scopes. Every
  commit step writes the message to `$TMPDIR/b2e-msg.txt`, checks it with `bunx commitlint < "$TMPDIR/b2e-msg.txt"`
  (no `✖`), then runs `git commit -S -F "$TMPDIR/b2e-msg.txt"`.
- **Search.** `rg` and `fd`; `jaq` for JSON. `grep`, `find` and `jq` are blocked by a hook.
- **Tests.** Unit: `npx vitest run --project <pkg> <path filter>` (projects are folder names: `billing`,
  `billing-contracts`, `identity`, `identity-contracts`, `notifications`, `transport`, `contracts`, `graphql`,
  `auth`, `mailer`, `config`, `scripts`, `monolith`, `gateway`, `identity-service`, `billing-service`,
  `notifications-service`). Integration: `INTEGRATION=1 npx vitest run --project billing:int <filter>` /
  `--project identity:int` (Docker, Citus via `startTestPostgres()`, sandbox off). App e2e:
  `npx vitest run --project monolith:e2e` / `gateway:e2e` / `identity-service:e2e` / `billing-service:e2e` (sandbox
  off: they bind loopback ports). Full: `bun run test` (sandbox off). Int specs that check tenant isolation start
  Postgres with `startTestPostgres({ localExecution: false })`.
- **Gates before every commit.** `bun run check` (biome, eslint `--max-warnings=0`, prettier, tsc, boundaries,
  api-subpaths) → exit 0. After the migration task: `bun run db:check` → exit 0 and `bun run db:generate` leaves
  `git status --porcelain -- domains/billing/src/infrastructure/persistence/migrations` empty. After a proto task:
  `bun run proto:lint && bun run proto:gen` leave no diff after the commit's own regeneration, and the change stays
  additive (new fields, messages and RPCs only; never delete, renumber or retype; no `deprecated` options because
  ts-proto turns them into `@deprecated` and `no-deprecated` fails the gate): when E Task 5 has merged run
  `node scripts/check-proto-breaking.mjs --against feat/domains-tenancy`, otherwise E-5's verified `git archive`
  form (buf's own `.git#branch=…` input cannot read a worktree, whose `.git` is a file; core's review fixed the same):
  `rm -rf "$TMPDIR/proto-base" && mkdir -p "$TMPDIR/proto-base" && git archive feat/domains-tenancy domains/<x>/contracts/src/proto | tar -x -C "$TMPDIR/proto-base" && (cd domains/<x>/contracts && npx buf breaking --config ../../../libs/contracts/buf.yaml --against "$TMPDIR/proto-base/domains/<x>/contracts" --against-config ../../../libs/contracts/buf.yaml)`
  → exit 0. After an env change: `node scripts/check-env-example.mjs` → exit 0. After a Kafka contract change:
  `bun run build && node scripts/docker/check-kafka-topics.mjs` → exit 0.
- **Formatting.** After editing TypeScript run `npx biome check --write <files>`; after Markdown/YAML run
  `npx prettier --write <files>`.
- **Layering.** `apps → domains → contracts → libs` (`scripts/check-boundaries.mjs`). `libs/` names no domain, also
  in specs and fixtures (`scripts/libs-vocabulary.spec.ts`: write `orders`, `catalog`, `alerts`, `shipments`,
  `acmepay`, `dispatch`; never `identity`, `billing`, `notifications`, `payment(s)`, `stripe`, `checkout`). G-11
  placement: ports, presentation, adapters, edge constants and permission vocabularies → `src/api/index.ts` (and
  `API_VALUES` in `src/api/api-surface.spec.ts`); commands, handlers, domain types and errors, persistence, Core
  modules, domain config namespaces → root `src/index.ts`; fakes → `src/testing/index.ts`. A file reachable from
  `src/api/index.ts` never imports a root-only file. The gateway imports identity only through
  `@domain/identity/api`.
- **Migrations.** Only Task 1 writes a migration: billing `0001_tenant_columns`, generated with
  `(cd domains/billing && npx drizzle-kit generate --name tenant_columns)` (offline), then hand-edited exactly as
  the task says (`db:check` compares snapshots, so SQL hand edits are safe). Never edit a committed migration. If
  drizzle-kit asks an interactive question, stop: the schema edit is bigger than the task says. **Next free billing
  index after this plan: `0002`** (SH-6 distribution, then B3-5/6, G-1, L-10 in that order). No identity migration in
  this plan (core's next free identity index stays `0008`). Billing tables are Citus-ready (`// citus: tenant`, PK
  and every unique start with `tenant_id`, no FK to another domain schema) but not distributed.
- **Dependencies (controller runs `bun install`; the task commits `bun.lock` with its change).** Task 3:
  `apps/billing-service/package.json` and `apps/notifications-service/package.json` `dependencies` +
  `"@platform/auth": "workspace:*"` (for `RbacModule.forRoot()`). Task 9:
  `domains/identity/package.json` `dependencies` + `"@platform/mailer": "workspace:*"`;
  `apps/identity-service/package.json` `dependencies` + `"@platform/mailer": "workspace:*"`;
  `apps/notifications-service/package.json` `dependencies` + `"@domain/identity-contracts": "workspace:*"`;
  `apps/identity-service/package.json` `devDependencies` + `"@nestjs/bullmq": "catalog:"` (its e2e specs override
  `getQueueToken(MAIL_QUEUE)`). No third-party package outside the catalog and no new catalog version.
- **Code style.** Relative imports end in `.js`; no `any`; no `console.*`; `override` on subclass members; `T[]`;
  ids from `generateId()`; every import declared in that package's `package.json`; injected classes imported as
  values.
- **C contract.** `req.user.tenantId` / `socket.data.user.tenantId` are read as-is (core Task 10). Invitation
  acceptance does not create an account or set a password, so it is not a credential endpoint and gets no
  `@RateLimit(IDENTITY_RATE_LIMITS.CREDENTIALS)`; every handler here throws on failure.
- **E contract.** The smoke journey `POST /v1/auth/register` → 201 with `accessToken`, then `GET /v1/auth/me` → 200
  with the same `email` keeps working after every task.
- **Docker.** Never touch the developer's containers or the `boilerplate` compose project; Testcontainers pick random
  ports.

## Review Focus

Five input classes that would bite users and that no happy-path test exercises. Each has a named test in its owning
task.

1. **Billing scope bypass.** `all=true` from an admin of tenant A; the same `Idempotency-Key` used by one user in two
   tenants; a webhook whose signed metadata names tenant B for a payment of tenant A; a webhook with no or garbage
   tenant metadata (legacy session, `payout.paid`); a gRPC request whose `tenant_id` differs from the propagated
   principal's. Expected: A's rows only; two payments; payment untouched and acknowledged; a `PLATFORM_TENANT_ID`
   `stripe_events` row with `user_id` NULL; 403 `TENANT_MISMATCH`. Tests: Task 1
   (`billing.repositories.int-spec.ts`, `handle-stripe-webhook.handler.spec.ts`), Task 2
   (`list-payments.handler.spec.ts`, `create-checkout-session.handler.spec.ts`).
2. **Hostile gRPC metadata.** `x-user-id` not a UUID; `x-tenant-id` nil, malformed or present without `x-user-id`;
   `x-staff: TRUE` / `1` / `yes`; `x-user-roles: owner,root,owner`; no metadata at all. Expected: `UNAUTHENTICATED`
   `INVALID_PRINCIPAL` for a user with a bad tenant, the tenant ignored without a user, `staff` true only for the
   exact string `true`, unknown roles dropped, no principal (not an error) without a user. Test: Task 3
   `grpc-principal.spec.ts`.
3. **Legacy and malformed envelopes.** An envelope without `tenantId`/`actorId` (in flight during a deploy),
   `actorId: null`, `tenantId` = the nil UUID, `tenantId` not a UUID, and a tenant-scoped contract published outside
   any principal. Expected: the first three parse and the consumer sets no principal; the fourth fails validation
   (dead-lettered); the producer throws `INVALID_EVENT` for the last unless the caller passes `tenantId`. Test: Task
   4 `envelope-tenancy.spec.ts`, `kafka-context.interceptor.spec.ts`.
4. **Last-owner and owner-rank abuse.** The sole owner demotes themself, leaves, or is removed; two owners demote each
   other concurrently; an admin grants `owner`, demotes an owner or removes an owner; a member edits roles; roles `[]`
   or `['root']`. Expected: 422 `LAST_OWNER` (exactly one of the concurrent demotions succeeds), 403
   `OWNER_RANK_REQUIRED`, 403 `FORBIDDEN`, 400/422 `INVALID_ROLES`. Tests: Task 8
   (`update-member-roles.handler.spec.ts`, `tenancy.repositories.int-spec.ts` concurrency block).
5. **Invitation token misuse.** Accept with another account's email (case and whitespace variants of the right one
   must pass), an expired token, a replayed (already accepted) token, an unknown or malformed token, an invitee who
   is already a member, two concurrent accepts, and a staff switch-in token. Expected: 403 `INVITATION_EMAIL_MISMATCH`,
   404 `INVITATION_INVALID` (expired, replayed and unknown look the same), 400 for a malformed token, 409
   `MEMBERSHIP_EXISTS` (the invitation is not consumed), exactly one membership, 403 `TENANT_ACCESS_DENIED` for
   staff. Test: Task 9 `accept-invitation.handler.spec.ts`, int block.

### Merge order around core

Core: `b2/platform` (Tasks 1-2) → `b2/schema` (3-9) → `b2/tokens` (10-13) → `b2/context` (17-18), `b2/flows`
(14-16), `b2/ops` (19). Edge: Lane P after `b2/context`; Lane B Task 1 after `b2/tokens` (and L-7, L-8a), Task 2
after Lane P merged, then merge `b2/billing`; Lane I after `b2/flows` and `b2/context` (Task 11 also after Lane P);
Lane J after Lanes B, P and I.
SH-6 (billing distribution) starts after `b2/billing` merges; SH-5 (identity) needs nothing from this plan.

```ts
// billing.v1 (additive): CreateCheckoutSessionRequest.tenant_id = 8, ListPaymentsRequest.tenant_id = 4,
// Payment.tenant_id = 11 → ts-proto `tenantId: string` on all three.
// domain/payment-status.enum.ts
export enum PaymentStatus { Pending = 'pending', Succeeded = 'succeeded', Failed = 'failed', Expired = 'expired',
  Refunded = 'refunded', PartiallyRefunded = 'partially_refunded', Disputed = 'disputed' }
// domain/payment.aggregate.ts
interface PaymentSnapshot { readonly tenantId: string; /* … */ readonly requestHash: string | null; }
interface InitiatePaymentProps { id: string; tenantId: string; userId: string; priceId: string; quantity: number;
  idempotencyKey?: string | null | undefined; requestHash?: string | null | undefined; now: Date; }
Payment#tenantId: string; Payment#requestHash: string | null;
// domain/stripe-owner.ts
export interface StripeOwner { readonly tenantId: string; readonly userId: string | null }
export const PLATFORM_OWNER: StripeOwner; // { tenantId: PLATFORM_TENANT_ID, userId: null }
export function stripeOwnerOf(object: unknown): StripeOwner;
// application/repositories
interface ListPaymentsCriteria { tenantId: string; userId?: string | undefined; limit: number; cursor?: string | undefined }
abstract class PaymentsRepository {
  create(payment: Payment): Promise<CreatePaymentResult>; // unique (tenant_id, user_id, idempotency_key)
  findById(tenantId: string, id: string): Promise<Payment | null>;
  findForCheckoutSession(ref: { tenantId: string; paymentId: string | null; sessionId: string }): Promise<Payment | null>;
  save(payment: Payment): Promise<void>; // WHERE tenant_id AND id AND version
  list(criteria: ListPaymentsCriteria): Promise<CursorPage<Payment>>;
}
interface StripeEventReceipt { tenantId: string; userId: string | null; id: string; type: string }
// application/queries/list-payments/list-payments.query.ts
class ListPaymentsQuery { constructor(criteria: { tenantId: string; userId?: string; limit?: number; cursor?: string }) }
// test/billing-database.ts (int specs only)
export function startBillingDatabase(providers: Provider[], options?: { runMigrations?: boolean; localExecution?: boolean }): Promise<BillingDatabase>;
```

Tables after `0001` (sharding design §2.3; `// citus: tenant`, not distributed): `billing.payments` PK
`payments_tenant_id_id_pk (tenant_id, id)`, unique indexes `payments_tenant_id_stripe_checkout_session_id_uq
  (tenant_id, stripe_checkout_session_id)` and `payments_tenant_id_user_id_idempotency_key_uq (tenant_id, user_id,
  idempotency_key)`, index `payments_tenant_id_user_id_id_idx (tenant_id, user_id, id)`, new columns `tenant_id uuid
  NOT NULL`, `request_hash text`; `billing.stripe_events` PK `stripe_events_tenant_id_id_pk (tenant_id, id)`, new
columns `tenant_id uuid NOT NULL`, `user_id uuid` (nullable), index `stripe_events_processed_at_idx` kept; enum
`billing.payment_status` + `refunded`, `partially_refunded`, `disputed`. Rows that existed before `0001` get
`tenant_id = PLATFORM_TENANT_ID` (no tenant can list them; B3's system pool can).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.** Create `domains/billing/src/domain/stripe-owner.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { generateId, PLATFORM_TENANT_ID } from '@platform/common';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { PLATFORM_OWNER, stripeOwnerOf } from './stripe-owner.js';

describe('stripeOwnerOf', () => {
  const tenantId = generateId();
  const userId = generateId();

  it('reads the tenant and user we signed into the object metadata', () => {
    expect(stripeOwnerOf({ metadata: { tenantId, userId, paymentId: generateId() } })).toEqual({
      tenantId,
      userId,
    });
  });

  it('keeps the tenant and drops a user that is not a UUID', () => {
    expect(stripeOwnerOf({ metadata: { tenantId, userId: 'usr_1' } })).toEqual({
      tenantId,
      userId: null,
    });
  });

  it.each([
    ['no metadata (payout.paid)', { object: 'payout' }],
    ['empty metadata (a session created before B2)', { metadata: {} }],
    ['a tenant that is not a UUID', { metadata: { tenantId: 'acme', userId } }],
    ['the nil UUID', { metadata: { tenantId: PLATFORM_TENANT_ID, userId } }],
    ['null', null],
  ])('falls back to the platform owner for %s', (_label, object) => {
    expect(stripeOwnerOf(object)).toBe(PLATFORM_OWNER);
    expect(PLATFORM_OWNER).toEqual({ tenantId: PLATFORM_TENANT_ID, userId: null });
  });
});
```

```ts
  const base = {
    id: generateId(),
    userId: generateId(),
    priceId: 'price_pro',
    quantity: 1,
    now: new Date(),
  };

  it('belongs to the tenant it was initiated in and keeps the request hash', () => {

      tenantId: TEST_TENANT_ID,
      requestHash: 'a'.repeat(64),

      requestHash: 'a'.repeat(64),
    });

  it.each([PLATFORM_TENANT_ID, 'not-a-uuid', ''])('refuses tenant %j', (tenantId) => {
    expect(() => Payment.initiate({ ...base, tenantId })).toThrow(InvalidPaymentException);
  });
});
```

```ts
it('signs paymentId, userId and tenantId into the session metadata (B2-26, ledger D5)', async () => {
  const result = await handler.execute(new CreateCheckoutSessionCommand(request()));

  expect(stripe.createCheckoutSession).toHaveBeenCalledWith(

it('scopes the Idempotency-Key to the tenant: one key in two tenants makes two payments', async () => {
  const otherTenant = generateId();
  const first = await handler.execute(
    new CreateCheckoutSessionCommand(request({ idempotencyKey: 'order-42' })),
  );
  const second = await handler.execute(
    new CreateCheckoutSessionCommand(
      request({ idempotencyKey: 'order-42', tenantId: otherTenant }),
    ),
  );

  expect(second.paymentId).not.toBe(first.paymentId);

});
```

The existing specs of this file must keep resolving `payment`, which now lives in `TEST_TENANT_ID`: a session
without tenant metadata resolves to the platform tenant and finds nothing. So, in the outer `describe`, add

```ts
/** The metadata checkout signs into the session (B2-26): what the webhook resolves the tenant from. */
const signedMetadata = () => ({
  paymentId: payment.id,
  userId: payment.userId,
  tenantId: TEST_TENANT_ID,
});
```

and pass `metadata: signedMetadata()` in **every** existing `makeCheckoutSession({ … })` call of the file: the
`completed()` helper and the `async_payment_succeeded`, `async_payment_failed` and `expired` tests. In the test
"acknowledges events for unknown payments and impossible transitions" the first delivery becomes
`completed({ id: 'cs_unknown', client_reference_id: null, metadata: { userId: payment.userId, tenantId: TEST_TENANT_ID } })`
(no `paymentId`: otherwise `paymentIdOf` falls back to the metadata and finds the payment). Then replace every
`store.state.events.get('<id>')` / `.has('<id>')` with the composite key `` `${TEST_TENANT_ID}:<id>` `` for events
whose object carries `signedMetadata()`, and `` `${PLATFORM_TENANT_ID}:<id>` `` for the `customer.created` event
(no metadata); the receipt the first test expects becomes `{ tenantId: TEST_TENANT_ID, userId: payment.userId, id:
'evt_completed_1', type: 'checkout.session.completed' }`.

Append to `billing.migrations.spec.ts` (inside the outer `describe`; `readFileSync` from `node:fs`, `join` from
`node:path`, `PLATFORM_TENANT_ID` from `@platform/common`):

```ts
describe('0001_tenant_columns (hand-edited)', () => {
  const text = readFileSync(join(billingMigrations.folder, '0001_tenant_columns.sql'), 'utf8');

  it('backfills rows that predate tenancy to the platform tenant, then drops the default', () => {
    for (const table of ['payments', 'stripe_events']) {
      expect(text).toContain(
        `ALTER TABLE "billing"."${table}" ADD COLUMN "tenant_id" uuid DEFAULT '${PLATFORM_TENANT_ID}'::uuid NOT NULL;`,
      );
      expect(text).toContain(
        `ALTER TABLE "billing"."${table}" ALTER COLUMN "tenant_id" DROP DEFAULT;`,
      );
    }
  });

  it('drops the old column-level primary keys before adding the tenant-leading ones', () => {
    for (const table of ['payments', 'stripe_events']) {
      const drop = text.indexOf(
        `ALTER TABLE "billing"."${table}" DROP CONSTRAINT "${table}_pkey";`,
      );
      const add = text.indexOf(
        `ADD CONSTRAINT "${table}_tenant_id_id_pk" PRIMARY KEY("tenant_id","id")`,
      );
      expect(drop).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
      expect(add).toBeGreaterThan(drop);
    }
  });

  it('adds the three ledger payment statuses (ledger design S-6, D5)', () => {
    for (const value of ['refunded', 'partially_refunded', 'disputed']) {
      expect(text).toContain(`ALTER TYPE "billing"."payment_status" ADD VALUE '${value}';`);
    }
  });
});
```

Create `domains/billing/test/billing-database.ts`:

```ts
import { type INestApplicationContext, Module, type Provider } from '@nestjs/common';
import { NestFactory } from '@nestjs/core';
import { AppConfigModule } from '@platform/config';
import { DatabaseModule, DRIZZLE, type DrizzleDB } from '@platform/database';
import { startTestPostgres } from '@platform/testing/postgres';
import { ClsModule } from 'nestjs-cls';
import { vi } from 'vitest';
import { billingMigrations } from '../src/infrastructure/persistence/billing.migrations.js';
import {
  type BillingSchema,
  billingSchema,
} from '../src/infrastructure/persistence/billing.schema.js';

export interface BillingDatabase {
  readonly app: INestApplicationContext;
  readonly db: DrizzleDB<BillingSchema>;
  close(): Promise<void>;
}

/**
 * A fresh Citus 14.2 database with the platform set and billing's history applied by the real
 * `DatabaseModule` (unless `runMigrations: false`), and `providers` on top. Int specs only.
 * Tenant-isolation specs pass `localExecution: false` (S0, sharding design §6).
 */
export async function startBillingDatabase(
  providers: Provider[],
  options: { runMigrations?: boolean; localExecution?: boolean } = {},
): Promise<BillingDatabase> {
  const pg = await startTestPostgres({ localExecution: options.localExecution ?? true });
  vi.stubEnv('DATABASE_URL', pg.url);
  vi.stubEnv('SERVICE_NAME', 'billing-int');

  @Module({
    imports: [
      AppConfigModule.forRoot(),
      ClsModule.forRoot({ global: true }),
      DatabaseModule.forRootAsync({
        schema: billingSchema,
        migrations: [billingMigrations],
        runMigrations: options.runMigrations ?? true,
      }),
    ],
    providers,
  })
  class BillingIntModule {}

  try {
    const app = await NestFactory.createApplicationContext(BillingIntModule, {
      logger: false,
      abortOnError: false,
    });
    return {
      app,
      db: app.get<DrizzleDB<BillingSchema>>(DRIZZLE),
      close: async () => {
        await app.close();
        await pg.stop();
        vi.unstubAllEnvs();
      },
    };
  } catch (error) {
    await pg.stop();
    vi.unstubAllEnvs();
    throw error;
  }
}
```

Create `domains/billing/src/infrastructure/persistence/billing.repositories.int-spec.ts`:

```ts
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { generateId, PLATFORM_TENANT_ID } from '@platform/common';
import { sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { type BillingDatabase, startBillingDatabase } from '../../../test/billing-database.js';
import { makePayment } from '../../../test/billing-test.utils.js';
import { billingMigrations } from './billing.migrations.js';
import { DrizzlePaymentsRepository } from './drizzle-payments.repository.js';
import { DrizzleStripeEventsRepository } from './drizzle-stripe-events.repository.js';

/*
 * Billing repositories against Citus 14.2 (single node, local execution off: tenant isolation) with
 * billing's real history. Opt-in: INTEGRATION=1 npx vitest run --project billing:int repositories
 * Not distributed here (SH-6 distributes); every query already carries tenant_id (sharding §2.1.5).
 */
const statements = (file: string): string[] =>
  readFileSync(join(billingMigrations.folder, file), 'utf8')
    .split('--> statement-breakpoint')
    .map((statement) => statement.trim())
    .filter((statement) => statement !== '');

describe.skipIf(process.env['INTEGRATION'] !== '1')('billing repositories on Citus', () => {
  let database: BillingDatabase | undefined;
  let payments: DrizzlePaymentsRepository;
  let events: DrizzleStripeEventsRepository;
  const tenantA = generateId();
  const tenantB = generateId();
  const user = generateId();

  beforeAll(async () => {
    database = await startBillingDatabase(
      [DrizzlePaymentsRepository, DrizzleStripeEventsRepository],
      {
        localExecution: false,
      },
    );
    payments = database.app.get(DrizzlePaymentsRepository);
    events = database.app.get(DrizzleStripeEventsRepository);
  });

  afterAll(async () => {
    await database?.close();
  });

  it('lists, finds and locks only inside the tenant', async () => {
    const a = makePayment({ tenantId: tenantA, userId: user });
    const b = makePayment({ tenantId: tenantB, userId: user });
    await payments.create(a);
    await payments.create(b);

    const listA = await payments.list({ tenantId: tenantA, limit: 10 });
    expect(listA.items.map((p) => p.id)).toEqual([a.id]);
    await expect(payments.findById(tenantB, a.id)).resolves.toBeNull();
    await expect(payments.findById(tenantA, a.id)).resolves.toMatchObject({ tenantId: tenantA });
    await expect(
      payments.findForCheckoutSession({ tenantId: tenantB, paymentId: a.id, sessionId: 'cs_none' }),
    ).resolves.toBeNull();
  });

  it('dedupes an Idempotency-Key per (tenant, user): the same key in another tenant is a new payment', async () => {
    const first = await payments.create(
      makePayment({ tenantId: tenantA, userId: user, idempotencyKey: 'k-1' }),
    );
    const replay = await payments.create(
      makePayment({ tenantId: tenantA, userId: user, idempotencyKey: 'k-1' }),
    );
    const other = await payments.create(
      makePayment({ tenantId: tenantB, userId: user, idempotencyKey: 'k-1' }),
    );

    expect(first.created).toBe(true);
    expect(replay).toMatchObject({ created: false, payment: { id: first.payment.id } });
    expect(other.created).toBe(true);
  });

  it('records Stripe events per tenant, with a NULL user for platform events', async () => {
    await expect(
      events.markProcessed({ tenantId: tenantA, userId: user, id: 'evt_1', type: 't' }),
    ).resolves.toBe(true);
    await expect(
      events.markProcessed({ tenantId: tenantA, userId: user, id: 'evt_1', type: 't' }),
    ).resolves.toBe(false);
    await expect(
      events.markProcessed({
        tenantId: PLATFORM_TENANT_ID,
        userId: null,
        id: 'evt_payout',
        type: 'payout.paid',
      }),
    ).resolves.toBe(true);
    const rows = await database!.db.execute<{ tenant_id: string; user_id: string | null }>(
      sql`select tenant_id, user_id from billing.stripe_events where id = 'evt_payout'`,
    );
    expect([...rows]).toEqual([{ tenant_id: PLATFORM_TENANT_ID, user_id: null }]);
  });

  describe('0001 on a database that already has payments', () => {
    let legacy: BillingDatabase | undefined;

    beforeAll(async () => {
      legacy = await startBillingDatabase([], { runMigrations: false });
      for (const statement of statements('0000_init.sql'))
        await legacy.db.execute(sql.raw(statement));
      await legacy.db.execute(
        sql.raw(
          `insert into billing.payments (user_id, price_id, quantity) values ('${user}', 'price_old', 1)`,
        ),
      );
      await legacy.db.execute(
        sql.raw(`insert into billing.stripe_events (id, type) values ('evt_old', 'x')`),
      );
      for (const statement of statements('0001_tenant_columns.sql'))
        await legacy.db.execute(sql.raw(statement));
    });

    afterAll(async () => {
      await legacy?.close();
    });

    it('moves pre-tenancy rows to the platform tenant and keeps tenant_id NOT NULL without a default', async () => {
      const rows = await legacy!.db.execute<{ tenant_id: string }>(
        sql`select tenant_id from billing.payments union all select tenant_id from billing.stripe_events`,
      );
      expect([...rows].map((r) => r.tenant_id)).toEqual([PLATFORM_TENANT_ID, PLATFORM_TENANT_ID]);
      // drizzle-orm 0.45 wraps driver errors in DrizzleQueryError ("Failed query: …"); the
      // postgres.js error (SQLSTATE 23502 not_null_violation) is its `cause`.
      await expect(
        legacy!.db.execute(
          sql.raw(
            `insert into billing.payments (user_id, price_id, quantity) values ('${user}', 'p', 1)`,
          ),
        ),
      ).rejects.toMatchObject({
        cause: expect.objectContaining({ code: '23502', column_name: 'tenant_id' }),
      });
    });
  });
});
```

```ts
// citus: tenant (distributed by tenant_id in SH-6). No FK to identity: tenant_id and user_id are plain
// columns so billing's migration set stays self-contained (sharding design §2.1.3).
export const payments = billingPgSchema.table(
  'payments',
  {
    tenantId: uuid().notNull(),
    // The application always supplies a uuidv7 (Payment.initiate); the default covers raw SQL.
    id: uuid()
      .notNull()
      .default(sql`uuidv7()`),
    userId: uuid().notNull(),
    priceId: text().notNull(),
    quantity: integer().notNull(),
    // Minor units. int8 read as a JS number (exact up to 2^53); NULL until Stripe priced the session.
    amountTotal: bigint({ mode: 'number' }),
    currency: text(),
    status: paymentStatusEnum().notNull().default(PaymentStatus.Pending),
    stripeCheckoutSessionId: text(),
    stripePaymentIntentId: text(),
    // Client Idempotency-Key, unique per (tenant, user). NULLs never collide.
    idempotencyKey: text(),
    // sha256 hex of the normalized checkout request (ledger S-2); NULL for rows that predate it.
    requestHash: text(),
    paidAt: timestamptz(),
    // Optimistic lock (UPDATE ... WHERE version = $expected).
    version: integer().notNull().default(0),
    createdAt: timestamptz().notNull().defaultNow(),
    updatedAt: timestamptz()
      .notNull()
      .defaultNow()
      .$onUpdate(() => new Date()),
  },
  (t) => [
    primaryKey({ name: 'payments_tenant_id_id_pk', columns: [t.tenantId, t.id] }),
    // "My payments in this tenant, newest first": WHERE tenant_id = $1 AND user_id = $2 ORDER BY id DESC.
    index('payments_tenant_id_user_id_id_idx').on(t.tenantId, t.userId, t.id),
    uniqueIndex('payments_tenant_id_stripe_checkout_session_id_uq').on(
      t.tenantId,
      t.stripeCheckoutSessionId,
    ),
    uniqueIndex('payments_tenant_id_user_id_idempotency_key_uq').on(
      t.tenantId,
      t.userId,
      t.idempotencyKey,
    ),
  ],
);

/**
 * Processed Stripe webhook events, per tenant: the tenant and user come from the signed event's
 * metadata; events that carry none (payouts, fees, sessions created before B2) use the nil
 * `PLATFORM_TENANT_ID` and a NULL user. One Stripe event id always maps to one tenant.
 * citus: tenant. Purged hourly after `STRIPE_EVENTS_RETENTION_DAYS`.
 */
export const stripeEvents = billingPgSchema.table(
  'stripe_events',
  {
    tenantId: uuid().notNull(),
    /** Stripe event id (`evt_…`). */
    id: text().notNull(),
    userId: uuid(),
    type: text().notNull(),
    processedAt: timestamptz().notNull().defaultNow(),
  },
  (t) => [
    primaryKey({ name: 'stripe_events_tenant_id_id_pk', columns: [t.tenantId, t.id] }),
    // The purge's `WHERE processed_at < $cutoff` range scan.
    index('stripe_events_processed_at_idx').on(t.processedAt),
  ],
);
```

      Create `domain/stripe-owner.ts`:

```ts
import { isPlatformTenantId, isUuid, PLATFORM_TENANT_ID } from '@platform/common';

/** Who a Stripe object belongs to, as signed into its metadata at checkout (B2-26, ledger D5). */
export interface StripeOwner {
  /** The payment's tenant, or `PLATFORM_TENANT_ID` when the object carries none. */
  readonly tenantId: string;
  /** The paying user; `null` for platform-level objects (payouts, fees). */
  readonly userId: string | null;
}

/** Objects without tenant metadata: payouts, unattributed fees, sessions created before B2. */
export const PLATFORM_OWNER: StripeOwner = Object.freeze({
  tenantId: PLATFORM_TENANT_ID,
  userId: null,
});

/**
 * Reads `metadata.tenantId` / `metadata.userId` of a Stripe object (Checkout Session, PaymentIntent,
 * Charge: checkout mirrors the metadata onto the PaymentIntent). Only signed data reaches this
 * function (the webhook verified the signature first). Anything that is not a tenant UUID falls back
 * to the platform owner, never to a guess.
 */
export function stripeOwnerOf(object: unknown): StripeOwner {
  const metadata = (object as { metadata?: unknown } | null | undefined)?.metadata;
  if (typeof metadata !== 'object' || metadata === null) return PLATFORM_OWNER;
  const { tenantId, userId } = metadata as Record<string, unknown>;
  if (!isUuid(tenantId) || isPlatformTenantId(tenantId)) return PLATFORM_OWNER;
  return { tenantId, userId: isUuid(userId) ? userId : null };
}
```

      it stays, and `@domain/billing`'s root barrel keeps re-exporting the same value). If that line is not found

```ts
const prepareStatements = (db: DrizzleDB) => ({
  byId: db
    .select()
    .from(payments)
    .where(
      and(
        eq(payments.tenantId, sql.placeholder('tenantId')),
        eq(payments.id, sql.placeholder('id')),
      ),
    )
    .limit(1)
    .prepare('billing_payment_by_tenant_id'),
  // Keyset pages, newest first (uuidv7 ids = time order), always inside one tenant (router query
  // on Citus): payments_tenant_id_user_id_id_idx for a user, the (tenant_id, id) PK for the tenant.
  byUser: db
    .select()
    .from(payments)
    .where(
      and(
        eq(payments.tenantId, sql.placeholder('tenantId')),
        eq(payments.userId, sql.placeholder('userId')),
      ),
    )
    .orderBy(keysetOrder(payments.id))
    .limit(sql.placeholder('limit'))
    .prepare('billing_payments_by_tenant_user'),
  byUserAfter: db
    .select()
    .from(payments)
    .where(
      and(
        eq(payments.tenantId, sql.placeholder('tenantId')),
        eq(payments.userId, sql.placeholder('userId')),
        lt(payments.id, sql.placeholder('cursorId')),
      ),
    )
    .orderBy(keysetOrder(payments.id))
    .limit(sql.placeholder('limit'))
    .prepare('billing_payments_by_tenant_user_after'),
  byTenant: db
    .select()
    .from(payments)
    .where(eq(payments.tenantId, sql.placeholder('tenantId')))
    .orderBy(keysetOrder(payments.id))
    .limit(sql.placeholder('limit'))
    .prepare('billing_payments_by_tenant'),
  byTenantAfter: db
    .select()
    .from(payments)
    .where(
      and(
        eq(payments.tenantId, sql.placeholder('tenantId')),
        lt(payments.id, sql.placeholder('cursorId')),
      ),
    )
    .orderBy(keysetOrder(payments.id))
    .limit(sql.placeholder('limit'))
    .prepare('billing_payments_by_tenant_after'),
});
```

      and the methods with

```ts
  async create(payment: Payment): Promise<CreatePaymentResult> {
    const row = toPaymentRow(payment);
    // The unique (tenant_id, user_id, idempotency_key) index turns a replayed key into a no-op.
    const [inserted] = await this.txHost.tx
      .insert(payments)
      .values(row)
      .onConflictDoNothing({
        target: [payments.tenantId, payments.userId, payments.idempotencyKey],
      })
      .returning();
    if (inserted !== undefined) return { payment: fromPaymentRow(inserted), created: true };

    if (isNil(row.idempotencyKey)) {
      throw new Error(`Payment ${row.id} was not inserted although it has no idempotency key`);
    }
    const [existing] = await this.txHost.tx
      .select()
      .from(payments)
      .where(
        and(
          eq(payments.tenantId, row.tenantId),
          eq(payments.userId, row.userId),
          eq(payments.idempotencyKey, row.idempotencyKey),
        ),
      )
      .limit(1);
    if (existing === undefined) throw new PaymentConcurrentlyModifiedException(row.id);
    return { payment: fromPaymentRow(existing), created: false };
  }

  async findById(tenantId: string, id: string): Promise<Payment | null> {
    const [row] = await this.statements.byId.execute({ tenantId, id });
    return row === undefined ? null : fromPaymentRow(row);
  }

  async findForCheckoutSession(ref: {
    tenantId: string;
    paymentId: string | null;
    sessionId: string;
  }): Promise<Payment | null> {
    const bySession = eq(payments.stripeCheckoutSessionId, ref.sessionId);
    // tenant_id equality first: FOR UPDATE needs the distribution column on Citus (sharding §2.1.6).
    const rows = await this.txHost.tx
      .select()
      .from(payments)
      .where(
        and(
          eq(payments.tenantId, ref.tenantId),
          ref.paymentId === null ? bySession : or(eq(payments.id, ref.paymentId), bySession),
        ),
      )
      .limit(2)
      .for('update');
    const row = rows.find((r) => r.stripeCheckoutSessionId === ref.sessionId) ?? rows[0];
    return row === undefined ? null : fromPaymentRow(row);
  }
```

```ts
const key = (tenantId: string, id: string): string => `${tenantId}:${id}`;

export class InMemoryBillingStore {
  state: StoreState = { payments: new Map(), events: new Map() };

  snapshot(): StoreState {
    return cloneDeep(this.state);
  }

  restore(snapshot: StoreState): void {
    this.state = snapshot;
  }

  seed(...payments: Payment[]): void {
    for (const payment of payments) {
      const s = payment.toSnapshot();
      this.state.payments.set(key(s.tenantId, s.id), s);
    }
  }

  /** The stored payment with this id, whatever its tenant (ids are uuidv7: unique). */
  payment(id: string): PaymentSnapshot | undefined {
    return [...this.state.payments.values()].find((p) => p.id === id);
  }
}

export class InMemoryPaymentsRepository implements PaymentsRepository {
  constructor(readonly store: InMemoryBillingStore) {}

  async create(payment: Payment): Promise<CreatePaymentResult> {
    const s = payment.toSnapshot();
    if (s.idempotencyKey !== null) {
      const existing = [...this.store.state.payments.values()].find(
        (p) =>
          p.tenantId === s.tenantId &&
          p.userId === s.userId &&
          p.idempotencyKey === s.idempotencyKey,
      );
      if (existing) return { payment: Payment.restore(existing), created: false };
    }
    this.store.state.payments.set(key(s.tenantId, s.id), s);
    return { payment: Payment.restore(s), created: true };
  }

  async findById(tenantId: string, id: string): Promise<Payment | null> {
    const s = this.store.state.payments.get(key(tenantId, id));
    return s ? Payment.restore(s) : null;
  }

  async findForCheckoutSession(ref: {
    tenantId: string;
    paymentId: string | null;
    sessionId: string;
  }): Promise<Payment | null> {
    const inTenant = [...this.store.state.payments.values()].filter(
      (p) => p.tenantId === ref.tenantId,
    );
    const s =
      inTenant.find((p) => p.stripeCheckoutSessionId === ref.sessionId) ??
      inTenant.find((p) => p.id === ref.paymentId);
    return s ? Payment.restore(s) : null;
  }

  async save(payment: Payment): Promise<void> {
    const s = payment.toSnapshot();
    const stored = this.store.state.payments.get(key(s.tenantId, s.id));
    if (!stored || stored.version !== s.version)
      throw new PaymentConcurrentlyModifiedException(s.id);
    this.store.state.payments.set(key(s.tenantId, s.id), { ...s, version: s.version + 1 });
    payment.markPersisted();
  }

  async list(criteria: ListPaymentsCriteria): Promise<CursorPage<Payment>> {
    const after = isEmpty(criteria.cursor) ? undefined : decodeIdCursor(String(criteria.cursor)).id;
    const rows = [...this.store.state.payments.values()]
      .filter((p) => p.tenantId === criteria.tenantId)
      .filter((p) => criteria.userId === undefined || p.userId === criteria.userId)
      .filter((p) => after === undefined || p.id < after)
      .sort((a, b) => b.id.localeCompare(a.id))
      .slice(0, keysetFetchLimit(criteria.limit))
      .map((p) => Payment.restore(p));
    return keysetPage(rows, criteria.limit);
  }
}

export class InMemoryStripeEventsRepository implements StripeEventsRepository {
  constructor(readonly store: InMemoryBillingStore) {}

  /** `processed_at` per `${tenantId}:${id}` (kept apart so `state.events` holds receipts only). */
  readonly processedAt = new Map<string, Date>();

  async markProcessed(event: StripeEventReceipt): Promise<boolean> {
    const k = key(event.tenantId, event.id);
    if (this.store.state.events.has(k)) return false;
    this.store.state.events.set(k, { ...event });
    this.processedAt.set(k, new Date());
    return true;
  }

  async deleteProcessedBefore(cutoff: Date, limit: number): Promise<number> {
    const expired = [...this.processedAt]
      .filter(([, at]) => at < cutoff)
      .slice(0, limit)
      .map(([k]) => k);
    for (const k of expired) {
      this.store.state.events.delete(k);
      this.processedAt.delete(k);
    }
    return expired.length;
  }
}
```

```ts
// billing.constants.ts
BillingErrorCode.TENANT_MISMATCH = 'TENANT_MISMATCH'; // 403
// domain/billing.errors.ts
export class TenantMismatchException extends PermissionDeniedException { constructor() } // code TENANT_MISMATCH
// application/tenant-scope.ts
/** The request's principal, which must be acting in `tenantId` (and as `userId` when given). */
export function requireTenantPrincipal(tenantId: string, userId?: string): Principal; // @throws PRINCIPAL_MISSING (401), TenantMismatchException (403)
// domain/checkout-request-hash.ts
export interface CheckoutRequestShape { priceId: string; quantity: number; customerEmail: string; successUrl?: string | undefined; cancelUrl?: string | undefined }
export function checkoutRequestHash(request: CheckoutRequestShape): string; // sha256 hex of the normalized request
Payment#matchesRequestHash(hash: string): boolean; // true when no hash is stored (legacy row) or equal
// domain/events/payment-succeeded.event.ts: trailing `readonly tenantId: string = PLATFORM_TENANT_ID`
// contracts: PAYMENT_SUCCEEDED_EVENT is tenant-scoped (envelope tenantId required on publish)
// billing.permissions.ts: BILLING_RBAC.grants = { member: [Checkout], admin: [ReadAll] } (I-5)
```

Semantics: `GET /v1/billing/payments?all=true` and GraphQL `payments(all: true)` list every payment **of the active
tenant** and need `billing:read-all` (admins and owners). Checkout and listing require a principal whose tenant
(and user) equal the request's; the Stripe webhook runs as `{ userId, tenantId }` from the signed metadata, and
without a principal for platform-level events.

**Wave:** Lane B, after Task 1, once Lane P (Tasks 3-4) has merged into `feat/domains-tenancy` (rebase `b2/billing`
on it first).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.** Create `domains/billing/src/application/tenant-scope.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { createPrincipal, generateId, runWithPrincipal } from '@platform/common';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { requireTenantPrincipal } from './tenant-scope.js';

describe('requireTenantPrincipal', () => {
  const userId = generateId();
  const tenantId = generateId();
  const asUser = <T>(fn: () => T): T =>
    runWithPrincipal(createPrincipal({ userId, tenantId, roles: ['member'] }), fn);

  it('returns the principal acting in the tenant (and as the user)', () => {
    expect(asUser(() => requireTenantPrincipal(tenantId, userId))).toMatchObject({
      userId,
      tenantId,
    });
    expect(asUser(() => requireTenantPrincipal(tenantId))).toMatchObject({ tenantId });
  });

  it('403 TENANT_MISMATCH for another tenant or another user', () => {
    expect(() => asUser(() => requireTenantPrincipal(generateId(), userId))).toThrow(
      expect.objectContaining({ code: 'TENANT_MISMATCH' }),
    );
    expect(() => asUser(() => requireTenantPrincipal(tenantId, generateId()))).toThrow(
      expect.objectContaining({ code: 'TENANT_MISMATCH' }),
    );
  });

  it('401 PRINCIPAL_MISSING without a principal', () => {
    expect(() => requireTenantPrincipal(tenantId, userId)).toThrow(
      expect.objectContaining({ code: 'PRINCIPAL_MISSING' }),
    );
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { checkoutRequestHash } from './checkout-request-hash.js';

describe('checkoutRequestHash', () => {
  const base = { priceId: 'price_pro', quantity: 2, customerEmail: 'Buyer@Example.com' };

  it('is a sha256 hex that ignores price id padding and email case', () => {
    const hash = checkoutRequestHash(base);
    expect(hash).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/);
    expect(
      checkoutRequestHash({ ...base, priceId: ' price_pro ', customerEmail: 'buyer@example.com' }),
    ).toBe(hash);
  });

  it.each([
    ['quantity', { quantity: 3 }],
    ['price', { priceId: 'price_basic' }],
    ['email', { customerEmail: 'other@example.com' }],
    ['success URL', { successUrl: 'https://evil.test/ok' }],
    ['cancel URL', { cancelUrl: 'https://evil.test/no' }],
  ])('changes with the %s (ledger S-2: all six fields count)', (_field, change) => {
    expect(checkoutRequestHash({ ...base, ...change })).not.toBe(checkoutRequestHash(base));
  });
});
```

```ts
const as = <T>(principal: { userId: string; tenantId: string; roles: TenantRole[] }, fn: () => T) =>
  runWithPrincipal(createPrincipal(principal), fn);

describe('tenant scope (B2-26)', () => {
  const tenantA = TEST_TENANT_ID;
  const tenantB = generateId();
  const ada = generateId();
  const bob = generateId();
  let store: InMemoryBillingStore;
  let scoped: ListPaymentsHandler;

  beforeEach(() => {
    store = new InMemoryBillingStore();
    scoped = new ListPaymentsHandler(
      new InMemoryPaymentsRepository(store),
      createTestRbacRegistry(BILLING_RBAC),
    );
    store.seed(
      makePayment({ tenantId: tenantA, userId: ada }),
      makePayment({ tenantId: tenantA, userId: bob }),
      makePayment({ tenantId: tenantB, userId: ada }),
    );
  });

  it("all (no userId) lists the whole tenant for an admin, never another tenant's rows", async () => {
    const page = await as({ userId: ada, tenantId: tenantA, roles: ['admin'] }, () =>
      scoped.execute(new ListPaymentsQuery({ tenantId: tenantA, limit: 10 })),
    );
    expect(page.items.map((p) => p.tenantId)).toEqual([tenantA, tenantA]);
  });

  it('403 for all without billing:read-all, recomputed from the principal roles (service side)', async () => {
    await expect(
      as({ userId: ada, tenantId: tenantA, roles: ['member'] }, () =>
        scoped.execute(new ListPaymentsQuery({ tenantId: tenantA, limit: 10 })),
      ),
    ).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'FORBIDDEN', details: { required: ['billing:read-all'] } });
  });

  it('403 TENANT_MISMATCH when the request names a tenant the principal is not acting in', async () => {
    await expect(
      as({ userId: ada, tenantId: tenantA, roles: ['owner'] }, () =>
        scoped.execute(new ListPaymentsQuery({ tenantId: tenantB, userId: ada, limit: 10 })),
      ),
    ).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'TENANT_MISMATCH' });
  });
});
```

Create `apps/monolith/test/billing-tenancy.e2e-spec.ts`:

```ts
import { getTableColumns } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { generateId } from '@platform/common';
import { PaymentStatus, payments } from '@domain/billing';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { createMonolithE2eApp, type MonolithE2e } from './support/monolith-e2e-app.js';

await vi.hoisted(async () => {
  const { MONOLITH_E2E_ENV } = await import('./support/e2e-env.js');
  Object.assign(process.env, MONOLITH_E2E_ENV);
});

interface Tokens {
  accessToken: string;
  tenantId: string;
  user: { id: string };
}
const AT = '2026-09-01T10:00:00.000Z';

describe('billing is tenant-scoped (monolith, B2-26)', () => {
  let e2e: MonolithE2e;
  const listings: unknown[][] = [];
  let ada: Tokens;
  let bob: Tokens;

  const row = (tenantId: string, userId: string) => {
    const values: Record<string, unknown> = {
      tenantId,
      id: generateId(),
      userId,
      priceId: 'price_e2e',
      quantity: 1,
      amountTotal: '2500',
      currency: 'usd',
      status: PaymentStatus.Succeeded,
      version: 1,
      createdAt: AT,
      updatedAt: AT,
    };
    return Object.fromEntries(
      Object.keys(getTableColumns(payments)).map((c) => [c, values[c] ?? null]),
    );
  };

  const register = async (email: string): Promise<Tokens> => {
    const res = await e2e.app.inject({
      method: 'POST',
      url: '/v1/auth/register',
      payload: { email, password: 'correct horse battery staple', displayName: email },
    });
    expect(res.statusCode, res.body).toBe(201);
    return res.json<Tokens>();
  };

  beforeAll(async () => {
    e2e = await createMonolithE2eApp({
      postgresRows: (sql, params) => {
        if (!sql.startsWith('select') || !sql.includes('"billing"."payments"')) return [];
        listings.push(params);
        const tenantId = params.find((p) => p === ada?.tenantId || p === bob?.tenantId);
        return tenantId === undefined ? [] : [row(String(tenantId), ada.user.id)];
      },
    });
    ada = await register('ada.billing@example.com');
    bob = await register('bob.billing@example.com');
  });

  afterAll(async () => {
    await e2e?.app.close();
  });

  it("all=true from an owner reads only the owner's active tenant", async () => {
    const res = await e2e.app.inject({
      method: 'GET',
      url: '/v1/billing/payments?all=true&limit=5',
      headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${ada.accessToken}` },
    });
    expect(res.statusCode, res.body).toBe(200);
    expect(listings.at(-1)).toContain(ada.tenantId);
    expect(listings.at(-1)).not.toContain(bob.tenantId);
  });

  it('GraphQL payments(all: true) is scoped the same way', async () => {
    const res = await e2e.app.inject({
      method: 'POST',
      url: '/graphql',
      headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${bob.accessToken}`, 'content-type': 'application/json' },
      payload: { query: '{ payments(all: true, limit: 5) { items { id userId } } }' },
    });
    expect(res.statusCode, res.body).toBe(200);
    expect(res.json<{ errors?: unknown }>().errors).toBeUndefined();
    expect(listings.at(-1)).toContain(bob.tenantId);
  });
});
```

```ts
export class TenantMismatchException extends PermissionDeniedException {
```

      (import `PermissionDeniedException` from `@platform/common` if not yet imported). Create
      `application/tenant-scope.ts`:

```ts
import { type Principal, requirePrincipal } from '@platform/common';
import { TenantMismatchException } from '../domain/billing.errors.js';

/**
 * Billing is tenant-scoped: the request's tenant (and user, when given) must be the principal's,
 * which the edge put in CLS (JwtAuthGuard in the monolith, GrpcContextInterceptor in
 * billing-service). Defence in depth: the edge already copies both from `req.user`.
 *
 * @throws UnauthenticatedException PRINCIPAL_MISSING (401) outside an authenticated call
 * @throws TenantMismatchException (403)
 */
export function requireTenantPrincipal(tenantId: string, userId?: string): Principal {
  const principal = requirePrincipal();
  if (principal.tenantId !== tenantId || (userId !== undefined && principal.userId !== userId)) {
    throw new TenantMismatchException();
  }
  return principal;
}
```

      Create `domain/checkout-request-hash.ts`:

```ts
import { sha256Hex } from '@platform/common';
import { toLower, trim } from 'lodash-es';

/** The checkout fields that reach Stripe (ledger design S-2). */
export interface CheckoutRequestShape {
  priceId: string;
  quantity: number;
  customerEmail: string;
  successUrl?: string | undefined;
  cancelUrl?: string | undefined;
}

/**
 * sha256 hex of the normalized checkout request, stored on the payment so a replayed
 * Idempotency-Key with ANY different field (not just price and quantity) is a 409. Absent URLs hash
 * as '' (the configured defaults apply later and are the same for every replay).
 */
export function checkoutRequestHash(request: CheckoutRequestShape): string {
  return sha256Hex(
    JSON.stringify([
      trim(request.priceId),
      request.quantity,
      toLower(trim(request.customerEmail)),
      request.successUrl ?? '',
      request.cancelUrl ?? '',
    ]),
  );
}
```

      `domain/payment.aggregate.ts`: after `matchesRequest` add

```ts
  /** S-2: a stored hash must equal the replay's; payments created before B2 have none. */
  matchesRequestHash(hash: string): boolean {
    return this.state.requestHash === null || this.state.requestHash === hash;
  }
```

```ts

  throw new IdempotencyKeyReusedException();
}
```

```ts
// Service-side recomputation (spec §7): the roles travelled, the permissions are ours.

  !hasPermissions(principalPermissions(this.rbac, principal), [BillingPermission.ReadAll])
) {

```

      (imports: `hasPermissions`, `principalPermissions`, `RbacRegistry` from `@platform/auth`;
      `PermissionDeniedException` from `@platform/common`; `BillingPermission` from `../../../billing.permissions.js`;
      `requireTenantPrincipal` from `../../tenant-scope.js`). `handle-stripe-webhook.handler.ts`: move the owner
      resolution out of `applyOnce` into `execute` and run the work as the signed principal:

```ts
  async execute(command: HandleStripeWebhookCommand): Promise<HandleStripeWebhookResponse> {
    const event = this.stripe.constructWebhookEvent(command.payload, command.signature);
    // (L-7's api_version check stays here, unchanged.)
    const owner = stripeOwnerOf(event.data.object);
    const work = () => this.process(event, owner);
    // Spec §9: act as the user and tenant Stripe signed; platform-level events have no user and run
    // without a principal (B3-11 moves their writes to the system pool).
    return owner.userId === null
      ? work()
      : runWithPrincipal(createPrincipal({ userId: owner.userId, tenantId: owner.tenantId }), work);
  }

  private async process(event: Stripe.Event, owner: StripeOwner): Promise<HandleStripeWebhookResponse> {
    const outcome = await this.applyOnce(event, owner);
    if (outcome.payment !== null) this.publisher.mergeObjectContext(outcome.payment).commit();
    return { received: true, eventId: event.id, eventType: event.type, duplicate: outcome.duplicate };
  }
```

      with `applyOnce(event: Stripe.Event, owner: StripeOwner)` using the passed `owner` instead of computing it
      (imports `createPrincipal`, `runWithPrincipal` from `@platform/common`). `payment-succeeded.relay.ts`: the
      publish options gain `tenantId: event.tenantId, actorId: event.userId,`. `contracts/src/events/billing.events.ts`:

```ts
// @platform/transport (root)
GRPC_METADATA_KEYS.TENANT_ID = 'x-tenant-id';
GRPC_METADATA_KEYS.STAFF = 'x-staff'; // value 'true', or absent
interface RpcCallerContext {
  requestId?;
  correlationId?;
  userId?;
  roles?;
  tenantId?: string | undefined;
  staff?: boolean | undefined;
}
interface IncomingRpcContext {
  requestId?;
  correlationId?;
  userId?;
  roles: string[];
  tenantId?: string;
  staff: boolean;
}
/** The principal a caller asserted, or undefined without x-user-id. @throws UnauthenticatedException INVALID_PRINCIPAL */
export function principalFromRpcCaller(caller: IncomingRpcContext): Principal | undefined;
/** The caller context of a principal (what the gateway forwards on every call). */
export function rpcCallerOf(principal: Principal): RpcCallerContext;
// callerContextFromCls(cls, overrides): userId/tenantId/roles/staff come from CLS_PRINCIPAL; overrides win.
// GrpcContextInterceptor: sets CLS_PRINCIPAL from the metadata; no longer writes RPC_CLS_KEYS.USER_ID.
// @platform/auth (root)
/** Permissions of a principal, recomputed from its tenant roles (spec §7). [] without a tenant. */
export function principalPermissions(
  registry: RbacRegistry,
  principal: Principal | undefined,
): string[];
```

Wiring (I-5): `BillingGrpcModule`, `IdentityGrpcModule`, `NotificationsGrpcModule` import
`RbacModule.forFeature(<X>_RBAC)`; billing-service and notifications-service import `RbacModule.forRoot()`
(identity-service gets it from `AuthModule.forRootAsync()`; importing `forRoot()` twice would create two
registries).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.** Create `libs/transport/src/grpc/grpc-principal.spec.ts` (neutral

```ts
import { AsyncLocalStorage } from 'node:async_hooks';
import { Metadata } from '@grpc/grpc-js';
import type { CallHandler, ExecutionContext } from '@nestjs/common';
import { CLS_PRINCIPAL, createPrincipal, generateId, PLATFORM_TENANT_ID } from '@platform/common';
import { ClsService } from 'nestjs-cls';
import { defer, lastValueFrom, of } from 'rxjs';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { callerContextFromCls } from './grpc-caller-context.js';
import { GrpcContextInterceptor } from './grpc-context.interceptor.js';
import { createOutgoingMetadata, readIncomingMetadata } from './grpc-metadata.js';
import { principalFromRpcCaller, rpcCallerOf } from './grpc-principal.js';

const userId = generateId();
const tenantId = generateId();

function rpcContext(metadata: unknown): ExecutionContext {
  return {
    getType: () => 'rpc',
    switchToRpc: () => ({ getContext: () => metadata, getData: () => ({}) }),
  } as unknown as ExecutionContext;
}

const metadataOf = (entries: Record<string, string>): Metadata => {
  const metadata = new Metadata();
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(entries)) metadata.set(key, value);
  return metadata;
};

describe('gRPC principal metadata', () => {
  it('round-trips user, tenant, roles and staff', () => {
    const outgoing = createOutgoingMetadata({ userId, tenantId, roles: ['admin'], staff: true });
    expect(outgoing.get('x-tenant-id')).toEqual([tenantId]);
    expect(outgoing.get('x-staff')).toEqual(['true']);
    expect(readIncomingMetadata(outgoing)).toMatchObject({
      userId,
      tenantId,
      roles: ['admin'],
      staff: true,
    });
  });

  it('omits x-staff unless staff is true', () => {
    expect(createOutgoingMetadata({ userId, tenantId, staff: false }).get('x-staff')).toEqual([]);
  });

  it.each(['TRUE', '1', 'yes', ' true'])(
    'reads x-staff %j as false (only the exact string "true")',
    (value) => {
      expect(
        readIncomingMetadata(metadataOf({ 'x-user-id': userId, 'x-staff': value })).staff,
      ).toBe(false);
    },
  );
});

describe('principalFromRpcCaller', () => {
  it('builds the principal and drops unknown roles', () => {
    const caller = readIncomingMetadata(
      metadataOf({
        'x-user-id': userId,
        'x-tenant-id': tenantId,
        'x-user-roles': 'owner,root,owner',
      }),
    );
    expect(principalFromRpcCaller(caller)).toEqual({
      userId,
      tenantId,
      roles: ['owner'],
      staff: false,
    });
  });

  it('is undefined without a user (the tenant alone means nothing)', () => {
    expect(
      principalFromRpcCaller(readIncomingMetadata(metadataOf({ 'x-tenant-id': tenantId }))),
    ).toBeUndefined();
    expect(principalFromRpcCaller(readIncomingMetadata(undefined))).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('a user without a tenant is user-scoped: roles and staff are dropped', () => {
    const caller = readIncomingMetadata(
      metadataOf({ 'x-user-id': userId, 'x-user-roles': 'admin', 'x-staff': 'true' }),
    );
    expect(principalFromRpcCaller(caller)).toEqual({
      userId,
      tenantId: null,
      roles: [],
      staff: false,
    });
  });

  it.each([
    ['a user id that is not a UUID', { 'x-user-id': 'u-1', 'x-tenant-id': tenantId }],
    ['a malformed tenant', { 'x-user-id': userId, 'x-tenant-id': 'acme' }],
    ['the nil (platform) tenant', { 'x-user-id': userId, 'x-tenant-id': PLATFORM_TENANT_ID }],
  ])('rejects %s with INVALID_PRINCIPAL', (_label, entries) => {
    expect(() => principalFromRpcCaller(readIncomingMetadata(metadataOf(entries)))).toThrow(
      expect.objectContaining({ code: 'INVALID_PRINCIPAL' }),
    );
  });

  it('rpcCallerOf is the inverse for a tenant principal', () => {
    const principal = createPrincipal({ userId, tenantId, roles: ['member'], staff: false });
    expect(rpcCallerOf(principal)).toEqual({ userId, tenantId, roles: ['member'] });
  });
});

describe('GrpcContextInterceptor and callerContextFromCls', () => {
  const cls = new ClsService(new AsyncLocalStorage());
  const interceptor = new GrpcContextInterceptor(cls);
  const seen = (metadata: unknown) => {
    const next: CallHandler = { handle: () => defer(() => of(cls.get<unknown>(CLS_PRINCIPAL))) };
    return lastValueFrom(interceptor.intercept(rpcContext(metadata), next));
  };

  it('puts the caller principal into CLS for the handler', async () => {
    await expect(
      seen(createOutgoingMetadata({ userId, tenantId, roles: ['admin'], staff: true })),
    ).resolves.toEqual({ userId, tenantId, roles: ['admin'], staff: true });
  });

  it('sets no principal for an anonymous call', async () => {
    await expect(seen(createOutgoingMetadata({}))).resolves.toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('fails the call with UNAUTHENTICATED INVALID_PRINCIPAL for a hostile tenant', async () => {
    await expect(
      seen(metadataOf({ 'x-user-id': userId, 'x-tenant-id': PLATFORM_TENANT_ID })),
    ).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'INVALID_PRINCIPAL',
    });
  });

  it('forwards the CLS principal on outgoing calls; an override for the same user keeps it', () => {
    cls.run(() => {
      cls.set(CLS_PRINCIPAL, createPrincipal({ userId, tenantId, roles: ['owner'], staff: false }));
      expect(callerContextFromCls(cls)).toMatchObject({ userId, tenantId, roles: ['owner'] });
      expect(callerContextFromCls(cls)).not.toHaveProperty('staff');
      expect(callerContextFromCls(cls, { userId })).toMatchObject({ userId, tenantId });
    });
  });

  it("never pairs another user's id with the principal's tenant, roles or staff flag", () => {
    cls.run(() => {
      cls.set(CLS_PRINCIPAL, createPrincipal({ userId, tenantId, roles: ['owner'], staff: true }));
      const other = generateId();
      const caller = callerContextFromCls(cls, { userId: other });
      expect(caller).toMatchObject({ userId: other });
      expect(caller).not.toHaveProperty('tenantId');
      expect(caller).not.toHaveProperty('roles');
      expect(caller).not.toHaveProperty('staff');
    });
  });

  it('never forwards client request headers: only the CLS principal travels', () => {
    cls.run(() => {
      // A hostile HTTP client sends the internal metadata names as headers.
      cls.set(CLS_REQ, {
        headers: { 'x-user-id': generateId(), 'x-tenant-id': generateId(), 'x-staff': 'true' },
      });
      cls.set(CLS_PRINCIPAL, createPrincipal({ userId, tenantId, roles: ['member'] }));
      const metadata = createOutgoingMetadata(callerContextFromCls(cls));
      expect(metadata.get('x-user-id')).toEqual([userId]);
      expect(metadata.get('x-tenant-id')).toEqual([tenantId]);
      expect(metadata.get('x-staff')).toEqual([]);
    });
  });
});
```

      Create `libs/transport/src/grpc/grpc-principal.ts`:

```ts
import { createPrincipal, type Principal } from '@platform/common';
import type { IncomingRpcContext, RpcCallerContext } from './grpc-metadata.js';

/**
 * The principal a gRPC caller asserted in metadata (spec §7). The gateway authenticated the user and
 * forwards `x-user-id`, `x-tenant-id`, `x-user-roles` and `x-staff` on every call; services trust them
 * only inside the mesh (gRPC mTLS, or GRPC_ALLOW_INSECURE behind a mesh that does mTLS).
 * - no `x-user-id` → `undefined` (anonymous: register, login, a signed third-party webhook);
 * - no `x-tenant-id` → a user-scoped principal (roles and staff need a tenant, so they are dropped);
 * - anything malformed → `UnauthenticatedException` `INVALID_PRINCIPAL` (fail closed).
 */
export function principalFromRpcCaller(caller: IncomingRpcContext): Principal | undefined {
  if (caller.userId === undefined) return undefined;
  if (caller.tenantId === undefined)
    return createPrincipal({ userId: caller.userId, tenantId: null });
  return createPrincipal({
    userId: caller.userId,
    tenantId: caller.tenantId,
    roles: caller.roles,
    staff: caller.staff,
  });
}

/** What an outgoing call asserts for `principal`. `staff` only when true. */
export function rpcCallerOf(principal: Principal): RpcCallerContext {
  return {
    userId: principal.userId,
    ...(principal.tenantId === null ? {} : { tenantId: principal.tenantId }),
    ...(principal.roles.length === 0 ? {} : { roles: [...principal.roles] }),
    ...(principal.staff ? { staff: true } : {}),
  };
}
```

```ts
let principal: ReturnType<typeof principalFromRpcCaller>;
try {
  principal = principalFromRpcCaller(caller);
} catch (error) {
  // INVALID_PRINCIPAL → UNAUTHENTICATED through the controller's exception filter.
  return throwError(() => error);
}
```

      with `if (principal !== undefined) cls.set(CLS_PRINCIPAL, principal);` (the injected `ClsService` is the same
      store `getPrincipal()` reads). Update the class comment: "`CLS_PRINCIPAL` = the caller's principal (B2-23);
      `RpcCallerContext` keys documented in `GRPC_METADATA_KEYS`." `grpc-caller-context.ts`: replace the
      `if (cls?.isActive()) { … }` block with

```ts
if (cls?.isActive()) {
  // The principal written at the edge (JwtAuthGuard) or adopted from metadata (GrpcContextInterceptor)
  // travels on every hop: user, tenant, roles and staff (spec §7). An override naming ANOTHER user
  // gets none of them: a tenant and roles are only ever asserted for the user they belong to.
  const principal = cls.get<unknown>(CLS_PRINCIPAL);
  if (
    isPrincipal(principal) &&
    (overrides.userId === undefined || overrides.userId === principal.userId)
  ) {
    Object.assign(context, rpcCallerOf(principal));
  }
}
```

      the now-unused `isArray`, `isPlainObject`, `isString`, `RPC_CLS_KEYS` imports; the final
      `return { ...context, ...pickDefined(overrides) };` stays) and the doc bullets "`userId`, `tenantId`, `roles`,
      `staff` come from the CLS principal; never from request headers" and "an override with another `userId` drops

```ts
import type { Principal } from '@platform/common';
import type { RbacRegistry } from './rbac.registry.js';

/**
 * Permissions of a principal, recomputed from its tenant roles through the registry. Services and
 * consumers use it: roles travel (gRPC metadata, Kafka envelopes), permissions never do (spec §7).
 */
export function principalPermissions(
  registry: RbacRegistry,
  principal: Principal | undefined,
): string[] {
  return principal === undefined || principal.tenantId === null
    ? []
    : registry.resolve(principal.roles);
}
```

```ts
// @platform/contracts
interface EventEnvelope<T> {
  /* … */ tenantId?: string | undefined;
  actorId?: string | null | undefined;
  payload: T;
}
// envelope schema: tenantId z.uuid().optional() (the nil UUID = platform), actorId z.uuid().nullable().optional()
interface EventContract<T> {
  readonly topic: string;
  readonly payload: z.ZodType<T>;
  readonly tenantScoped: boolean;
}
export interface EventContractOptions {
  /** Producers must set the envelope tenantId (consumers stay tolerant). */ tenantScoped?: boolean;
}
export function defineEventContract<T>(
  topic: string,
  payload: z.ZodType<T>,
  options?: EventContractOptions,
): EventContract<T>;
interface EventEnvelopeMeta {
  id;
  source;
  correlationId?;
  occurredAt?;
  tenantId?: string | undefined;
  actorId?: string | null | undefined;
}
// createEventEnvelope: a tenant-scoped contract without meta.tenantId throws ZodError (path ['tenantId'])
// @platform/transport
interface KafkaPublishOptions {
  /* … */ tenantId?: string | undefined;
  actorId?: string | null | undefined;
}
// KafkaProducer.createEnvelope: tenantId/actorId default to the CLS principal (tenantId when not null, actorId = userId);
//   an explicit actorId: null means "no actor" (system work).
// KafkaContextInterceptor: CLS_PRINCIPAL = { userId: actorId, tenantId } when both are UUIDs and the tenant is not
//   the platform tenant; otherwise no principal (never an error: the pipe validates the envelope).
```
