```ts
import { generateId, PLATFORM_TENANT_ID } from '@platform/common';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { z } from 'zod';
import { createEventEnvelope, defineEventContract, parseEventEnvelope } from './event-contract.js';

const payload = z.object({ orderId: z.uuid() });
const ORDER_PLACED = defineEventContract('orders.order-placed.v1', payload, { tenantScoped: true });
const CATALOG_SYNCED = defineEventContract('catalog.catalog-synced.v1', payload);
const meta = { id: generateId(), source: 'orders-service' };
const body = { orderId: generateId() };

describe('envelope tenancy', () => {
  it('records whether a contract is tenant-scoped (default false)', () => {
    expect(ORDER_PLACED.tenantScoped).toBe(true);
    expect(CATALOG_SYNCED.tenantScoped).toBe(false);
  });

  it('carries tenantId and actorId', () => {
    const tenantId = generateId();
    const actorId = generateId();
    expect(createEventEnvelope(ORDER_PLACED, body, { ...meta, tenantId, actorId })).toMatchObject({
      tenantId,
      actorId,
    });
  });

  it('refuses to produce a tenant-scoped event without a tenant', () => {
    expect(() => createEventEnvelope(ORDER_PLACED, body, meta)).toThrow(z.ZodError);
    expect(() => createEventEnvelope(CATALOG_SYNCED, body, meta)).not.toThrow();
  });

  it('accepts the platform tenant and a null actor (system work)', () => {
    expect(
      createEventEnvelope(ORDER_PLACED, body, {
        ...meta,
        tenantId: PLATFORM_TENANT_ID,
        actorId: null,
      }),
    ).toMatchObject({ tenantId: PLATFORM_TENANT_ID, actorId: null });
  });

  it('parses legacy envelopes without tenantId/actorId (in flight during a deploy)', () => {
    const legacy = {
      ...meta,
      type: ORDER_PLACED.topic,
      version: 1,
      occurredAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      payload: body,
    };
    expect(parseEventEnvelope(ORDER_PLACED, legacy)).not.toHaveProperty('tenantId');
  });

  it('rejects a tenantId that is not a UUID', () => {
    const bad = {
      ...meta,
      type: ORDER_PLACED.topic,
      version: 1,
      occurredAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      tenantId: 'acme',
      payload: body,
    };
    expect(() => parseEventEnvelope(ORDER_PLACED, bad)).toThrow(z.ZodError);
  });
});
```

      Create `libs/transport/src/kafka/kafka-principal.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { AsyncLocalStorage } from 'node:async_hooks';
import type { CallHandler, ExecutionContext } from '@nestjs/common';
import { KafkaContext } from '@nestjs/microservices';
import { CLS_PRINCIPAL, createPrincipal, generateId, PLATFORM_TENANT_ID } from '@platform/common';
import { defineEventContract } from '@platform/contracts';
import { ClsService } from 'nestjs-cls';
import { defer, lastValueFrom, of } from 'rxjs';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { z } from 'zod';
import { KafkaContextInterceptor } from './kafka-context.interceptor.js';
import { type KafkaEventEmitter, KafkaProducer } from './kafka-producer.service.js';

const ORDER_PLACED = defineEventContract(
  'orders.order-placed.v1',
  z.object({ orderId: z.uuid() }),
  {
    tenantScoped: true,
  },
);
const client: KafkaEventEmitter = { emit: () => of(undefined), connect: async () => undefined };
const userId = generateId();
const tenantId = generateId();

function kafkaContext(data: unknown): ExecutionContext {
  const message = { key: 'k', value: data, headers: {}, offset: '1' };
  const kafka = new KafkaContext([
    message as never,
    0,
    ORDER_PLACED.topic,
    {} as never,
    () => Promise.resolve(),
    {} as never,
  ]);
  return {
    getType: () => 'rpc',
    switchToRpc: () => ({ getContext: () => kafka, getData: () => data }),
  } as unknown as ExecutionContext;
}

describe('KafkaProducer fills tenantId/actorId from the CLS principal', () => {
  const cls = new ClsService(new AsyncLocalStorage());
  const producer = new KafkaProducer(client, { source: 'orders-service' }, cls);
  const body = { orderId: generateId() };

  it('uses the principal by default', () => {
    cls.run(() => {
      cls.set(CLS_PRINCIPAL, createPrincipal({ userId, tenantId, roles: ['member'] }));
      expect(producer.createEnvelope(ORDER_PLACED, body)).toMatchObject({
        tenantId,
        actorId: userId,
      });
    });
  });

  it('explicit options win, and actorId: null means no actor', () => {
    const other = generateId();
    cls.run(() => {
      cls.set(CLS_PRINCIPAL, createPrincipal({ userId, tenantId, roles: ['member'] }));
      expect(
        producer.createEnvelope(ORDER_PLACED, body, { tenantId: other, actorId: null }),
      ).toMatchObject({
        tenantId: other,
        actorId: null,
      });
    });
  });

  it('INVALID_EVENT for a tenant-scoped event outside any principal and without options', () => {
    expect(() => producer.createEnvelope(ORDER_PLACED, body)).toThrow(
      expect.objectContaining({ code: 'INVALID_EVENT' }),
    );
  });
});

describe('KafkaContextInterceptor restores the actor principal', () => {
  const cls = new ClsService(new AsyncLocalStorage());
  const interceptor = new KafkaContextInterceptor(cls);
  const seen = (data: unknown) => {
    const next: CallHandler = { handle: () => defer(() => of(cls.get<unknown>(CLS_PRINCIPAL))) };
    return lastValueFrom(interceptor.intercept(kafkaContext(data), next));
  };
  const envelope = (extra: Record<string, unknown>) => ({
    id: generateId(),
    payload: {},
    ...extra,
  });

  it('{ userId: actorId, tenantId } with no roles', async () => {
    await expect(seen(envelope({ tenantId, actorId: userId }))).resolves.toEqual({
      userId,
      tenantId,
      roles: [],
      staff: false,
    });
  });

  it.each([
    ['a legacy envelope', {}],
    ['a null actor', { tenantId, actorId: null }],
    ['the platform tenant', { tenantId: PLATFORM_TENANT_ID, actorId: userId }],
    ['a malformed tenant', { tenantId: 'acme', actorId: userId }],
  ])('no principal for %s', async (_label, extra) => {
    await expect(seen(envelope(extra))).resolves.toBeUndefined();
  });
});
```

```ts
/** `{ userId: actorId, tenantId }` of an (unvalidated) envelope, when both are usable; never throws. */
function actorPrincipal(data: unknown): Principal | undefined {
  const tenantId = field(data, 'tenantId');
  const actorId = field(data, 'actorId');
  if (!isUuid(tenantId) || isPlatformTenantId(tenantId) || !isUuid(actorId)) return undefined;
  try {
    return createPrincipal({ userId: actorId, tenantId });
  } catch {
    return undefined;
  }
}
```

### Task 5: DataLoader batches keep the request CLS (B2-25)

**Files:**

- Test (create): `libs/graphql/src/loaders/data-loader.cls.spec.ts`
- Modify (only if the spec fails): `libs/graphql/src/loaders/data-loader.registry.ts`

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: `runWithPrincipal`, `getPrincipal`, `createPrincipal` (`@platform/common`, core Task 1);
  `DataLoaderRegistry` (exists).
- Produces: a pinned guarantee (spec §7): a DataLoader batch function runs in the CLS context of the operation whose
  resolvers called `load()`, so it sees that request's principal; concurrent operations never see each other's.
  This is what core Task 12 relies on when it leaves `GetUsersByIds` (the `users` loader behind `Payment.user`)
  unscoped: its batch runs as the request's principal, so B3's RLS scopes it without code changes here.

**Wave:** Lane P, after Task 4.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the spec.** Create `libs/graphql/src/loaders/data-loader.cls.spec.ts` (neutral vocabulary):

```ts
import { createPrincipal, generateId, getPrincipal, runWithPrincipal } from '@platform/common';
import DataLoader from 'dataloader';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { DataLoaderRegistry } from './data-loader.registry.js';

const principal = () =>
  createPrincipal({ userId: generateId(), tenantId: generateId(), roles: ['member'] });

describe('DataLoader batches and the request CLS (spec §7, B2-25)', () => {
  const seenByBatch: (string | undefined)[] = [];
  const registry = new DataLoaderRegistry();
  registry.register(
    'orders',
    () =>
      new DataLoader<string, string>(async (ids) => {
        // Simulate I/O before reading the context, as a real batch (gRPC, SQL) would.
        await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 5));
        seenByBatch.push(getPrincipal()?.tenantId ?? undefined);
        return ids.map((id) => `${getPrincipal()?.tenantId ?? 'none'}:${id}`);
      }),
  );

  it('a batch sees the principal of the operation that loaded the keys', async () => {
    const ada = principal();
    const result = await runWithPrincipal(ada, async () => {
      const loaders = registry.createLoaders();
      const orders = loaders['orders'] as DataLoader<string, string>;
      return Promise.all([orders.load('o1'), orders.load('o2')]);
    });
    expect(result).toEqual([`${ada.tenantId}:o1`, `${ada.tenantId}:o2`]);
  });

  it('two concurrent operations never see each other (one batch each, own principal)', async () => {
    seenByBatch.length = 0;
    const ada = principal();
    const bob = principal();
    const operation = (p: ReturnType<typeof principal>) =>
      runWithPrincipal(p, async () => {
        const orders = registry.createLoaders()['orders'] as DataLoader<string, string>;
        await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 1));
        return Promise.all([orders.load('a'), orders.load('b')]);
      });

    const [forAda, forBob] = await Promise.all([operation(ada), operation(bob)]);

    expect(forAda).toEqual([`${ada.tenantId}:a`, `${ada.tenantId}:b`]);
    expect(forBob).toEqual([`${bob.tenantId}:a`, `${bob.tenantId}:b`]);
    expect([...seenByBatch].sort()).toEqual([ada.tenantId, bob.tenantId].sort());
  });

  it('a loader created lazily in one operation is never reused by another', async () => {
    const ada = principal();
    const bob = principal();
    const shared = registry.createLoaders();
    const first = await runWithPrincipal(ada, () =>
      (shared['orders'] as DataLoader<string, string>).load('x'),
    );
    const second = await runWithPrincipal(bob, () => registry.createLoaders()['orders']!.load('x'));
    expect(first).toBe(`${ada.tenantId}:x`);
    expect(second).toBe(`${bob.tenantId}:x`);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run it.** `npx vitest run --project graphql data-loader.cls` → Expected: PASS (AsyncLocalStorage
      follows DataLoader's `process.nextTick`/promise-job scheduling, so the batch runs in the context of the first
      `load()` of its tick). This task pins the behaviour; it adds no production code when it passes. **If it
      FAILS**, make each loader capture the context at creation: in `data-loader.registry.ts`, inside the getter,
      replace `const loader = factory();` with

```ts
// Pin the batch to the operation's async context (AsyncLocalStorage.snapshot, Node ≥ 20).
const loader = AsyncLocalStorage.snapshot()(factory);
```

      (import `AsyncLocalStorage` from `node:async_hooks`), and re-run → Expected: PASS.

libs-vocabulary` → PASS, `bun run check` → exit 0.

### Task 6: The `identity.v1.TenantsService` contract

**Files:**

- Modify: `domains/identity/contracts/src/proto/identity/v1/identity.proto`,
  `domains/identity/contracts/src/generated/identity/v1/identity.pb.ts` (regenerated)
- Test (create): `domains/identity/contracts/src/grpc/tenants-service.contract.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: the identity.v1 proto as left by core Tasks 8-16 (I-9); nothing in the existing messages changes.
- Produces (ts-proto, exported by `@domain/identity-contracts` through `export * from './generated/…'`):

```proto
// Tenancy (B2-12..B2-14). Requests name the acting user and tenant explicitly (copied from the access
// token by the gateway); the principal in the gRPC metadata repeats them and the service cross-checks.
service TenantsService {
  rpc ListMyTenants(ListMyTenantsRequest) returns (MyTenantList);
  rpc CreateTenant(CreateTenantRequest) returns (MyTenant);
  rpc RenameTenant(RenameTenantRequest) returns (Tenant);
  rpc ListMembers(ListMembersRequest) returns (MemberPage);
  rpc UpdateMemberRoles(UpdateMemberRolesRequest) returns (Member);
  rpc RemoveMember(RemoveMemberRequest) returns (google.protobuf.Empty);
  rpc LeaveTenant(LeaveTenantRequest) returns (google.protobuf.Empty);
  rpc CreateInvitation(CreateInvitationRequest) returns (Invitation);
  rpc AcceptInvitation(AcceptInvitationRequest) returns (MyTenant);
}
message Tenant { string id = 1; string slug = 2; string name = 3; bool personal = 4; google.protobuf.Timestamp created_at = 5; }
message MyTenant { Tenant tenant = 1; repeated string roles = 2; google.protobuf.Timestamp last_active_at = 3; }
message MyTenantList { repeated MyTenant items = 1; }
message Member { string user_id = 1; string email = 2; string display_name = 3; repeated string roles = 4; google.protobuf.Timestamp joined_at = 5; }
message MemberPage { repeated Member items = 1; optional string next_cursor = 2; }
message Invitation { string id = 1; string tenant_id = 2; string email = 3; repeated string roles = 4; google.protobuf.Timestamp expires_at = 5; google.protobuf.Timestamp created_at = 6; }
message ListMyTenantsRequest { string user_id = 1; }
message CreateTenantRequest { string user_id = 1; string name = 2; }
message RenameTenantRequest { string tenant_id = 1; string actor_id = 2; string name = 3; }
message ListMembersRequest { string tenant_id = 1; int32 limit = 2; optional string cursor = 3; }
message UpdateMemberRolesRequest { string tenant_id = 1; string actor_id = 2; string user_id = 3; repeated string roles = 4; }
message RemoveMemberRequest { string tenant_id = 1; string actor_id = 2; string user_id = 3; }
message LeaveTenantRequest { string tenant_id = 1; string user_id = 2; }
message CreateInvitationRequest { string tenant_id = 1; string actor_id = 2; string email = 3; repeated string roles = 4; }
message AcceptInvitationRequest { string user_id = 1; string token = 2; }
```

Generated TypeScript (ts-proto, `Timestamp` → `Date`, absent message fields optional): `Tenant`, `MyTenant`
(`tenant?: Tenant`, `roles: string[]`, `lastActiveAt?: Date`), `MyTenantList`, `Member`, `MemberPage`
(`nextCursor?: string`), `Invitation`, the nine `*Request` interfaces, `TENANTS_SERVICE_NAME = 'TenantsService'`,
`TenantsServiceClient`, `TenantsServiceController`, `TenantsServiceControllerMethods`. `MyTenant` (not
`TenantMembership`) avoids clashing with core's repository type `TenantMembership` in identity code.

**Wave:** Lane I, first task. Starts when core `b2/flows` (Tasks 14-16, the last identity.v1 edits of core) and
`b2/context` have merged.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.** Append to `identity.proto`, after the last service block, the `service TenantsService`
      above with one comment line per RPC:

```proto
service TenantsService {
  // The caller's tenants with their roles, most recently active first.
  rpc ListMyTenants(ListMyTenantsRequest) returns (MyTenantList);
  // A new tenant with the caller as its only owner.
  rpc CreateTenant(CreateTenantRequest) returns (MyTenant);
  // Renames the actor's active tenant (owner only: tenant:manage).
  rpc RenameTenant(RenameTenantRequest) returns (Tenant);
  // Members of a tenant (keyset pages on user id, newest first).
  rpc ListMembers(ListMembersRequest) returns (MemberPage);
  // Replaces a member's roles (members:manage; owner rank to grant or revoke owner; never the last owner).
  rpc UpdateMemberRoles(UpdateMemberRolesRequest) returns (Member);
  // Removes a member (members:manage; owner rank for an owner; never the last owner).
  rpc RemoveMember(RemoveMemberRequest) returns (google.protobuf.Empty);
  // The caller leaves a tenant (never the last owner).
  rpc LeaveTenant(LeaveTenantRequest) returns (google.protobuf.Empty);
  // Invites an email with roles and mails the accept link (members:manage; owner rank to invite an owner).
  rpc CreateInvitation(CreateInvitationRequest) returns (Invitation);
  // The logged-in caller (whose email must match) joins the invitation's tenant.
  rpc AcceptInvitation(AcceptInvitationRequest) returns (MyTenant);
}
```

      and the messages of the Interfaces block, one field per line, each message preceded by a one-line comment
      (`// A tenant (workspace).`, `// One of the caller's tenants and their roles in it.`, …; `MemberPage.next_cursor`:
      `// Pass as cursor for the next page; absent on the last page.`; `AcceptInvitationRequest.token`:
      `// The raw token from the invitation mail (base64url, 43 chars); stored hashed.`). Then
      `bun run proto:lint && bun run proto:gen` (buf `STANDARD` lint: request names end in `Request`, shared
      responses are allowed by the `except` list of `libs/contracts/buf.yaml`).

```ts
// application/ports/tenants.port.ts (./api)
export abstract class TenantsPort {
  abstract listMyTenants(input: ListMyTenantsRequest): Promise<MyTenantList>;
  /** @throws DomainValidationException (blank or long name), TenantSlugTakenException (409) */
  abstract createTenant(input: CreateTenantRequest): Promise<MyTenant>;
  /** @throws TenantAccessDeniedException (403), PermissionDeniedException FORBIDDEN (not an owner), EntityNotFoundException */
  abstract renameTenant(input: RenameTenantRequest): Promise<Tenant>;
}
// application/services/tenant-actor.ts (root)
/** The principal acting in `tenantId` as `actorId`, else TenantAccessDeniedException (403). */
export function requireTenantActor(tenantId: string, actorId: string): Principal;
/** The principal, which must be `userId` itself (user-scoped calls: my tenants, create), else TenantAccessDeniedException. */
export function requireSelf(userId: string): Principal;
/** The actor's roles in the tenant: the DB membership (authoritative; a demoted token is stale), or, for a staff
 * switch-in principal (no membership), the token roles. @throws TenantAccessDeniedException */
export function actorTenantRoles(principal: Principal, memberships: MembershipsRepository): Promise<TenantRole[]>;
// application/mappers/tenancy.mapper.ts (root)
export function toTenantContract(tenant: TenantRecord): Tenant;
export function toMyTenantContract(membership: TenantMembership): MyTenant;
export function normalizeTenantName(name: string): string; // trimmed; @throws DomainValidationException (path 'name')
// commands and queries (root)
export class ListMyTenantsQuery extends Query<MyTenantList> { constructor(readonly userId: string) }
export class CreateTenantCommand extends Command<MyTenant> { constructor(readonly userId: string, readonly name: string) }
export class RenameTenantCommand extends Command<Tenant> { constructor(readonly tenantId: string, readonly actorId: string, readonly name: string) }
```

```ts
import { createPrincipal, generateId, runWithPrincipal } from '@platform/common';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { createInMemoryIdentityPersistence } from '../../testing/in-memory-identity.js';
import { actorTenantRoles, requireSelf, requireTenantActor } from './tenant-actor.js';

describe('tenant actor', () => {
  const userId = generateId();
  const tenantId = generateId();
  const as = <T>(roles: ('owner' | 'admin' | 'member')[], staff: boolean, fn: () => T) =>
    runWithPrincipal(createPrincipal({ userId, tenantId, roles, staff }), fn);

  it('requireTenantActor returns the principal acting in the tenant as the actor', () => {
    expect(as(['member'], false, () => requireTenantActor(tenantId, userId))).toMatchObject({
      userId,
      tenantId,
    });
  });

  it.each([
    ['another tenant', () => requireTenantActor(generateId(), userId)],
    ['another actor', () => requireTenantActor(tenantId, generateId())],
  ])('403 TENANT_ACCESS_DENIED for %s', (_label, call) => {
    expect(() => as(['owner'], false, call)).toThrow(
      expect.objectContaining({ code: 'TENANT_ACCESS_DENIED' }),
    );
  });

  it('requireSelf accepts only the principal itself; 401 without one', () => {
    expect(as(['member'], false, () => requireSelf(userId))).toMatchObject({ userId });
    expect(() => as(['owner'], false, () => requireSelf(generateId()))).toThrow(
      expect.objectContaining({ code: 'TENANT_ACCESS_DENIED' }),
    );
    expect(() => requireSelf(userId)).toThrow(
      expect.objectContaining({ code: 'PRINCIPAL_MISSING' }),
    );
  });

  it('actorTenantRoles reads the membership, not the (possibly stale) token roles', async () => {
    const { memberships } = createInMemoryIdentityPersistence();
    await memberships.insert({ tenantId, userId, roles: ['member'], lastActiveAt: null });
    const principal = createPrincipal({ userId, tenantId, roles: ['owner'] });
    await expect(actorTenantRoles(principal, memberships)).resolves.toEqual(['member']);
  });

  it('actorTenantRoles uses the token roles for staff (no membership) and 403s a non-member', async () => {
    const { memberships } = createInMemoryIdentityPersistence();
    const staff = createPrincipal({ userId, tenantId, roles: ['admin'], staff: true });
    await expect(actorTenantRoles(staff, memberships)).resolves.toEqual(['admin']);
    const stranger = createPrincipal({ userId, tenantId, roles: ['owner'] });
    await expect(actorTenantRoles(stranger, memberships)).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'TENANT_ACCESS_DENIED',
    });
  });
});
```

```ts
import { createPrincipal, generateId, runWithPrincipal } from '@platform/common';
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { TENANT_SLUG_PATTERN } from '../../../domain/tenant-slug.js';
import { createInMemoryIdentityPersistence } from '../../../testing/in-memory-identity.js';
import { CreateTenantCommand } from './create-tenant.command.js';
import { CreateTenantHandler } from './create-tenant.handler.js';

describe('CreateTenantHandler', () => {
  let persistence: ReturnType<typeof createInMemoryIdentityPersistence>;
  let handler: CreateTenantHandler;
  const userId = generateId();
  const self = createPrincipal({ userId, tenantId: generateId(), roles: ['owner'] });
  const run = (command: CreateTenantCommand) =>
    runWithPrincipal(self, () => handler.execute(command));

  beforeEach(() => {
    persistence = createInMemoryIdentityPersistence();
    handler = new CreateTenantHandler(
      persistence.tenants,
      persistence.memberships,
      persistence.transactions,
    );
  });

  it('creates a non-personal tenant with the caller as its only owner', async () => {
    const created = await run(new CreateTenantCommand(userId, '  Acme Corp  '));

    expect(created).toMatchObject({
      tenant: { name: 'Acme Corp', personal: false },
      roles: ['owner'],
    });
    expect(created.tenant?.slug).toMatch(TENANT_SLUG_PATTERN);
    expect(created.tenant?.slug).toMatch(/^acme-corp-[0-9a-f]{8}$/);
    await expect(persistence.memberships.find(created.tenant!.id, userId)).resolves.toMatchObject({
      roles: ['owner'],
    });
  });

  it.each(['', '   ', 'x'.repeat(101)])(
    'rejects the name %j with VALIDATION_FAILED',
    async (name) => {
      await expect(run(new CreateTenantCommand(userId, name))).rejects.toMatchObject({
        code: 'VALIDATION_FAILED',
      });
      expect(persistence.store.tenants.size).toBe(0);
    },
  );

  it('403 TENANT_ACCESS_DENIED when the request names another user (gRPC cross-check)', async () => {
    await expect(run(new CreateTenantCommand(generateId(), 'Acme'))).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'TENANT_ACCESS_DENIED',
    });
    expect(persistence.store.tenants.size).toBe(0);
  });
});
```

Create `application/commands/rename-tenant/rename-tenant.handler.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { createPrincipal, generateId, runWithPrincipal, type TenantRole } from '@platform/common';
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { TenantAuditLog } from '../../services/tenant-audit-log.service.js';
import { createInMemoryIdentityPersistence } from '../../../testing/in-memory-identity.js';
import { RenameTenantCommand } from './rename-tenant.command.js';
import { RenameTenantHandler } from './rename-tenant.handler.js';

describe('RenameTenantHandler', () => {
  let persistence: ReturnType<typeof createInMemoryIdentityPersistence>;
  let handler: RenameTenantHandler;
  const tenantId = generateId();
  const owner = generateId();
  const admin = generateId();

  const as = (userId: string, roles: TenantRole[], name: string) =>
    runWithPrincipal(createPrincipal({ userId, tenantId, roles }), () =>
      handler.execute(new RenameTenantCommand(tenantId, userId, name)),
    );

  beforeEach(async () => {
    persistence = createInMemoryIdentityPersistence();
    await persistence.tenants.insert({
      id: tenantId,
      slug: `acme-${tenantId.slice(-8)}`,
      name: 'Acme',
      personal: false,
    });
    await persistence.memberships.insert({
      tenantId,
      userId: owner,
      roles: ['owner'],
      lastActiveAt: null,
    });
    await persistence.memberships.insert({
      tenantId,
      userId: admin,
      roles: ['admin'],
      lastActiveAt: null,
    });
    handler = new RenameTenantHandler(
      persistence.tenants,
      persistence.memberships,
      persistence.transactions,
      new TenantAuditLog(persistence.auditEvents),
    );
  });

  it('renames for an owner and audits tenant.renamed { from, to }', async () => {
    await expect(as(owner, ['owner'], ' Acme Labs ')).resolves.toMatchObject({
      id: tenantId,
      name: 'Acme Labs',
    });
    expect(persistence.store.auditEvents).toEqual([
      expect.objectContaining({
        tenantId,
        actorId: owner,
        type: 'tenant.renamed',
        data: { from: 'Acme', to: 'Acme Labs' },
      }),
    ]);
  });

  it('403 FORBIDDEN for an admin, even with a forged owner token (the membership decides)', async () => {
    await expect(as(admin, ['owner'], 'Hijacked')).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'FORBIDDEN' });
    await expect(persistence.tenants.findById(tenantId)).resolves.toMatchObject({ name: 'Acme' });
  });
});
```

Create `application/queries/list-my-tenants/list-my-tenants.handler.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { createPrincipal, generateId, runWithPrincipal } from '@platform/common';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { createInMemoryIdentityPersistence } from '../../../testing/in-memory-identity.js';
import { ListMyTenantsHandler } from './list-my-tenants.handler.js';
import { ListMyTenantsQuery } from './list-my-tenants.query.js';

describe('ListMyTenantsHandler', () => {
  it("lists the caller's tenants with roles, most recently active first", async () => {
    const persistence = createInMemoryIdentityPersistence();
    const userId = generateId();
    const [a, b] = [generateId(), generateId()];
    await persistence.tenants.insert({
      id: a,
      slug: `a-${a.slice(-8)}`,
      name: 'A',
      personal: true,
    });
    await persistence.tenants.insert({
      id: b,
      slug: `b-${b.slice(-8)}`,
      name: 'B',
      personal: false,
    });
    await persistence.memberships.insert({
      tenantId: a,
      userId,
      roles: ['owner'],
      lastActiveAt: new Date(1_000),
    });
    await persistence.memberships.insert({
      tenantId: b,
      userId,
      roles: ['member'],
      lastActiveAt: new Date(2_000),
    });

    const handler = new ListMyTenantsHandler(persistence.memberships);
    const as = (principalId: string) =>
      runWithPrincipal(
        createPrincipal({ userId: principalId, tenantId: a, roles: ['owner'] }),
        () => handler.execute(new ListMyTenantsQuery(userId)),
      );

    const list = await as(userId);

    expect(list.items.map((t) => [t.tenant?.name, t.roles])).toEqual([
      ['B', ['member']],
      ['A', ['owner']],
    ]);
    // Another user's tenants are never listed (identity-service trusts no request user id).
    await expect(as(generateId())).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'TENANT_ACCESS_DENIED' });
  });
});
```

Create `apps/monolith/test/tenants.e2e-spec.ts` (env pattern of the other monolith e2e specs: `await vi.hoisted(async
  () => { const { MONOLITH_E2E_ENV } = await import('./support/e2e-env.js'); Object.assign(process.env,
  MONOLITH_E2E_ENV); });`):

```ts
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { createMonolithE2eApp, type MonolithE2e } from './support/monolith-e2e-app.js';

await vi.hoisted(async () => {
  const { MONOLITH_E2E_ENV } = await import('./support/e2e-env.js');
  Object.assign(process.env, MONOLITH_E2E_ENV);
});

interface Tokens {
  accessToken: string;
  tenantId: string;
  user: { id: string };
}
const PASSWORD = 'correct horse battery staple';

describe('tenant endpoints (monolith, B2-12)', () => {
  let e2e: MonolithE2e;
  let ada: Tokens;
  const auth = (token: string) => ({ authorization: `Bearer ${token}` });

  beforeAll(async () => {
    e2e = await createMonolithE2eApp();
    const res = await e2e.app.inject({
      method: 'POST',
      url: '/v1/auth/register',
      payload: { email: 'ada.tenants@example.com', password: PASSWORD, displayName: 'Ada' },
    });
    ada = res.json<Tokens>();
  });

  afterAll(async () => {
    await e2e?.app.close();
  });

  it('GET /v1/tenants lists the personal workspace; POST creates one with me as owner', async () => {
    const before = await e2e.app.inject({
      method: 'GET',
      url: '/v1/tenants',
      headers: auth(ada.accessToken),
    });
    expect(before.statusCode, before.body).toBe(200);
    expect(before.json()).toEqual([
      expect.objectContaining({ id: ada.tenantId, personal: true, roles: ['owner'] }),
    ]);

    const created = await e2e.app.inject({
      method: 'POST',
      url: '/v1/tenants',
      headers: auth(ada.accessToken),
      payload: { name: 'Acme' },
    });
    expect(created.statusCode, created.body).toBe(201);
    expect(created.json()).toMatchObject({ name: 'Acme', personal: false, roles: ['owner'] });

    const after = await e2e.app.inject({
      method: 'GET',
      url: '/v1/tenants',
      headers: auth(ada.accessToken),
    });
    expect(after.json<unknown[]>()).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  it('PATCH /v1/tenant renames the active tenant for its owner; 400 for a blank name', async () => {
    const ok = await e2e.app.inject({
      method: 'PATCH',
      url: '/v1/tenant',
      headers: auth(ada.accessToken),
      payload: { name: 'Ada Labs' },
    });
    expect(ok.statusCode, ok.body).toBe(200);
    expect(ok.json()).toMatchObject({ id: ada.tenantId, name: 'Ada Labs' });

    const blank = await e2e.app.inject({
      method: 'PATCH',
      url: '/v1/tenant',
      headers: auth(ada.accessToken),
      payload: { name: '   ' },
    });
    expect(blank.statusCode).toBe(400);
  });

  it('PATCH /v1/tenant is 403 for a member (no tenant:manage)', async () => {
    const bob = await e2e.app.inject({
      method: 'POST',
      url: '/v1/auth/register',
      payload: { email: 'bob.tenants@example.com', password: PASSWORD, displayName: 'Bob' },
    });
    const bobId = bob.json<Tokens>().user.id;
    await e2e.identity.memberships.insert({
      tenantId: ada.tenantId,
      userId: bobId,
      roles: ['member'],
      lastActiveAt: null,
    });
    const login = await e2e.app.inject({
      method: 'POST',
      url: '/v1/auth/login',
      payload: { email: 'bob.tenants@example.com', password: PASSWORD, tenantId: ada.tenantId },
    });
    expect(login.statusCode, login.body).toBe(200);

    const res = await e2e.app.inject({
      method: 'PATCH',
      url: '/v1/tenant',
      headers: auth(login.json<Tokens>().accessToken),
      payload: { name: 'Bob was here' },
    });
    expect(res.statusCode).toBe(403);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the application layer.** Create `application/services/tenant-actor.ts`:

```ts
import { type Principal, requirePrincipal, type TenantRole } from '@platform/common';
import { TenantAccessDeniedException } from '../../domain/identity.errors.js';
import type { MembershipsRepository } from '../persistence/memberships.repository.js';

/**
 * The request's principal, which must be acting in `tenantId` as `actorId` (both copied from the
 * access token by the edge; this cross-checks the CLS principal of JwtAuthGuard or
 * GrpcContextInterceptor). One error for every mismatch, as for login and switch.
 */
export function requireTenantActor(tenantId: string, actorId: string): Principal {
  const principal = requirePrincipal();
  if (principal.tenantId !== tenantId || principal.userId !== actorId) {
    throw new TenantAccessDeniedException();
  }
  return principal;
}

/**
 * User-scoped calls (my tenants, create a tenant): the request's user must be the principal. The
 * gateway copies both from the same token; identity-service trusts no request user id on its own.
 */
export function requireSelf(userId: string): Principal {
  const principal = requirePrincipal();
  if (principal.userId !== userId) throw new TenantAccessDeniedException();
  return principal;
}

/**
 * The actor's roles in its tenant. The membership row is authoritative (an access token keeps the
 * roles it was issued with for its whole TTL); a staff switch-in principal has no membership and acts
 * with the token's `admin` role (spec §5.2).
 */
export async function actorTenantRoles(
  principal: Principal,
  memberships: MembershipsRepository,
): Promise<TenantRole[]> {
  if (principal.tenantId === null) throw new TenantAccessDeniedException();
  if (principal.staff) return [...principal.roles];
  const membership = await memberships.find(principal.tenantId, principal.userId);
  if (membership === null) throw new TenantAccessDeniedException();
  return [...membership.roles];
}
```

Create `application/mappers/tenancy.mapper.ts`:

```ts
import type { MyTenant, Tenant } from '@domain/identity-contracts';
import { DomainValidationException } from '@platform/common';
import { IDENTITY_LIMITS } from '../../identity.constants.js';
import type { TenantMembership } from '../persistence/memberships.repository.js';
import type { TenantRecord } from '../persistence/tenants.repository.js';

export function toTenantContract(tenant: TenantRecord): Tenant {
  return {
    id: tenant.id,
    slug: tenant.slug,
    name: tenant.name,
    personal: tenant.personal,
    createdAt: tenant.createdAt,
  };
}

export function toMyTenantContract(membership: TenantMembership): MyTenant {
  return {
    tenant: toTenantContract(membership.tenant),
    roles: [...membership.roles],
    ...(membership.lastActiveAt === null ? {} : { lastActiveAt: membership.lastActiveAt }),
  };
}

/** Trimmed tenant name, 1..TENANT_NAME_MAX_LENGTH characters (code points). */
export function normalizeTenantName(name: string): string {
  const trimmed = name.trim();
  const length = [...trimmed].length;
  if (length === 0 || length > IDENTITY_LIMITS.TENANT_NAME_MAX_LENGTH) {
    throw new DomainValidationException('Invalid tenant name', {
      issues: [
        { path: 'name', message: `Must be 1-${IDENTITY_LIMITS.TENANT_NAME_MAX_LENGTH} characters` },
      ],
    });
  }
  return trimmed;
}
```

```ts
// application/queries/list-my-tenants/list-my-tenants.handler.ts
import type { MyTenantList } from '@domain/identity-contracts';
import { type IQueryHandler, QueryHandler } from '@nestjs/cqrs';
import { toMyTenantContract } from '../../mappers/tenancy.mapper.js';
import { MembershipsRepository } from '../../persistence/memberships.repository.js';
import { requireSelf } from '../../services/tenant-actor.js';
import { ListMyTenantsQuery } from './list-my-tenants.query.js';

@QueryHandler(ListMyTenantsQuery)
export class ListMyTenantsHandler implements IQueryHandler<ListMyTenantsQuery> {
  constructor(private readonly memberships: MembershipsRepository) {}

  async execute({ userId }: ListMyTenantsQuery): Promise<MyTenantList> {
    requireSelf(userId);
    return { items: (await this.memberships.listForUser(userId)).map(toMyTenantContract) };
  }
}
```

```ts
// application/commands/create-tenant/create-tenant.handler.ts
import type { MyTenant } from '@domain/identity-contracts';
import { CommandHandler, type ICommandHandler } from '@nestjs/cqrs';
import { generateId } from '@platform/common';
import { deriveTenantSlug } from '../../../domain/tenant-slug.js';
import { normalizeTenantName, toMyTenantContract } from '../../mappers/tenancy.mapper.js';
import { MembershipsRepository } from '../../persistence/memberships.repository.js';
import { TenantsRepository } from '../../persistence/tenants.repository.js';
import { TransactionRunner } from '../../persistence/transaction-runner.js';
import { requireSelf } from '../../services/tenant-actor.js';
import { CreateTenantCommand } from './create-tenant.command.js';

@CommandHandler(CreateTenantCommand)
export class CreateTenantHandler implements ICommandHandler<CreateTenantCommand> {
  constructor(
    private readonly tenants: TenantsRepository,
    private readonly memberships: MembershipsRepository,
    private readonly transactions: TransactionRunner,
  ) {}

  async execute({ userId, name }: CreateTenantCommand): Promise<MyTenant> {
    requireSelf(userId);
    const tenantName = normalizeTenantName(name);
    const id = generateId();
    // Tenant and owner membership in one transaction: never a tenant without an owner.
    await this.transactions.run(async () => {
      await this.tenants.insert({
        id,
        slug: deriveTenantSlug(tenantName, id),
        name: tenantName,
        personal: false,
      });
      await this.memberships.insert({ tenantId: id, userId, roles: ['owner'], lastActiveAt: null });
    });
    const membership = (await this.memberships.listForUser(userId)).find((m) => m.tenantId === id);
    if (membership === undefined)
      throw new Error(`Tenant ${id} was created without its owner membership`);
    return toMyTenantContract(membership);
  }
}
```

```ts
import { Command } from '@nestjs/cqrs';

/** Renames the actor's active tenant (owners only: `tenant:manage`). Audited. */
export class RenameTenantCommand extends Command<Tenant> {

    readonly actorId: string,
    readonly name: string,

```

```ts
// application/commands/rename-tenant/rename-tenant.handler.ts
import type { Tenant } from '@domain/identity-contracts';
import { CommandHandler, type ICommandHandler } from '@nestjs/cqrs';
import {
  EntityNotFoundException,
  PermissionDeniedException,
  satisfiesTenantRole,
} from '@platform/common';
import { normalizeTenantName, toTenantContract } from '../../mappers/tenancy.mapper.js';
import { AUDIT_EVENT_TYPES } from '../../persistence/audit-events.repository.js';
import { MembershipsRepository } from '../../persistence/memberships.repository.js';
import { TenantsRepository } from '../../persistence/tenants.repository.js';
import { TransactionRunner } from '../../persistence/transaction-runner.js';
import { TenantAuditLog } from '../../services/tenant-audit-log.service.js';
import { actorTenantRoles, requireTenantActor } from '../../services/tenant-actor.js';
import { RenameTenantCommand } from './rename-tenant.command.js';

@CommandHandler(RenameTenantCommand)
export class RenameTenantHandler implements ICommandHandler<RenameTenantCommand> {
  constructor(
    private readonly tenants: TenantsRepository,
    private readonly memberships: MembershipsRepository,
    private readonly transactions: TransactionRunner,
    private readonly audit: TenantAuditLog,
  ) {}

  async execute({ tenantId, actorId, name }: RenameTenantCommand): Promise<Tenant> {
    const principal = requireTenantActor(tenantId, actorId);
    const to = normalizeTenantName(name);
    if (!satisfiesTenantRole(await actorTenantRoles(principal, this.memberships), 'owner')) {
      throw new PermissionDeniedException('Only an owner can rename the workspace', {
        details: { required: ['tenant:manage'] },
      });
    }
    return this.transactions.run(async () => {
      const tenant = await this.tenants.findById(tenantId);
      if (tenant === null) throw new EntityNotFoundException('Tenant', tenantId);
      await this.tenants.rename(tenantId, to);
      await this.audit.record({
        tenantId,
        actorId,
        type: AUDIT_EVENT_TYPES.TENANT_RENAMED,
        data: { from: tenant.name, to },
      });
      return toTenantContract({ ...tenant, name: to });
    });
  }
}
```

      Interfaces block (imports types from `@domain/identity-contracts`; class doc "Tenancy for the presentation
      layer: local adapter = CQRS buses, remote adapter = `identity.v1.TenantsService` (Task 11)"). Create
      `infrastructure/adapters/local/tenants-local.adapter.ts`:

```ts
import type {
  CreateTenantRequest,
  ListMyTenantsRequest,
  MyTenant,
  MyTenantList,
  RenameTenantRequest,
  Tenant,
} from '@domain/identity-contracts';
import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { CommandBus, QueryBus } from '@nestjs/cqrs';
import { CreateTenantCommand } from '../../../application/commands/create-tenant/create-tenant.command.js';
import { RenameTenantCommand } from '../../../application/commands/rename-tenant/rename-tenant.command.js';
import type { TenantsPort } from '../../../application/ports/tenants.port.js';
import { ListMyTenantsQuery } from '../../../application/queries/list-my-tenants/list-my-tenants.query.js';

/** Monolith binding of `TenantsPort`: the CQRS buses of `IdentityCoreModule`. */
@Injectable()
export class TenantsLocalAdapter implements TenantsPort {
  constructor(
    private readonly commandBus: CommandBus,
    private readonly queryBus: QueryBus,
  ) {}

  listMyTenants(input: ListMyTenantsRequest): Promise<MyTenantList> {
    return this.queryBus.execute(new ListMyTenantsQuery(input.userId));
  }

  createTenant(input: CreateTenantRequest): Promise<MyTenant> {
    return this.commandBus.execute(new CreateTenantCommand(input.userId, input.name));
  }

  renameTenant(input: RenameTenantRequest): Promise<Tenant> {
    return this.commandBus.execute(
      new RenameTenantCommand(input.tenantId, input.actorId, input.name),
    );
  }
}
```

      Create `presentation/http/responses/tenant.response.ts`:

```ts
import type { MyTenant, Tenant } from '@domain/identity-contracts';
import { ApiProperty, ApiPropertyOptional } from '@nestjs/swagger';
import { normalizeTenantRoles, TENANT_ROLES, type TenantRole } from '@platform/common';

export class TenantResponse {
  @ApiProperty({ format: 'uuid' }) id: string;
  @ApiProperty() slug: string;
  @ApiProperty() name: string;
  @ApiProperty({ description: 'The workspace created at registration' }) personal: boolean;

  static from(tenant: Tenant): TenantResponse {
    return Object.assign(new TenantResponse(), {
      id: tenant.id,
      slug: tenant.slug,
      name: tenant.name,
      personal: tenant.personal,
    });
  }
}

export class MyTenantResponse extends TenantResponse {
  @ApiProperty({ enum: TENANT_ROLES, isArray: true }) roles: TenantRole[];
  @ApiPropertyOptional({ type: String, format: 'date-time', nullable: true }) lastActiveAt:
    string | null;

  static fromMine(mine: MyTenant): MyTenantResponse {
    if (mine.tenant === undefined) throw new Error('MyTenant without its tenant');
    return Object.assign(new MyTenantResponse(), TenantResponse.from(mine.tenant), {
      roles: normalizeTenantRoles(mine.roles),
      lastActiveAt: mine.lastActiveAt?.toISOString() ?? null,
    });
  }
}
```

```ts
import { Body, Controller, Get, Patch, Post } from '@nestjs/common';
import {
  ApiBearerAuth,
  ApiCreatedResponse,
  ApiForbiddenResponse,
  ApiOkResponse,
  ApiOperation,
  ApiTags,
  ApiUnauthorizedResponse,
} from '@nestjs/swagger';
import { type AuthUser, CurrentUser, RequirePermissions } from '@platform/auth';
import { TenantsPort } from '../../application/ports/tenants.port.js';
import { IdentityPermission } from '../../identity.permissions.js';
import { TenantNameDto } from './dto/tenant-name.dto.js';
import { ProblemResponse } from './responses/problem.response.js';
import { MyTenantResponse, TenantResponse } from './responses/tenant.response.js';

/** `/v1/tenants` (mine, create) and `/v1/tenant` (the active tenant), identical in both topologies. */
@ApiTags('tenants')
@ApiBearerAuth()
@ApiUnauthorizedResponse({ type: ProblemResponse })
@Controller({ version: '1' })
export class TenantsController {
  constructor(private readonly tenants: TenantsPort) {}

  @Get('tenants')
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'My tenants and my roles in each, most recently active first' })
  @ApiOkResponse({ type: MyTenantResponse, isArray: true })
  async listMine(@CurrentUser('id') userId: string): Promise<MyTenantResponse[]> {
    const list = await this.tenants.listMyTenants({ userId });
    return list.items.map((mine) => MyTenantResponse.fromMine(mine));
  }

  @Post('tenants')
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Create a tenant with me as its owner' })
  @ApiCreatedResponse({ type: MyTenantResponse })
  async create(
    @Body() body: TenantNameDto,
    @CurrentUser('id') userId: string,
  ): Promise<MyTenantResponse> {
    return MyTenantResponse.fromMine(await this.tenants.createTenant({ userId, name: body.name }));
  }

  @Patch('tenant')
  @RequirePermissions(IdentityPermission.TenantManage)
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Rename the active tenant (owners: tenant:manage)' })
  @ApiOkResponse({ type: TenantResponse })
  @ApiForbiddenResponse({ type: ProblemResponse, description: 'Requires `tenant:manage`.' })
  async rename(
    @Body() body: TenantNameDto,
    @CurrentUser() user: AuthUser,
  ): Promise<TenantResponse> {
    return TenantResponse.from(
      await this.tenants.renameTenant({
        tenantId: user.tenantId,
        actorId: user.id,
        name: body.name,
      }),
    );
  }
}
```

      (`IdentityPermission` lives in `domains/identity/src/identity.permissions.ts` since core Task 2; adjust the

      its handler arrays; `identity-api.module.ts` `forLocal()` gains `TenantsController` in `controllers` (a local
      `TENANCY_CONTROLLERS = [TenantsController]` array, spread after `CONTROLLERS`) and

      (`forRemote()` gets them in Task 11, when the gRPC adapter exists). `src/index.ts` (root): export

```ts
// application/persistence/memberships.repository.ts (additions to core's abstract class)
export interface MemberRecord {
  readonly userId: string; readonly email: string; readonly displayName: string;
  readonly roles: readonly TenantRole[]; readonly joinedAt: Date;
}
abstract updateRoles(tenantId: string, userId: string, roles: readonly TenantRole[]): Promise<boolean>;
abstract delete(tenantId: string, userId: string): Promise<boolean>;
/** pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext('identity:tenant-owners:' || tenantId)), then the number of owners. Call it
 *  first inside the unit of work of every change that can remove an owner. */
abstract countOwnersLocked(tenantId: string): Promise<number>;
abstract findMember(tenantId: string, userId: string): Promise<MemberRecord | null>;
/** Keyset page on user_id (newest user first) within the tenant; cursor = encodeIdCursor(userId). */
abstract listMembers(tenantId: string, criteria: { limit: number; cursor?: string | undefined }): Promise<CursorPage<MemberRecord>>;
// domain/member-roles.policy.ts
/** @throws InvalidRolesException (empty, unknown or duplicated values) */
export function parseTenantRoles(values: readonly unknown[]): TenantRole[];
/** members:manage (admin rank) to change roles; owner rank when `current` or `next` contains owner. */
export function assertCanAssignRoles(actor: readonly TenantRole[], current: readonly TenantRole[] | null, next: readonly TenantRole[]): void;
/** members:manage to remove; owner rank to remove an owner. */
export function assertCanRemove(actor: readonly TenantRole[], target: readonly TenantRole[]): void;
// identity.constants.ts / identity.errors.ts
IdentityErrorCode.LAST_OWNER = 'LAST_OWNER';                     // LastOwnerException extends BusinessRuleViolationException (422)
IdentityErrorCode.OWNER_RANK_REQUIRED = 'OWNER_RANK_REQUIRED';   // OwnerRankRequiredException extends PermissionDeniedException (403)
// application/ports/members.port.ts (./api)
export abstract class MembersPort {
  abstract listMembers(input: ListMembersRequest): Promise<MemberPage>;
  abstract updateMemberRoles(input: UpdateMemberRolesRequest): Promise<Member>;
  abstract removeMember(input: RemoveMemberRequest): Promise<void>;
  abstract leaveTenant(input: LeaveTenantRequest): Promise<void>;
}
// commands and queries (root)
export class ListMembersQuery extends Query<MemberPage> { constructor(readonly tenantId: string, readonly limit: number, readonly cursor?: string) }
export class UpdateMemberRolesCommand extends Command<Member> { constructor(readonly tenantId: string, readonly actorId: string, readonly userId: string, readonly roles: readonly string[]) }
export class RemoveMemberCommand extends Command<void> { constructor(readonly tenantId: string, readonly actorId: string, readonly userId: string) }
export class LeaveTenantCommand extends Command<void> { constructor(readonly tenantId: string, readonly userId: string) }
export function toMemberContract(member: MemberRecord): Member;
```

REST (`MembersController`): `GET /v1/tenant/members?limit&cursor` → 200 `MemberPageResponse` (any member of the
active tenant); `PATCH /v1/tenant/members/:userId { roles }` → 200 `MemberResponse`
(`@RequirePermissions(IdentityPermission.MembersManage)`); `DELETE /v1/tenant/members/:userId` → 204 (same);
`POST /v1/tenant/leave` → 204. Errors: 403 `FORBIDDEN` / `OWNER_RANK_REQUIRED` / `TENANT_ACCESS_DENIED`, 404
`NOT_FOUND` (entity `Member`), 422 `LAST_OWNER` / `INVALID_ROLES`. Audited: `member.roles_changed { userId, from, to
  }`, `member.removed { userId, roles }`, `member.left { roles }`. The global last-admin lock is already gone (core
Task 11); this per-tenant lock replaces it.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.** Create `domain/member-roles.policy.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { assertCanAssignRoles, assertCanRemove, parseTenantRoles } from './member-roles.policy.js';

describe('member roles policy', () => {
  it.each([[[]], [['root']], [['admin', 'admin']], [['owner', 7]]])(
    'parseTenantRoles rejects %j',
    (values) => {
      expect(() => parseTenantRoles(values)).toThrow(
        expect.objectContaining({ code: 'INVALID_ROLES' }),
      );
    },
  );

  it('parseTenantRoles returns canonical order', () => {
    expect(parseTenantRoles(['member', 'owner'])).toEqual(['owner', 'member']);
  });

  it('a member cannot change roles (FORBIDDEN)', () => {
    expect(() => assertCanAssignRoles(['member'], ['member'], ['admin'])).toThrow(
      expect.objectContaining({ code: 'FORBIDDEN' }),
    );
  });

  it('an admin manages admins and members, but needs owner rank to grant or revoke owner', () => {
    expect(() => assertCanAssignRoles(['admin'], ['member'], ['admin'])).not.toThrow();
    expect(() => assertCanAssignRoles(['admin'], ['member'], ['owner'])).toThrow(
      expect.objectContaining({ code: 'OWNER_RANK_REQUIRED' }),
    );
    expect(() => assertCanAssignRoles(['admin'], ['owner'], ['admin'])).toThrow(
      expect.objectContaining({ code: 'OWNER_RANK_REQUIRED' }),
    );
    expect(() => assertCanAssignRoles(['owner'], ['owner'], ['admin'])).not.toThrow();
  });

  it('removing an owner needs owner rank', () => {
    expect(() => assertCanRemove(['admin'], ['member'])).not.toThrow();
    expect(() => assertCanRemove(['admin'], ['owner'])).toThrow(
      expect.objectContaining({ code: 'OWNER_RANK_REQUIRED' }),
    );
    expect(() => assertCanRemove(['member'], ['member'])).toThrow(
      expect.objectContaining({ code: 'FORBIDDEN' }),
    );
  });
});
```

Create `application/commands/update-member-roles/update-member-roles.handler.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { createPrincipal, generateId, runWithPrincipal, type TenantRole } from '@platform/common';
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { UserAggregate } from '../../../domain/user.aggregate.js';
import { createInMemoryIdentityPersistence } from '../../../testing/in-memory-identity.js';
import { TenantAuditLog } from '../../services/tenant-audit-log.service.js';
import { UpdateMemberRolesCommand } from './update-member-roles.command.js';
import { UpdateMemberRolesHandler } from './update-member-roles.handler.js';

describe('UpdateMemberRolesHandler', () => {
  let p: ReturnType<typeof createInMemoryIdentityPersistence>;
  let handler: UpdateMemberRolesHandler;
  const tenantId = generateId();
  const [ada, bob, eve] = [generateId(), generateId(), generateId()];

  const member = async (userId: string, roles: TenantRole[]) => {
    // InMemoryUsersRepository.insert takes the aggregate (it stores `user.toSnapshot()`).
    await p.users.insert(
      UserAggregate.register({
        id: userId,
        email: `${userId}@example.com`,
        displayName: `user ${userId.slice(-4)}`,
        passwordHash: 'x',
        now: new Date(),
      }),
    );
    await p.memberships.insert({ tenantId, userId, roles, lastActiveAt: null });
  };
  const as = (actor: string, target: string, roles: string[], staff = false) =>
    runWithPrincipal(
      createPrincipal({ userId: actor, tenantId, roles: staff ? ['admin'] : ['owner'], staff }),
      () => handler.execute(new UpdateMemberRolesCommand(tenantId, actor, target, roles)),
    );

  beforeEach(async () => {
    p = createInMemoryIdentityPersistence();
    await member(ada, ['owner']);
    await member(bob, ['admin']);
    await member(eve, ['member']);
    handler = new UpdateMemberRolesHandler(
      p.memberships,
      p.transactions,
      new TenantAuditLog(p.auditEvents),
    );
  });

  it('an admin promotes a member to admin; audited member.roles_changed', async () => {
    await expect(as(bob, eve, ['admin'])).resolves.toMatchObject({ userId: eve, roles: ['admin'] });
    expect(p.store.auditEvents).toEqual([
      expect.objectContaining({
        type: 'member.roles_changed',
        actorId: bob,
        data: { userId: eve, from: ['member'], to: ['admin'] },
      }),
    ]);
  });

  it('422 LAST_OWNER when the sole owner demotes themself', async () => {
    await expect(as(ada, ada, ['admin'])).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'LAST_OWNER' });
    await expect(p.memberships.find(tenantId, ada)).resolves.toMatchObject({ roles: ['owner'] });
  });

  it('an owner may step down once another owner exists', async () => {
    await as(ada, bob, ['owner']);
    await expect(as(ada, ada, ['admin'])).resolves.toMatchObject({ roles: ['admin'] });
  });

  it('403 OWNER_RANK_REQUIRED for an admin touching the owner role (token roles do not matter)', async () => {
    await expect(as(bob, eve, ['owner'])).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'OWNER_RANK_REQUIRED' });
    await expect(as(bob, ada, ['member'])).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'OWNER_RANK_REQUIRED' });
  });

  it('staff switch-in (admin, no membership) manages members but never owners', async () => {
    const staff = generateId();
    await expect(as(staff, eve, ['admin'], true)).resolves.toMatchObject({ roles: ['admin'] });
    await expect(as(staff, ada, ['member'], true)).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'OWNER_RANK_REQUIRED',
    });
  });

  it('404 for a user who is not a member; 422 INVALID_ROLES for unknown roles', async () => {
    await expect(as(ada, generateId(), ['member'])).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'NOT_FOUND' });
    await expect(as(ada, eve, ['root'])).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'INVALID_ROLES' });
  });

  it('403 FORBIDDEN for a member', async () => {
    await expect(as(eve, eve, ['admin'])).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'FORBIDDEN' });
  });
});
```

```ts
describe('members (B2-13)', () => {
  it('listMembers pages inside the tenant with user profiles; updateRoles and delete report a match', async () => {
    const tenantId = await newTenant('members');
    const ids = [await newUser('m1'), await newUser('m2'), await newUser('m3')];
    for (const userId of ids) {
      await memberships.insert({ tenantId, userId, roles: ['member'], lastActiveAt: null });
    }
    const first = await memberships.listMembers(tenantId, { limit: 2 });
    expect(first.items.map((m) => m.userId)).toEqual([...ids].sort().reverse().slice(0, 2));
    expect(first.items[0]).toMatchObject({
      email: expect.stringContaining('@'),
      roles: ['member'],
    });
    const rest = await memberships.listMembers(tenantId, {
      limit: 2,
      cursor: first.nextCursor ?? undefined,
    });
    expect(rest.items).toHaveLength(1);
    await expect(memberships.updateRoles(tenantId, ids[0]!, ['admin'])).resolves.toBe(true);
    await expect(memberships.updateRoles(generateId(), ids[0]!, ['admin'])).resolves.toBe(false);
    await expect(memberships.delete(tenantId, ids[1]!)).resolves.toBe(true);
    await expect(memberships.findMember(tenantId, ids[1]!)).resolves.toBeNull();
  });

  it('countOwnersLocked serialises owner changes: two owners demoting each other leave exactly one', async () => {
    const tenantId = await newTenant('owners');
    const [a, b] = [await newUser('oa'), await newUser('ob')];
    await memberships.insert({ tenantId, userId: a, roles: ['owner'], lastActiveAt: null });
    await memberships.insert({ tenantId, userId: b, roles: ['owner'], lastActiveAt: null });
    const demote = (target: string) =>
      transactions.run(async () => {
        const owners = await memberships.countOwnersLocked(tenantId);
        await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 100)); // hold the lock
        if (owners <= 1) return false;
        return memberships.updateRoles(tenantId, target, ['admin']);
      });
    const results = await Promise.all([demote(a), demote(b)]);
    expect(results.filter(Boolean)).toHaveLength(1);
    await expect(memberships.countOwnersLocked(tenantId)).resolves.toBe(1);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Errors and policy.** `identity.constants.ts` `IdentityErrorCode` gains

```ts
/** 422 — the change would leave the tenant without an owner. */
export class LastOwnerException extends BusinessRuleViolationException {
  constructor() {
    super('A workspace must keep at least one owner', { code: IdentityErrorCode.LAST_OWNER });
  }
}

/** 403 — only an owner can grant, revoke or remove the owner role. */
export class OwnerRankRequiredException extends PermissionDeniedException {
  constructor() {
    super('Only an owner can change or remove an owner', {
      code: IdentityErrorCode.OWNER_RANK_REQUIRED,
    });
  }
}
```

      Create `domain/member-roles.policy.ts`:

```ts
import {
  isTenantRole,
  normalizeTenantRoles,
  PermissionDeniedException,
  satisfiesTenantRole,
  type TenantRole,
} from '@platform/common';
import { InvalidRolesException, OwnerRankRequiredException } from './identity.errors.js';

/** Exact tenant roles: non-empty, known, no duplicates; canonical order. */
export function parseTenantRoles(values: readonly unknown[]): TenantRole[] {
  const unknown = values.filter((value) => !isTenantRole(value));
  if (values.length === 0 || unknown.length > 0 || new Set(values).size !== values.length) {
    throw new InvalidRolesException([
      { path: 'roles', message: 'One or more of owner, admin, member, without duplicates' },
    ]);
  }
  return normalizeTenantRoles(values);
}

const requireManager = (actor: readonly TenantRole[]): void => {
  if (!satisfiesTenantRole(actor, 'admin')) {
    throw new PermissionDeniedException('Managing members requires members:manage', {
      details: { required: ['members:manage'] },
    });
  }
};

export function assertCanAssignRoles(
  actor: readonly TenantRole[],
  current: readonly TenantRole[] | null,
  next: readonly TenantRole[],
): void {
  requireManager(actor);
  const touchesOwner = (current ?? []).includes('owner') || next.includes('owner');
  if (touchesOwner && !satisfiesTenantRole(actor, 'owner')) throw new OwnerRankRequiredException();
}

export function assertCanRemove(actor: readonly TenantRole[], target: readonly TenantRole[]): void {
  requireManager(actor);
  if (target.includes('owner') && !satisfiesTenantRole(actor, 'owner'))
    throw new OwnerRankRequiredException();
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Repository additions.** `memberships.repository.ts`: add `MemberRecord` and the five abstract methods of
      the Interfaces block (import `CursorPage` from `@platform/common`). `drizzle-memberships.repository.ts`: add to
      the imports `count`, `lt` from `drizzle-orm`, `keysetFetchLimit`, `keysetOrder`, `keysetPageBy`,
      `decodeIdCursor` from `@platform/database`, `type CursorPage` from `@platform/common`, `users` from
      `./identity.schema.js`, `type MemberRecord` from the port file, and append the methods:

```ts
  async updateRoles(tenantId: string, userId: string, roles: readonly TenantRole[]): Promise<boolean> {
    const updated = await this.txHost.tx
      .update(memberships)
      .set({ roles: [...roles] })
      .where(membershipOf(tenantId, userId))
      .returning({ userId: memberships.userId });
    return updated.length > 0;
  }

  async delete(tenantId: string, userId: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const deleted = await this.txHost.tx
      .delete(memberships)
      .where(membershipOf(tenantId, userId))
      .returning({ userId: memberships.userId });
    return deleted.length > 0;
  }

  async countOwnersLocked(tenantId: string): Promise<number> {
    const tx = this.txHost.tx;
    // Per-tenant mutex for owner changes (replaces the old global last-admin lock); released at
    // COMMIT/ROLLBACK, so it is safe behind PgBouncer transaction pooling (a session lock is not)
    // and runs on the Citus coordinator. Advisory locks only: no FOR UPDATE on Citus (sharding
    // design §2.1.6). Relies on READ COMMITTED (the transaction runner's default): the count below
    // is a separate statement, so its snapshot is taken after the lock is granted and sees the
    // owner change the previous holder committed. Must be called before any other read of the
    // unit of work.
    await tx.execute(sql`select pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext(${`identity:tenant-owners:${tenantId}`}))`);
    const [row] = await tx
      .select({ owners: count() })
      .from(memberships)
      .where(and(eq(memberships.tenantId, tenantId), sql`'owner' = any(${memberships.roles})`));
    return row?.owners ?? 0;
  }

  async findMember(tenantId: string, userId: string): Promise<MemberRecord | null> {
    const [row] = await this.memberQuery().where(membershipOf(tenantId, userId)).limit(1);
    return row ?? null;
  }

  async listMembers(
    tenantId: string,
    criteria: { limit: number; cursor?: string | undefined },
  ): Promise<CursorPage<MemberRecord>> {
    const after = criteria.cursor === undefined || criteria.cursor === '' ? undefined : decodeIdCursor(criteria.cursor).id;
    const rows = await this.memberQuery()
      .where(
        and(eq(memberships.tenantId, tenantId), after === undefined ? undefined : lt(memberships.userId, after)),
      )
      .orderBy(keysetOrder(memberships.userId))
      .limit(keysetFetchLimit(criteria.limit));
    return keysetPageBy(rows, criteria.limit, (member) => ({ id: member.userId }));
  }

  /** memberships ⋈ users: both reference tables, so the join is local on every Citus node. */
  private memberQuery() {
    return this.txHost.tx
      .select({
        userId: memberships.userId,
        email: users.email,
        displayName: users.displayName,
        roles: memberships.roles,
        joinedAt: memberships.createdAt,
      })
      .from(memberships)
      .innerJoin(users, eq(users.id, memberships.userId))
      .$dynamic();
  }
```

      `testing/in-memory-tenancy.ts` `InMemoryMembershipsRepository` appends (imports `decodeIdCursor`,
      `keysetPageBy` from `@platform/database`, `type MemberRecord`, `type CursorPage`):

```ts
  async updateRoles(tenantId: string, userId: string, roles: readonly TenantRole[]): Promise<boolean> {
    const key = membershipKey(tenantId, userId);
    const membership = this.store.memberships.get(key);
    if (!membership) return false;
    this.store.memberships.set(key, { ...membership, roles: normalizeTenantRoles(roles) });
    return true;
  }

  async delete(tenantId: string, userId: string): Promise<boolean> {
    return this.store.memberships.delete(membershipKey(tenantId, userId));
  }

  /** Single-threaded fake: no lock needed; the count is what the Drizzle version returns. */
  async countOwnersLocked(tenantId: string): Promise<number> {
    return [...this.store.memberships.values()].filter((m) => m.tenantId === tenantId && m.roles.includes('owner'))
      .length;
  }

  async findMember(tenantId: string, userId: string): Promise<MemberRecord | null> {
    const membership = this.store.memberships.get(membershipKey(tenantId, userId));
    return membership ? this.toMember(membership) : null;
  }

  async listMembers(
    tenantId: string,
    criteria: { limit: number; cursor?: string | undefined },
  ): Promise<CursorPage<MemberRecord>> {
    const after = criteria.cursor === undefined ? undefined : decodeIdCursor(criteria.cursor).id;
    const rows = [...this.store.memberships.values()]
      .filter((m) => m.tenantId === tenantId && (after === undefined || m.userId < after))
      .sort((a, b) => b.userId.localeCompare(a.userId))
      .slice(0, criteria.limit + 1)
      .flatMap((m) => {
        const member = this.toMember(m);
        return member ? [member] : [];
      });
    return keysetPageBy(rows, criteria.limit, (member) => ({ id: member.userId }));
  }

  private toMember(membership: MembershipRecord): MemberRecord | null {
    const user = this.store.users.get(membership.userId);
    if (!user) return null;
    return {
      userId: membership.userId,
      email: user.email,
      displayName: user.displayName,
      roles: membership.roles,
      joinedAt: membership.createdAt,
    };
  }
```

```ts
import type { Member } from '@domain/identity-contracts';
import type { MemberRecord } from '../persistence/memberships.repository.js';

export function toMemberContract(member: MemberRecord): Member {
  return {
    userId: member.userId,
    email: member.email,
    displayName: member.displayName,
    roles: [...member.roles],
    joinedAt: member.joinedAt,
  };
}
```

      `update-member-roles.handler.ts`:

```ts
@CommandHandler(UpdateMemberRolesCommand)
export class UpdateMemberRolesHandler implements ICommandHandler<UpdateMemberRolesCommand> {
  constructor(
    private readonly memberships: MembershipsRepository,
    private readonly transactions: TransactionRunner,
    private readonly audit: TenantAuditLog,
  ) {}

  async execute({ tenantId, actorId, userId, roles }: UpdateMemberRolesCommand): Promise<Member> {
    const principal = requireTenantActor(tenantId, actorId);
    const next = parseTenantRoles(roles);
    return this.transactions.run(async () => {
      // Lock first: every owner change in this tenant is serialised from here to COMMIT.
      const owners = await this.memberships.countOwnersLocked(tenantId);
      // Read the actor's roles AFTER the lock: an owner demoted by a concurrent request that
      // committed while we waited must not act with the rank they just lost.
      const actor = await actorTenantRoles(principal, this.memberships);
      const target = await this.memberships.find(tenantId, userId);
      if (target === null) throw new EntityNotFoundException('Member', userId);
      assertCanAssignRoles(actor, target.roles, next);
      if (target.roles.includes('owner') && !next.includes('owner') && owners <= 1) {
        throw new LastOwnerException();
      }
      await this.memberships.updateRoles(tenantId, userId, next);
      await this.audit.record({
        tenantId,
        actorId,
        type: AUDIT_EVENT_TYPES.MEMBER_ROLES_CHANGED,
        data: { userId, from: [...target.roles], to: next },
      });
      const member = await this.memberships.findMember(tenantId, userId);
      if (member === null) throw new EntityNotFoundException('Member', userId);
      return toMemberContract(member);
    });
  }
}
```

      `remove-member.handler.ts` (same constructor): `requireTenantActor`; inside `transactions.run`, in this order:
      `countOwnersLocked`, `const actor = await actorTenantRoles(principal, this.memberships)` (after the lock, as
      above), `find` target (404 `Member`), `assertCanRemove(actor, target.roles)`,
      `if (target.roles.includes('owner') && owners <= 1) throw new LastOwnerException();`, `delete`, audit
      `MEMBER_REMOVED` with `{ userId, roles: [...target.roles] }`. `leave-tenant.handler.ts` (same constructor):
      `const principal = requireTenantActor(tenantId, userId); if (principal.staff) throw new
      TenantAccessDeniedException();` inside `transactions.run`: `countOwnersLocked`, `find(tenantId, userId)` (null →
      `TenantAccessDeniedException`), `if (self.roles.includes('owner') && owners <= 1) throw new LastOwnerException();`,
      `delete`, audit `MEMBER_LEFT` with `actorId: userId`, `data: { roles: [...self.roles] }`.
      `list-members.handler.ts` (constructor: `MembershipsRepository`): `const principal =
      requireTenantActor(tenantId, requirePrincipal().userId);` then `await actorTenantRoles(principal,
      this.memberships);` (any current member, or staff, may list; a member removed after their token was issued
      gets 403 `TENANT_ACCESS_DENIED`), `const size = limit <= 0 ? 20 : Math.min(limit, 100);` (REST and GraphQL
      pass 0 for "default", like the proto's int32 default), `memberships.listMembers(tenantId, { limit: size,

      page.nextCursor }) }`. `application/ports/members.port.ts`: the abstract class of

```ts
import { ApiProperty } from '@nestjs/swagger';
import { TENANT_ROLES, type TenantRole } from '@platform/common';
import { ArrayMaxSize, ArrayNotEmpty, ArrayUnique, IsArray, IsIn } from 'class-validator';

export class MemberRolesDto {
  @ApiProperty({ enum: TENANT_ROLES, enumName: 'TenantRole', isArray: true, example: ['admin'] })
  @IsArray()
  @ArrayNotEmpty()
  @ArrayUnique()
  @ArrayMaxSize(TENANT_ROLES.length)
  @IsIn(TENANT_ROLES, { each: true })
  roles: TenantRole[];
}
```

```ts
import { Type } from 'class-transformer';
import { IsInt, IsOptional, IsString, Max, MaxLength, Min } from 'class-validator';

  @Max(100)

  @ApiPropertyOptional({ description: 'The `nextCursor` of the previous page' })

  @IsString()

}
```

      nextCursor: string | null }` with `static from(page: MemberPage)`. `presentation/http/members.controller.ts`:

```ts
const userIdPipe = new ParseUUIDPipe({ version: '7' });

@ApiTags('tenant members')
@ApiBearerAuth()
@ApiUnauthorizedResponse({ type: ProblemResponse })
@Controller({ path: 'tenant', version: '1' })
export class MembersController {
  constructor(private readonly members: MembersPort) {}

  @Get('members')
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Members of the active tenant (keyset pages)' })
  @ApiOkResponse({ type: MemberPageResponse })
  async list(
    @Query() query: ListMembersQueryDto,
    @CurrentUser() user: AuthUser,
  ): Promise<MemberPageResponse> {
    return MemberPageResponse.from(
      await this.members.listMembers({
        tenantId: user.tenantId,
        limit: query.limit ?? 0,
        cursor: query.cursor,
      }),
    );
  }

  @Patch('members/:userId')
  @RequirePermissions(IdentityPermission.MembersManage)
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: "Replace a member's roles",
    description: 'Owner rank to grant or revoke owner; never the last owner.',
  })
  @ApiOkResponse({ type: MemberResponse })
  @ApiForbiddenResponse({
    type: ProblemResponse,
    description: '`FORBIDDEN`, `OWNER_RANK_REQUIRED`',
  })
  @ApiUnprocessableEntityResponse({
    type: ProblemResponse,
    description: '`LAST_OWNER`, `INVALID_ROLES`',
  })
  async updateRoles(
    @Param('userId', userIdPipe) userId: string,
    @Body() body: MemberRolesDto,
    @CurrentUser() user: AuthUser,
  ): Promise<MemberResponse> {
    return MemberResponse.from(
      await this.members.updateMemberRoles({
        tenantId: user.tenantId,
        actorId: user.id,
        userId,
        roles: body.roles,
      }),
    );
  }

  @Delete('members/:userId')
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.NO_CONTENT)
  @RequirePermissions(IdentityPermission.MembersManage)
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Remove a member from the active tenant' })
  @ApiNoContentResponse()
  async remove(
    @Param('userId', userIdPipe) userId: string,
    @CurrentUser() user: AuthUser,
  ): Promise<void> {
    await this.members.removeMember({ tenantId: user.tenantId, actorId: user.id, userId });
  }

  @Post('leave')
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.NO_CONTENT)
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Leave the active tenant (the last owner cannot)' })
  @ApiNoContentResponse()
  async leave(@CurrentUser() user: AuthUser): Promise<void> {
    await this.members.leaveTenant({ tenantId: user.tenantId, userId: user.id });
  }
}
```

```ts
// @domain/identity-contracts (root): the template is a contract between identity (enqueues) and the mail worker
export const IDENTITY_MAIL_TEMPLATES_DIR: string; // contracts/src|dist/mail/templates
export const IDENTITY_MAIL_TEMPLATES = { TENANT_INVITATION: 'tenant-invitation' } as const;
export interface TenantInvitationMailContext {
  tenantName: string; inviterName: string; roles: string; token: string; acceptUrl: string | null; expiresAt: string;
}
// @platform/config mail namespace (F34): MAIL_APP_URL (optional http(s) URL; https in production unless
//   SMTP_ALLOW_INSECURE) → MailConfig.appUrl: string | undefined (no trailing slash)
// @platform/mailer: defaultMailContext(from, now?, appUrl?) adds `appUrl` when set; MailService.sendNow passes it
// identity (root)
export abstract class InvitationMailer { abstract send(mail: { to: string; invitationId: string; context: TenantInvitationMailContext }): Promise<void> }
export class CreateInvitationCommand extends Command<Invitation> { constructor(readonly tenantId: string, readonly actorId: string, readonly email: string, readonly roles: readonly string[]) }
export class AcceptInvitationCommand extends Command<MyTenant> { constructor(readonly userId: string, readonly token: string) }
export function toInvitationContract(invitation: InvitationRecord): Invitation;
IdentityErrorCode.INVITATION_INVALID = 'INVITATION_INVALID';               // InvitationInvalidException extends EntityNotFoundException (404)
IdentityErrorCode.INVITATION_EMAIL_MISMATCH = 'INVITATION_EMAIL_MISMATCH'; // InvitationEmailMismatchException extends PermissionDeniedException (403)
// ./api
export abstract class InvitationsPort {
  abstract createInvitation(input: CreateInvitationRequest): Promise<Invitation>;
  abstract acceptInvitation(input: AcceptInvitationRequest): Promise<MyTenant>;
}
```

REST (`InvitationsController`): `POST /v1/tenant/invitations { email, roles }` → 201 `InvitationResponse` (`{ id,
  tenantId, email, roles, expiresAt }`; `@RequirePermissions(IdentityPermission.MembersManage)`; owner rank to invite
an owner); `POST /v1/invitations/accept { token }` → 200 `MyTenantResponse` (any logged-in user whose email equals
the invitation's). Not a credential endpoint (no account is created, no password set): no `@RateLimit`. The raw
token exists only in the response-less mail job (BullMQ in Redis, deleted as soon as it is sent or gives up:
`INVITATION_MAIL_RETENTION`) and the mail; never in Kafka, logs or the database (sha256 only). Delivery: identity **enqueues** on the shared `mail` queue; the processes that run
`MailProcessor` (monolith, notifications-service) register `IDENTITY_MAIL_TEMPLATES_DIR`; identity-service enqueues
only (`worker: false`).

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { mailConfig } from './mail.config.js';

const prod = {
  NODE_ENV: 'production',
  SMTP_HOST: 'smtp.acme.io',
  MAIL_FROM: 'Acme <no-reply@acme.io>',
};

describe('MAIL_APP_URL (F34)', () => {
  it('is optional and drops a trailing slash', () => {
    expect(mailConfig.parse({}).appUrl).toBeUndefined();
    expect(mailConfig.parse({ MAIL_APP_URL: 'http://localhost:3000/' }).appUrl).toBe(
      'http://localhost:3000',
    );
  });

  it('rejects other schemes', () => {
    expect(() => mailConfig.parse({ MAIL_APP_URL: 'javascript:alert(1)' })).toThrow();
  });

  it('must be https in production, unless SMTP_ALLOW_INSECURE (local compose)', () => {
    expect(() => mailConfig.parse({ ...prod, MAIL_APP_URL: 'http://app.acme.io' })).toThrow(
      /MAIL_APP_URL/,
    );
    expect(mailConfig.parse({ ...prod, MAIL_APP_URL: 'https://app.acme.io' }).appUrl).toBe(
      'https://app.acme.io',
    );
    expect(
      mailConfig.parse({
        ...prod,
        MAIL_APP_URL: 'http://localhost:3000',
        SMTP_ALLOW_INSECURE: 'true',
      }).appUrl,
    ).toBe('http://localhost:3000');
  });
});
```
