```ts
  const file = join(
    IDENTITY_MAIL_TEMPLATES_DIR,
    `${IDENTITY_MAIL_TEMPLATES.TENANT_INVITATION}.hbs`,
  );

  it('ships the invitation template next to the module', () => {
    expect(existsSync(file)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('uses only the variables of TenantInvitationMailContext plus the layout ones', () => {
    const used = new Set(
      [...readFileSync(file, 'utf8').matchAll(/\{\{[#/]?(?:if )?([a-zA-Z]+)/g)].map((m) => m[1]),
    );
    for (const name of used) {
      expect([
        'tenantName',
        'inviterName',
        'roles',
        'token',
        'acceptUrl',
        'expiresAt',
        'appName',
        'if',
      ]).toContain(name);
    }
  });
});
```

Create `application/commands/create-invitation/create-invitation.handler.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { createPrincipal, generateId, runWithPrincipal, type TenantRole } from '@platform/common';
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { hashInvitationToken } from '../../../domain/invitation-token.js';
import { createInMemoryIdentityPersistence } from '../../../testing/in-memory-identity.js';
import type { InvitationMailer } from '../../ports/invitation-mailer.port.js';
import { TenantAuditLog } from '../../services/tenant-audit-log.service.js';
import { CreateInvitationCommand } from './create-invitation.command.js';
import { CreateInvitationHandler } from './create-invitation.handler.js';

describe('CreateInvitationHandler', () => {
  let p: ReturnType<typeof createInMemoryIdentityPersistence>;
  let mailer: { send: ReturnType<typeof vi.fn> };
  let handler: CreateInvitationHandler;
  const tenantId = generateId();
  const [owner, admin] = [generateId(), generateId()];

  const invite = (actor: string, email: string, roles: string[]) =>
    runWithPrincipal(createPrincipal({ userId: actor, tenantId, roles: ['owner'] }), () =>
      handler.execute(new CreateInvitationCommand(tenantId, actor, email, roles)),
    );

  beforeEach(async () => {
    p = createInMemoryIdentityPersistence();
    await p.tenants.insert({
      id: tenantId,
      slug: `acme-${tenantId.slice(-8)}`,
      name: 'Acme',
      personal: false,
    });
    for (const [userId, roles] of [
      [owner, ['owner']],
      [admin, ['admin']],
    ] as [string, TenantRole[]][]) {
      await p.memberships.insert({ tenantId, userId, roles, lastActiveAt: null });
    }
    mailer = { send: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined) };
    handler = new CreateInvitationHandler(
      p.tenants,
      p.memberships,
      p.invitations,
      p.users,
      p.transactions,
      new TenantAuditLog(p.auditEvents),
      mailer as unknown as InvitationMailer,
      { appUrl: 'https://app.example.io' },
    );
  });

  it('stores only the token hash, mails the raw token with the accept link, audits invitation.created', async () => {
    const invitation = await invite(admin, '  New.Person@Example.com ', ['member']);

    expect(invitation).toMatchObject({
      tenantId,
      email: 'new.person@example.com',
      roles: ['member'],
    });
    const [mail] = mailer.send.mock.calls[0] as [
      { to: string; invitationId: string; context: { token: string; acceptUrl: string } },
    ];
    expect(mail.to).toBe('new.person@example.com');
    expect(mail.invitationId).toBe(invitation.id);
    expect(mail.context.acceptUrl).toBe(
      `https://app.example.io/invitations/accept?token=${encodeURIComponent(mail.context.token)}`,
    );
    const stored = [...p.store.invitations.values()][0];
    expect(stored?.tokenHash).toBe(hashInvitationToken(mail.context.token));
    expect(JSON.stringify(stored)).not.toContain(mail.context.token);
    expect(p.store.auditEvents.at(-1)).toMatchObject({
      type: 'invitation.created',
      data: { invitationId: invitation.id, email: 'new.person@example.com', roles: ['member'] },
    });
  });

  it('403 OWNER_RANK_REQUIRED for an admin inviting an owner; nothing stored or mailed', async () => {
    await expect(invite(admin, 'x@example.com', ['owner'])).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'OWNER_RANK_REQUIRED',
    });
    expect(p.store.invitations.size).toBe(0);
    expect(mailer.send).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('acceptUrl is null without MAIL_APP_URL (the mail still carries the token)', async () => {
    handler = new CreateInvitationHandler(
      p.tenants,
      p.memberships,
      p.invitations,
      p.users,
      p.transactions,
      new TenantAuditLog(p.auditEvents),
      mailer as unknown as InvitationMailer,
      { appUrl: undefined },
    );
    await invite(owner, 'y@example.com', ['admin']);
    expect(mailer.send.mock.calls[0]?.[0]).toMatchObject({ context: { acceptUrl: null } });
  });
});
```

Create `application/commands/accept-invitation/accept-invitation.handler.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { createPrincipal, generateId, runWithPrincipal } from '@platform/common';
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { generateInvitationToken, INVITATION_TTL_MS } from '../../../domain/invitation-token.js';
import { UserAggregate } from '../../../domain/user.aggregate.js';
import { createInMemoryIdentityPersistence } from '../../../testing/in-memory-identity.js';
import { TenantAuditLog } from '../../services/tenant-audit-log.service.js';
import { AcceptInvitationCommand } from './accept-invitation.command.js';
import { AcceptInvitationHandler } from './accept-invitation.handler.js';

describe('AcceptInvitationHandler', () => {
  let p: ReturnType<typeof createInMemoryIdentityPersistence>;
  let handler: AcceptInvitationHandler;
  const tenantId = generateId();
  const home = generateId();
  const bob = generateId();
  let token: string;

  const accept = (userId: string, raw: string, staff = false) =>
    runWithPrincipal(
      createPrincipal({ userId, tenantId: home, roles: staff ? ['admin'] : ['owner'], staff }),
      () => handler.execute(new AcceptInvitationCommand(userId, raw)),
    );

  const invite = async (email: string, expiresAt = new Date(Date.now() + INVITATION_TTL_MS)) => {
    const generated = generateInvitationToken();
    await p.invitations.insert({
      tenantId,
      id: generateId(),
      email,
      roles: ['admin'],
      tokenHash: generated.tokenHash,
      invitedBy: generateId(),
      expiresAt,
    });
    return generated.token;
  };

  beforeEach(async () => {
    p = createInMemoryIdentityPersistence();
    await p.tenants.insert({
      id: tenantId,
      slug: `acme-${tenantId.slice(-8)}`,
      name: 'Acme',
      personal: false,
    });
    await p.users.insert(
      UserAggregate.register({
        id: bob,
        email: 'bob@example.com',
        displayName: 'Bob',
        passwordHash: 'x',
        now: new Date(),
      }),
    );
    handler = new AcceptInvitationHandler(
      p.invitations,
      p.memberships,
      p.users,
      p.transactions,
      new TenantAuditLog(p.auditEvents),
    );
    token = await invite('bob@example.com');
  });

  it('joins the tenant with the invited roles, once; audited invitation.accepted', async () => {
    await expect(accept(bob, token)).resolves.toMatchObject({
      tenant: { id: tenantId },
      roles: ['admin'],
    });
    await expect(p.memberships.find(tenantId, bob)).resolves.toMatchObject({ roles: ['admin'] });
    expect(p.store.auditEvents.at(-1)).toMatchObject({
      tenantId,
      actorId: bob,
      type: 'invitation.accepted',
    });
    await expect(accept(bob, token)).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'INVITATION_INVALID' });
  });

  it('403 INVITATION_EMAIL_MISMATCH for another account; the invitation stays usable', async () => {
    const eve = generateId();
    await p.users.insert(
      UserAggregate.register({
        id: eve,
        email: 'eve@example.com',
        displayName: 'Eve',
        passwordHash: 'x',
        now: new Date(),
      }),
    );
    await expect(accept(eve, token)).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'INVITATION_EMAIL_MISMATCH' });
    await expect(accept(bob, token)).resolves.toBeDefined();
  });

  it('404 INVITATION_INVALID for an expired, unknown or malformed token (indistinguishable)', async () => {
    const expired = await invite('bob@example.com', new Date(Date.now() - 1_000));
    for (const raw of [expired, generateInvitationToken().token, 'not-a-token']) {
      await expect(accept(bob, raw)).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'INVITATION_INVALID' });
    }
  });

  it('409 MEMBERSHIP_EXISTS for an existing member; the invitation is not consumed', async () => {
    await p.memberships.insert({ tenantId, userId: bob, roles: ['member'], lastActiveAt: null });
    await expect(accept(bob, token)).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'MEMBERSHIP_EXISTS' });
    expect([...p.store.invitations.values()].every((i) => i.acceptedAt === null)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('a staff switch-in token cannot accept invitations', async () => {
    await expect(accept(bob, token, true)).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'TENANT_ACCESS_DENIED' });
  });
});
```

(The users are created through `UserAggregate.register`; see the note in Task 8.) The in-memory
`InlineTransactionRunner` does NOT restore the store on rollback (it just runs `work()`); the `MEMBERSHIP_EXISTS`
case holds because the handler checks the membership before `markAccepted`, so nothing is written. Create
`infrastructure/mail/queued-invitation.mailer.spec.ts`: `QueuedInvitationMailer.send(...)` calls
`MailService.enqueue` with the message `{ to, subject: 'Ada invited you to Acme', template: 'tenant-invitation',
context, idempotencyKey }` (where `idempotencyKey` is `'tenant-invitation-' + invitationId`) as the first argument
and exactly `INVITATION_MAIL_RETENTION` (`{ removeOnComplete: true, removeOnFail: true }`) as the second: the job data carries
the raw token, so the job is deleted as soon as it completes or exhausts its retries (controller ruling).

Append to `tenancy.repositories.int-spec.ts` an "invitations accept" block: insert an invitation, `markAccepted`
twice concurrently (`Promise.all`) → exactly one `true`; `findByTokenHash` finds it across tenants (system read).

Create `apps/monolith/test/invitations.e2e-spec.ts` (env and register helpers as in `tenants.e2e-spec.ts`;
`e2e.mailQueue.jobs` is the recording queue of the monolith e2e app, typed `{ name: string; data: unknown; opts:
unknown }[]`, so cast `data` to `{ to?: string; context?: { token?: unknown } }` before reading it): Ada invites
`bob.inv@example.com` as `member` → 201 and one job named `tenant-invitation` on `e2e.mailQueue` whose
`data.context.token` is the raw token and whose `opts` match `{ removeOnComplete: true, removeOnFail: true }`; Bob
registers with that email and `POST /v1/invitations/accept { token }` → 200 `{ id: ada.tenantId, roles: ['member']
}`; replay → 404 `INVITATION_INVALID`; Carol (another email) accepting a second invitation for Bob → 403
`INVITATION_EMAIL_MISMATCH`; `{ token: '' }` → 400.

      and in `transform` `    /** Public web app URL without a trailing slash (mail links), or undefined. */\n    appUrl:
      env.MAIL_APP_URL?.replace(/\/+$/, ''),`. `mail.util.ts` `defaultMailContext(from, now = new Date(), appUrl?:
      string)`: return type `Required<Pick<BaseMailContext, 'appName' | 'year'>> & Pick<BaseMailContext, 'appUrl'>`
      and body `return { appName: …, year: …, ...(appUrl === undefined ? {} : { appUrl }) };`. `mail.service.ts`
      `sendNow`: `defaultMailContext(this.cfg.from, new Date(), this.cfg.appUrl)`. Job retention for secrets
      (controller ruling: a raw invitation token must not linger in Redis): `mail.types.ts` adds

```ts
/**
 * Overrides of the retention in `MAIL_JOB_OPTIONS`, for mails whose job data is a secret (one-time
 * codes, invitation tokens): `{ removeOnComplete: true, removeOnFail: true }` deletes the job as soon
 * as it is sent or gives up. The idempotency window then ends with the job.
 */
export type MailJobRetention = Pick<JobsOptions, 'removeOnComplete' | 'removeOnFail'>;
```

      (`import type { JobsOptions } from 'bullmq';`), `MailService.enqueue(mail: MailMessage, retention?:
      MailJobRetention)` spreads `...retention` after `...MAIL_JOB_OPTIONS` and before the `jobId`, its doc gains
      "`retention` shortens how long the job (and its data) stays in Redis", and `libs/mailer/src/index.ts` exports
      `type MailJobRetention`. A boolean `removeOnFail: true` rather than an age: BullMQ prunes failed jobs by age only
      when a later job fails, so an age could leave the token in Redis indefinitely; the failure itself is still
      logged by `MailProcessor` (masked recipient, no context). `libs/mailer/README.md`: document
      `MAIL_APP_URL` → `appUrl` (welcome, receipt and digest buttons now render). Root `.env.example` mail section:
      after `MAIL_FROM` add `# Public web app URL for mail links (invitation accept page); https in production.` and
      `MAIL_APP_URL=http://localhost:3000`; `docker-compose.yml` `x-env-base`: `MAIL_APP_URL:
      ${MAIL_APP_URL:-http://localhost:3000}`. Run `node scripts/check-env-example.mjs` → exit 0.

```ts
import { join } from 'node:path';

/**
 * Identity's mail templates. Identity enqueues on the shared BullMQ `mail` queue; every process that runs
 * `MailProcessor` (monolith, notifications-service) registers this folder:
 * `AppMailerModule.forRootAsync({ templateDirs: [..., IDENTITY_MAIL_TEMPLATES_DIR] })`. SWC `copyFiles` ships
 * the `.hbs` files to `dist`, so `import.meta.dirname` works from `src` and `dist`.
 */
export const IDENTITY_MAIL_TEMPLATES_DIR = join(import.meta.dirname, 'templates');

export const IDENTITY_MAIL_TEMPLATES = { TENANT_INVITATION: 'tenant-invitation' } as const;

/** Context of `tenant-invitation.hbs` (the layout adds `appName`, `year`, `appUrl`). */
export interface TenantInvitationMailContext {
  tenantName: string;
  inviterName: string;
  /** Human list, e.g. "admin". */
  roles: string;
  /** The raw invitation token (also embedded in `acceptUrl`). */
  token: string;
  /** `${MAIL_APP_URL}/invitations/accept?token=…`, or null when MAIL_APP_URL is not set. */
  acceptUrl: string | null;
  /** ISO-8601 expiry. */
  expiresAt: string;
}
```

```ts
/** 404 — unknown, expired or already accepted: one answer, so tokens cannot be probed. */
export class InvitationInvalidException extends EntityNotFoundException {
  constructor() {
    super('Invitation', 'token', { code: IdentityErrorCode.INVITATION_INVALID });
  }
}

/** 403 — the invitation was sent to another email address. */
export class InvitationEmailMismatchException extends PermissionDeniedException {
  constructor() {
    super('This invitation was sent to another email address', {
      code: IdentityErrorCode.INVITATION_EMAIL_MISMATCH,
    });
  }
}
```

```ts
import type { TenantInvitationMailContext } from '@domain/identity-contracts';

/** Sends the invitation mail (queued: delivery is retried by the mail worker). */
export abstract class InvitationMailer {
  abstract send(mail: {
    to: string;
    invitationId: string;
    context: TenantInvitationMailContext;

```

      `infrastructure/mail/queued-invitation.mailer.ts`:

```ts
import {
  IDENTITY_MAIL_TEMPLATES,
  type TenantInvitationMailContext,
} from '@domain/identity-contracts';
import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { MailService, type MailJobRetention } from '@platform/mailer';
import type { InvitationMailer } from '../../application/ports/invitation-mailer.port.js';

/**
 * The job data holds the raw invitation token: delete the job once it is sent or has given up
 * (MAIL_JOB_OPTIONS would keep it 24 h after success and 7 days after failure).
 */
export const INVITATION_MAIL_RETENTION: MailJobRetention = {
  removeOnComplete: true,
  removeOnFail: true,
};

@Injectable()
export class QueuedInvitationMailer implements InvitationMailer {
  constructor(private readonly mail: MailService) {}

  async send(mail: {
    to: string;
    invitationId: string;
    context: TenantInvitationMailContext;
  }): Promise<void> {
    await this.mail.enqueue(
      {
        to: mail.to,
        subject: `${mail.context.inviterName} invited you to ${mail.context.tenantName}`,
        template: IDENTITY_MAIL_TEMPLATES.TENANT_INVITATION,
        context: { ...mail.context },
        // One job per invitation id, so a duplicate enqueue in the same request is a no-op.
        idempotencyKey: `tenant-invitation-${mail.invitationId}`,
      },
      INVITATION_MAIL_RETENTION,
    );
  }
}
```

      `application/mappers/invitation.mapper.ts`: `toInvitationContract(i)` → `{ id, tenantId, email, roles: [...i.roles],
      expiresAt, createdAt }` (never the hash). `create-invitation.handler.ts`:

```ts
@CommandHandler(CreateInvitationCommand)
export class CreateInvitationHandler implements ICommandHandler<CreateInvitationCommand> {
  constructor(
    private readonly tenants: TenantsRepository,
    private readonly memberships: MembershipsRepository,
    private readonly invitations: InvitationsRepository,
    private readonly users: UsersRepository,
    private readonly transactions: TransactionRunner,
    private readonly audit: TenantAuditLog,
    private readonly mailer: InvitationMailer,
    @Inject(mailConfig.KEY) private readonly mail: Pick<MailConfig, 'appUrl'>,
  ) {}

  async execute({ tenantId, actorId, email, roles }: CreateInvitationCommand): Promise<Invitation> {
    const principal = requireTenantActor(tenantId, actorId);
    const invitedRoles = parseTenantRoles(roles);
    assertCanAssignRoles(await actorTenantRoles(principal, this.memberships), null, invitedRoles);
    const to = normalizeEmail(email);
    const { token, tokenHash } = generateInvitationToken();
    const now = new Date();
    const record: NewInvitation = {
      tenantId,
      id: generateId(),
      email: to,
      roles: invitedRoles,
      tokenHash,
      invitedBy: actorId,
      expiresAt: new Date(now.getTime() + INVITATION_TTL_MS),
    };
    const tenant = await this.transactions.run(async () => {
      const found = await this.tenants.findById(tenantId);
      if (found === null) throw new EntityNotFoundException('Tenant', tenantId);
      await this.invitations.insert(record);
      await this.audit.record({
        tenantId,
        actorId,
        type: AUDIT_EVENT_TYPES.INVITATION_CREATED,
        data: { invitationId: record.id, email: to, roles: invitedRoles },
        now,
      });
      return found;
    });
    const inviter = await this.users.findById(actorId);
    // After COMMIT: a mail never announces an invitation that rolled back.
    await this.mailer.send({
      to,
      invitationId: record.id,
      context: {
        tenantName: tenant.name,
        inviterName: inviter?.displayName ?? 'A workspace admin',
        roles: invitedRoles.join(', '),
        token,
        acceptUrl:
          this.mail.appUrl === undefined
            ? null
            : `${this.mail.appUrl}/invitations/accept?token=${encodeURIComponent(token)}`,
        expiresAt: record.expiresAt.toISOString(),
      },
    });
    return toInvitationContract({ ...record, acceptedAt: null, createdAt: now });
  }
}
```

      `accept-invitation.handler.ts`:

```ts
const TOKEN_SHAPE = /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43}$/; // base64url of 32 bytes (INVITATION_TOKEN_BYTES)

@CommandHandler(AcceptInvitationCommand)
export class AcceptInvitationHandler implements ICommandHandler<AcceptInvitationCommand> {
  constructor(
    private readonly invitations: InvitationsRepository,
    private readonly memberships: MembershipsRepository,
    private readonly users: UsersRepository,
    private readonly transactions: TransactionRunner,
    private readonly audit: TenantAuditLog,
  ) {}

  async execute({ userId, token }: AcceptInvitationCommand): Promise<MyTenant> {
    const principal = requirePrincipal();
    if (principal.userId !== userId || principal.staff) throw new TenantAccessDeniedException();
    if (!TOKEN_SHAPE.test(token)) throw new InvitationInvalidException();
    // System read 3 (spec §9): multi-shard on Citus; B3-11 moves it to the system repository.
    const invitation = await this.invitations.findByTokenHash(hashInvitationToken(token));
    const now = new Date();
    if (invitation === null || invitation.acceptedAt !== null || invitation.expiresAt <= now) {
      throw new InvitationInvalidException();
    }
    const user = await this.users.findById(userId);
    if (user === null || normalizeEmail(user.email) !== invitation.email) {
      throw new InvitationEmailMismatchException();
    }
    // Spec §9: act as the accepting user in the invitation's tenant (elevation justified by the token).
    await runWithPrincipal(createPrincipal({ userId, tenantId: invitation.tenantId }), () =>
      this.transactions.run(async () => {
        if ((await this.memberships.find(invitation.tenantId, userId)) !== null) {
          throw new MembershipAlreadyExistsException();
        }
        if (!(await this.invitations.markAccepted(invitation.tenantId, invitation.id, now))) {
          throw new InvitationInvalidException(); // a concurrent accept won
        }
        await this.memberships.insert({
          tenantId: invitation.tenantId,
          userId,
          roles: invitation.roles,
          lastActiveAt: null,
        });
        await this.audit.record({
          tenantId: invitation.tenantId,
          actorId: userId,
          type: AUDIT_EVENT_TYPES.INVITATION_ACCEPTED,
          data: { invitationId: invitation.id, roles: [...invitation.roles] },
          now,
        });
      }),
    );
    const joined = (await this.memberships.listForUser(userId)).find(
      (m) => m.tenantId === invitation.tenantId,
    );
    if (joined === undefined) throw new InvitationInvalidException();
    return toMyTenantContract(joined);
  }
}
```

      (Imports per file: `@platform/common` for `createPrincipal`, `EntityNotFoundException`, `generateId`,
      `requirePrincipal`, `runWithPrincipal`; `@platform/config` for `mailConfig`, `type MailConfig`; the identity
      files named in Interfaces.) Port `application/ports/invitations.port.ts` (Interfaces block),
      `invitations-local.adapter.ts` (bus calls: `new CreateInvitationCommand(input.tenantId, input.actorId,
      input.email, input.roles)`, `new AcceptInvitationCommand(input.userId, input.token)`).

```ts
// presentation/http/dto/create-invitation.dto.ts
export class CreateInvitationDto {
  @ApiProperty({ example: 'new.person@example.com', maxLength: IDENTITY_LIMITS.EMAIL_MAX_LENGTH })
  @NormalizeEmail()
  @IsEmail()
  @MaxLength(IDENTITY_LIMITS.EMAIL_MAX_LENGTH)
  email: string;

  @ApiProperty({ enum: TENANT_ROLES, enumName: 'TenantRole', isArray: true, example: ['member'] })
  @IsArray()
  @ArrayNotEmpty()
  @ArrayUnique()
  @ArrayMaxSize(TENANT_ROLES.length)
  @IsIn(TENANT_ROLES, { each: true })
  roles: TenantRole[];
}
// presentation/http/dto/accept-invitation.dto.ts
export class AcceptInvitationDto {
  @ApiProperty({ description: 'The code from the invitation mail', minLength: 43, maxLength: 43 })
  @TrimString()
  @IsString()
  @Length(43, 43)
  token: string;
}
```

  `tenancy.mapper.ts`, `models/{tenant,my-tenant,active-tenant,member,member-connection,invitation}.model.ts`,
  `inputs/{tenant-name,invite-member,accept-invitation,update-member-roles}.input.ts`, `args/members.args.ts`;
  `apps/monolith/test/tenancy-graphql.e2e-spec.ts`
- Test (create): `presentation/graphql/tenancy.mapper.spec.ts`
- Modify: `domains/identity/src/identity-api.module.ts` (`forLocal()` providers)

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: `TenantsPort`, `MembersPort`, `InvitationsPort` (Tasks 7-9); `TenantRoleEnum`, `UserModel`, the `me`
  query, `switchTenant` (core Tasks 8, 14); `AuthUser.tenantId/roles/staff` (core Task 10).
- Produces (GraphQL schema; code-first, no committed schema file):

```graphql
type Tenant {
  id: ID!
  slug: String!
  name: String!
  personal: Boolean!
}
type MyTenant {
  tenant: Tenant!
  roles: [TenantRole!]!
  lastActiveAt: DateTime
}
type ActiveTenant {
  tenantId: ID!
  tenant: Tenant
  roles: [TenantRole!]!
  staff: Boolean!
} # tenant null for staff switch-in
type Member {
  userId: ID!
  email: String!
  displayName: String!
  roles: [TenantRole!]!
  joinedAt: DateTime!
}
type MemberConnection {
  items: [Member!]!
  nextCursor: String
}
type Invitation {
  id: ID!
  tenantId: ID!
  email: String!
  roles: [TenantRole!]!
  expiresAt: DateTime!
}
extend type User {
  activeTenant: ActiveTenant
} # only on yourself (me); null for any other user
extend type Query {
  myTenants: [MyTenant!]!
  tenantMembers(limit: Int, cursor: String): MemberConnection!
}
extend type Mutation {
  createTenant(input: TenantNameInput!): MyTenant!
  renameTenant(input: TenantNameInput!): Tenant! # tenant:manage
  inviteMember(input: InviteMemberInput!): Invitation! # members:manage
  acceptInvitation(input: AcceptInvitationInput!): MyTenant!
  updateMemberRoles(input: UpdateMemberRolesInput!): Member! # members:manage
  removeMember(userId: UUID!): Boolean! # members:manage
  leaveTenant: Boolean!
}
```

`me` keeps returning `User` (no breaking change); `me { activeTenant { … } }` is the "active tenant and roles" of
spec §5.2. `switchTenant` stays core's (Task 14).

**Wave:** Lane I, after Task 9.

```ts
import { generateId } from '@platform/common';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { toActiveTenantModel, toMemberModel, toMyTenantModel } from './tenancy.mapper.js';

describe('tenancy GraphQL mapper', () => {
  const tenant = {
    id: generateId(),
    slug: 'acme-1234abcd',
    name: 'Acme',
    personal: false,
    createdAt: new Date(0),
  };

  it('maps MyTenant with canonical roles and a null lastActiveAt', () => {
    expect(toMyTenantModel({ tenant, roles: ['member', 'owner', 'root'] })).toEqual({
      tenant: { id: tenant.id, slug: tenant.slug, name: 'Acme', personal: false },
      roles: ['owner', 'member'],
      lastActiveAt: null,
    });
  });

  it('active tenant: the matching membership (its roles win over the token), or no tenant for staff', () => {
    const user = { tenantId: tenant.id, roles: ['admin' as const], staff: false };
    expect(toActiveTenantModel(user, [{ tenant, roles: ['member'] }])).toMatchObject({
      tenantId: tenant.id,
      tenant: { name: 'Acme' },
      roles: ['member'],
      staff: false,
    });
    expect(toActiveTenantModel({ ...user, staff: true }, [])).toEqual({
      tenantId: tenant.id,
      tenant: null,
      roles: ['admin'],
      staff: true,
    });
  });

  it('maps a member', () => {
    const joinedAt = new Date(1_000);
    expect(
      toMemberModel({ userId: 'u', email: 'a@b.io', displayName: 'A', roles: ['admin'], joinedAt }),
    ).toEqual({
      userId: 'u',
      email: 'a@b.io',
      displayName: 'A',
      roles: ['admin'],
      joinedAt,
    });
  });
});
```

Create `apps/monolith/test/tenancy-graphql.e2e-spec.ts` (monolith env pattern; helper
`gql(query, variables, token)` posting to `/graphql` and returning `{ data, errors }`): Ada registers;
`{ me { activeTenant { tenantId roles staff tenant { personal } } } }` → `{ tenantId: ada.tenantId, roles:
  ['OWNER'], staff: false, tenant: { personal: true } }`; `mutation { createTenant(input: { name: "Acme" }) { tenant {
  id name } roles } }` → `roles: ['OWNER']`; `{ myTenants { tenant { name } } }` → 2 items; `inviteMember(input: {
  email: "bob.gql@example.com", roles: [MEMBER] })` → an `Invitation` and a `tenant-invitation` job on `e2e.mailQueue`;
Bob registers and runs `acceptInvitation(input: { token })` → `{ tenant: { id: ada.tenantId }, roles: ['MEMBER'] }`;
Ada: `tenantMembers(limit: 10) { items { email roles } }` → both; `updateMemberRoles(input: { userId: bob, roles:
  [ADMIN] })` → `roles: ['ADMIN']`; `updateMemberRoles(input: { userId: ada, roles: [MEMBER] })` → `errors[0].extensions
  .code === 'LAST_OWNER'`; Bob (logged in to Ada's tenant via core's `switchTenant` or `login(tenantId)`):
`removeMember(userId: ada)` → `OWNER_RANK_REQUIRED`; `leaveTenant` → `true`; another user's `user(id: ada) {
  activeTenant { tenantId } }` with `users:read` → `activeTenant: null`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.** Models (each `@ObjectType('<Name>', { description })`; roles fields

```ts
// models/tenant.model.ts
@ObjectType('Tenant', { description: 'A workspace' })
export class TenantModel {
  @Field(() => ID) id: string;
  @Field() slug: string;
  @Field() name: string;
  @Field({ description: 'Created at registration for its first owner' }) personal: boolean;
}
// models/my-tenant.model.ts
@ObjectType('MyTenant', { description: 'One of your tenants and your roles in it' })
export class MyTenantModel {
  @Field(() => TenantModel) tenant: TenantModel;
  @Field(() => [TenantRoleEnum]) roles: TenantRole[];
  @Field(() => GraphQLISODateTime, { nullable: true }) lastActiveAt: Date | null;
}
// models/active-tenant.model.ts
@ObjectType('ActiveTenant', { description: 'The tenant your access token acts in' })
export class ActiveTenantModel {
  @Field(() => ID) tenantId: string;
  @Field(() => TenantModel, {
    nullable: true,
    description: 'Null for a staff switch-in (no membership)',
  })
  tenant: TenantModel | null;
  @Field(() => [TenantRoleEnum]) roles: TenantRole[];
  @Field({ description: 'True for a staff switch-in token' }) staff: boolean;
}
// models/member.model.ts
@ObjectType('Member', { description: 'A member of the active tenant' })
export class MemberModel {
  @Field(() => ID) userId: string;
  @Field() email: string;
  @Field() displayName: string;
  @Field(() => [TenantRoleEnum]) roles: TenantRole[];
  @Field(() => GraphQLISODateTime) joinedAt: Date;
}
// models/member-connection.model.ts
@ObjectType('MemberConnection', { description: 'A keyset page of members' })
export class MemberConnectionModel {
  @Field(() => [MemberModel]) items: MemberModel[];
  @Field(() => String, { nullable: true }) nextCursor: string | null;
}
// models/invitation.model.ts
@ObjectType('Invitation', { description: 'A pending invitation (the token only travels by mail)' })
export class InvitationModel {
  @Field(() => ID) id: string;
  @Field(() => ID) tenantId: string;
  @Field() email: string;
  @Field(() => [TenantRoleEnum]) roles: TenantRole[];
  @Field(() => GraphQLISODateTime) expiresAt: Date;
}
```

      Inputs (class-validator like `RegisterInput`): `TenantNameInput { name }` (`@TrimString() @IsString() @Length(1,
      IDENTITY_LIMITS.TENANT_NAME_MAX_LENGTH)`), `InviteMemberInput { email; roles }` (the decorators of
      `CreateInvitationDto` plus `@Field()` / `@Field(() => [TenantRoleEnum])`), `AcceptInvitationInput { token }`
      (`@TrimString() @IsString() @Length(43, 43)`), `UpdateMemberRolesInput { userId (@Field(() => GraphQLUUID)
      @IsUUID('7')); roles }`; `args/members.args.ts` `MembersArgs { limit?: Int (1..100); cursor?: String (≤
      MAX_CURSOR_LENGTH) }`. `tenancy.mapper.ts`:

```ts
import type { Invitation, Member, MyTenant, Tenant } from '@domain/identity-contracts';
import type { AuthUser } from '@platform/auth';
import { normalizeTenantRoles } from '@platform/common';
import type { ActiveTenantModel } from './models/active-tenant.model.js';
import type { InvitationModel } from './models/invitation.model.js';
import type { MemberModel } from './models/member.model.js';
import type { MyTenantModel } from './models/my-tenant.model.js';
import type { TenantModel } from './models/tenant.model.js';

export const toTenantModel = (t: Tenant): TenantModel => ({
  id: t.id,
  slug: t.slug,
  name: t.name,
  personal: t.personal,
});

export function toMyTenantModel(mine: MyTenant): MyTenantModel {
  if (mine.tenant === undefined) throw new Error('MyTenant without its tenant');
  return {
    tenant: toTenantModel(mine.tenant),
    roles: normalizeTenantRoles(mine.roles),
    lastActiveAt: mine.lastActiveAt ?? null,
  };
}

export function toActiveTenantModel(
  user: Pick<AuthUser, 'tenantId' | 'roles' | 'staff'>,
  mine: readonly MyTenant[],
): ActiveTenantModel {
  const membership = user.staff ? undefined : mine.find((m) => m.tenant?.id === user.tenantId);
  return {
    tenantId: user.tenantId,
    tenant: membership?.tenant === undefined ? null : toTenantModel(membership.tenant),
    // The membership row is authoritative (a demoted user's token keeps its roles until it expires);
    // a staff switch-in has no membership and acts with the token's roles.
    roles: normalizeTenantRoles(membership?.roles ?? user.roles),
    staff: user.staff,
  };
}

export const toMemberModel = (m: Member): MemberModel => ({
  userId: m.userId,
  email: m.email,
  displayName: m.displayName,
  roles: normalizeTenantRoles(m.roles),
  joinedAt: m.joinedAt ?? new Date(0),
});

export const toInvitationModel = (i: Invitation): InvitationModel => ({
  id: i.id,
  tenantId: i.tenantId,
  email: i.email,
  roles: normalizeTenantRoles(i.roles),
  expiresAt: i.expiresAt ?? new Date(0),
});
```

      `me-tenant.resolver.ts`:

```ts
@Resolver(() => UserModel)
export class MeTenantResolver {
  constructor(private readonly tenants: TenantsPort) {}

  @ResolveField(() => ActiveTenantModel, {
    nullable: true,
    description: 'Only on yourself (`me`): the tenant this access token acts in, with your roles',
  })
  async activeTenant(
    @Parent() user: UserModel,
    @CurrentUser() actor: AuthUser,
  ): Promise<ActiveTenantModel | null> {
    if (user.id !== actor.id) return null;
    const mine = actor.staff ? [] : (await this.tenants.listMyTenants({ userId: actor.id })).items;
    return toActiveTenantModel(actor, mine);
  }
}
```

      `tenancy.resolver.ts`:

```ts
@Resolver()
export class TenancyResolver {
  constructor(
    private readonly tenants: TenantsPort,
    private readonly members: MembersPort,
    private readonly invitations: InvitationsPort,
  ) {}

  @Query(() => [MyTenantModel], { description: 'Your tenants, most recently active first' })
  async myTenants(@CurrentUser('id') userId: string): Promise<MyTenantModel[]> {
    return (await this.tenants.listMyTenants({ userId })).items.map(toMyTenantModel);
  }

  @Query(() => MemberConnectionModel, {
    description: 'Members of the active tenant',
    complexity: 10,
  })
  async tenantMembers(
    @Args() args: MembersArgs,
    @CurrentUser() user: AuthUser,
  ): Promise<MemberConnectionModel> {
    const page = await this.members.listMembers({
      tenantId: user.tenantId,
      limit: args.limit ?? 0,
      cursor: args.cursor,
    });
    return { items: page.items.map(toMemberModel), nextCursor: page.nextCursor ?? null };
  }

  @Mutation(() => MyTenantModel, { description: 'Create a tenant with you as its owner' })
  async createTenant(
    @Args('input') input: TenantNameInput,
    @CurrentUser('id') userId: string,
  ): Promise<MyTenantModel> {
    return toMyTenantModel(await this.tenants.createTenant({ userId, name: input.name }));
  }

  @Mutation(() => TenantModel, { description: 'Rename the active tenant (tenant:manage)' })
  @RequirePermissions(IdentityPermission.TenantManage)
  async renameTenant(
    @Args('input') input: TenantNameInput,
    @CurrentUser() user: AuthUser,
  ): Promise<TenantModel> {
    return toTenantModel(
      await this.tenants.renameTenant({
        tenantId: user.tenantId,
        actorId: user.id,
        name: input.name,
      }),
    );
  }

  @Mutation(() => InvitationModel, {
    description: 'Invite an email to the active tenant (members:manage)',
  })
  @RequirePermissions(IdentityPermission.MembersManage)
  async inviteMember(
    @Args('input') input: InviteMemberInput,
    @CurrentUser() user: AuthUser,
  ): Promise<InvitationModel> {
    return toInvitationModel(
      await this.invitations.createInvitation({
        tenantId: user.tenantId,
        actorId: user.id,
        email: input.email,
        roles: input.roles,
      }),
    );
  }

  @Mutation(() => MyTenantModel, {
    description: 'Join a tenant with the code from an invitation mail',
  })
  async acceptInvitation(
    @Args('input') input: AcceptInvitationInput,
    @CurrentUser('id') userId: string,
  ): Promise<MyTenantModel> {
    return toMyTenantModel(await this.invitations.acceptInvitation({ userId, token: input.token }));
  }

  @Mutation(() => MemberModel, { description: "Replace a member's roles (members:manage)" })
  @RequirePermissions(IdentityPermission.MembersManage)
  async updateMemberRoles(
    @Args('input') input: UpdateMemberRolesInput,
    @CurrentUser() user: AuthUser,
  ): Promise<MemberModel> {
    return toMemberModel(
      await this.members.updateMemberRoles({
        tenantId: user.tenantId,
        actorId: user.id,
        userId: input.userId,
        roles: input.roles,
      }),
    );
  }

  @Mutation(() => Boolean, { description: 'Remove a member (members:manage)' })
  @RequirePermissions(IdentityPermission.MembersManage)
  async removeMember(
    @Args('userId', { type: () => GraphQLUUID }) userId: string,
    @CurrentUser() user: AuthUser,
  ): Promise<boolean> {
    await this.members.removeMember({ tenantId: user.tenantId, actorId: user.id, userId });
    return true;
  }

  @Mutation(() => Boolean, { description: 'Leave the active tenant (the last owner cannot)' })
  async leaveTenant(@CurrentUser() user: AuthUser): Promise<boolean> {
    await this.members.leaveTenant({ tenantId: user.tenantId, userId: user.id });
    return true;
  }
}
```

      (imports: `@nestjs/graphql` `Args`, `Mutation`, `Parent`, `Query`, `ResolveField`, `Resolver`; `@platform/auth`
      `type AuthUser`, `CurrentUser`, `RequirePermissions`; `@platform/graphql` `GraphQLUUID`; the ports, models, inputs,
      mapper and `IdentityPermission`.) `identity-api.module.ts` `forLocal()`: `TENANCY_PROVIDERS = [TenancyResolver,
      MeTenantResolver]` spread into `providers`. `src/api/index.ts` is unchanged: G-11's identity `./api` exports
      neither `UsersResolver` nor `AuthResolver` (verified on lane/g11), only the module, ports, config, limits and
      the models apps compose with; the resolvers are reached through `IdentityApiModule`.

```ts
import { generateId, PLATFORM_TENANT_ID } from '@platform/common';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  acceptInvitationRequestSchema,
  createInvitationRequestSchema,
  listMembersRequestSchema,
  updateMemberRolesRequestSchema,
} from './tenancy-rpc.payloads.js';

describe('tenancy RPC payloads', () => {
  const tenantId = generateId();
  const actorId = generateId();

  it('normalises proto-loader nulls and validates roles', () => {
    expect(listMembersRequestSchema.parse({ tenantId, limit: 0, cursor: null })).toEqual({
      tenantId,
      limit: 0,
    });
    expect(
      updateMemberRolesRequestSchema.safeParse({
        tenantId,
        actorId,
        userId: generateId(),
        roles: ['root'],
      }).success,
    ).toBe(false);
  });

  it('refuses the platform tenant and malformed ids', () => {
    expect(
      listMembersRequestSchema.safeParse({ tenantId: PLATFORM_TENANT_ID, limit: 1 }).success,
    ).toBe(false);
    expect(
      createInvitationRequestSchema.safeParse({
        tenantId,
        actorId: 'x',
        email: 'a@b.io',
        roles: ['member'],
      }).success,
    ).toBe(false);
  });

  it('normalises the invited email and checks the token shape', () => {
    expect(
      createInvitationRequestSchema.parse({
        tenantId,
        actorId,
        email: ' A@B.io ',
        roles: ['member'],
      }).email,
    ).toBe('a@b.io');
    expect(
      acceptInvitationRequestSchema.safeParse({ userId: actorId, token: 'short' }).success,
    ).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

Create `infrastructure/adapters/grpc/tenancy-grpc.adapters.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { IDENTITY_GRPC_PACKAGE, TENANTS_SERVICE_NAME } from '@domain/identity-contracts';
import { Metadata } from '@grpc/grpc-js';
import type { ClientGrpc } from '@nestjs/microservices';
import { generateId } from '@platform/common';
import { createMock } from '@platform/testing';
import { lastValueFrom, type Observable, of } from 'rxjs';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import type { IdentityGrpcCaller } from './identity-grpc.caller.js';
import { MembersGrpcAdapter } from './members-grpc.adapter.js';
import { TenantsGrpcAdapter } from './tenants-grpc.adapter.js';

const tenantId = generateId();

function adapters(service: Record<string, (req: unknown, md: Metadata) => Observable<unknown>>) {
  const client = createMock<ClientGrpc>({ getService: vi.fn().mockReturnValue(service) as never });
  const operations: string[] = [];
  const caller = createMock<IdentityGrpcCaller>({
    call: vi.fn((operation: string, invoke: (md: Metadata) => Observable<unknown>) => {
      operations.push(operation);
      return lastValueFrom(invoke(new Metadata()));
    }) as never,
  });
  const tenants = new TenantsGrpcAdapter(client, caller);
  const members = new MembersGrpcAdapter(client, caller);
  tenants.onModuleInit();
  members.onModuleInit();
  return { tenants, members, operations };
}

describe('tenancy gRPC adapters (gateway)', () => {
  it('name operations after the package and service (G-11) and normalise proto-loader nulls', async () => {
    const { tenants, operations } = adapters({
      listMyTenants: () =>
        of({
          items: [
            {
              tenant: { id: tenantId, slug: 's', name: 'N', personal: true, createdAt: null },
              roles: null,
              lastActiveAt: null,
            },
          ],
        }),
    });
    await expect(tenants.listMyTenants({ userId: generateId() })).resolves.toEqual({
      items: [
        {
          tenant: { id: tenantId, slug: 's', name: 'N', personal: true, createdAt: undefined },
          roles: [],
        },
      ],
    });
    expect(operations).toEqual([
      `${IDENTITY_GRPC_PACKAGE.package}.${TENANTS_SERVICE_NAME}/ListMyTenants`,
    ]);
  });

  it('drops an empty next_cursor and maps Empty to void', async () => {
    const { members } = adapters({
      listMembers: () => of({ items: null, nextCursor: '' }),
      leaveTenant: () => of({}),
    });
    await expect(members.listMembers({ tenantId, limit: 10 })).resolves.toEqual({ items: [] });
    await expect(members.leaveTenant({ tenantId, userId: generateId() })).resolves.toBeUndefined();
  });
});
```

```ts
const call = <T>(observable: Observable<T>) => lastValueFrom(observable);
const callerOf = (userId: string, tenantId: string) =>
  createOutgoingMetadata({ userId, tenantId, roles: ['owner'] });

it('serves ListMyTenants for the calling user', async () => {
  const userId = generateId();
  await expect(
    call(tenantsClient.listMyTenants({ userId }, callerOf(userId, generateId()))),
  ).resolves.toMatchObject({ items: [] });
});

it("PERMISSION_DENIED for another user's tenants: the request user must be the caller", async () => {
  await expect(
    call(
      tenantsClient.listMyTenants({ userId: generateId() }, callerOf(generateId(), generateId())),
    ),
  ).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: status.PERMISSION_DENIED });
});

it('UNAUTHENTICATED without a caller principal (RenameTenant)', async () => {
  await expect(
    call(
      tenantsClient.renameTenant(
        { tenantId: generateId(), actorId: generateId(), name: 'X' },
        new Metadata(),
      ),
    ),
  ).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: status.UNAUTHENTICATED });
});

it('PERMISSION_DENIED TENANT_ACCESS_DENIED when the request tenant is not the caller tenant', async () => {
  const actorId = generateId();
  const error = await call(
    tenantsClient.renameTenant(
      { tenantId: generateId(), actorId, name: 'X' },
      callerOf(actorId, generateId()),
    ),
  ).catch((e: unknown) => e);
  expect(error).toMatchObject({ code: status.PERMISSION_DENIED });
  expect(readErrorTrailers((error as { metadata?: unknown }).metadata).code).toBe(
    'TENANT_ACCESS_DENIED',
  );
});

it('INVALID_ARGUMENT for a malformed invitation', async () => {
  const actorId = generateId();
  const tenantId = generateId();
  await expect(
    call(
      tenantsClient.createInvitation(
        { tenantId, actorId, email: 'not-an-email', roles: ['member'] },
        callerOf(actorId, tenantId),
      ),
    ),
  ).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: status.INVALID_ARGUMENT });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Payload schemas.** Create `presentation/grpc/tenancy-rpc.payloads.ts`:

```ts
import type {
  AcceptInvitationRequest,
  CreateInvitationRequest,
  CreateTenantRequest,
  LeaveTenantRequest,
  ListMembersRequest,
  ListMyTenantsRequest,
  RemoveMemberRequest,
  RenameTenantRequest,
  UpdateMemberRolesRequest,
} from '@domain/identity-contracts';
import { isPlatformTenantId, MAX_CURSOR_LENGTH, TENANT_ROLES } from '@platform/common';
import { z } from 'zod';
import { normalizeEmail } from '../../domain/email.js';
import { IDENTITY_LIMITS } from '../../identity.constants.js';

/* Same limits as the REST DTOs; proto-loader nulls become undefined (see identity-rpc.payloads.ts). */
const id = z.uuid();
const tenantId = z
  .uuid()
  .refine((value) => !isPlatformTenantId(value), 'Must be the UUID of a tenant');
const roles = z.array(z.enum(TENANT_ROLES)).min(1).max(TENANT_ROLES.length);
const name = z.string().trim().min(1).max(IDENTITY_LIMITS.TENANT_NAME_MAX_LENGTH);

export const listMyTenantsRequestSchema = z.object({
  userId: id,
}) satisfies z.ZodType<ListMyTenantsRequest>;
export const createTenantRequestSchema = z.object({
  userId: id,
  name,
}) satisfies z.ZodType<CreateTenantRequest>;
export const renameTenantRequestSchema = z.object({
  tenantId,
  actorId: id,
  name,
}) satisfies z.ZodType<RenameTenantRequest>;
export const listMembersRequestSchema = z.object({
  tenantId,
  limit: z.number().int().min(0).max(100),
  cursor: z
    .string()
    .max(MAX_CURSOR_LENGTH)
    .nullish()
    .transform((cursor) => (cursor === null || cursor === '' ? undefined : cursor)),
}) satisfies z.ZodType<ListMembersRequest, unknown>;
export const updateMemberRolesRequestSchema = z.object({
  tenantId,
  actorId: id,
  userId: id,
  roles,
}) satisfies z.ZodType<UpdateMemberRolesRequest>;
export const removeMemberRequestSchema = z.object({
  tenantId,
  actorId: id,
  userId: id,
}) satisfies z.ZodType<RemoveMemberRequest>;
export const leaveTenantRequestSchema = z.object({
  tenantId,
  userId: id,
}) satisfies z.ZodType<LeaveTenantRequest>;
export const createInvitationRequestSchema = z.object({
  tenantId,
  actorId: id,
  email: z.string().transform(normalizeEmail).pipe(z.email().max(IDENTITY_LIMITS.EMAIL_MAX_LENGTH)),
  roles,
}) satisfies z.ZodType<CreateInvitationRequest, unknown>;
export const acceptInvitationRequestSchema = z.object({
  userId: id,
  token: z.string().regex(/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43}$/),
}) satisfies z.ZodType<AcceptInvitationRequest>;
```

      (If a `satisfies` line does not type-check because of the input type parameter, drop the second type
      argument or the `satisfies` clause on that schema only; the output shape is what matters.)

- [ ] **Step 4: Controller and package spec.** Create `presentation/grpc/tenants-grpc.controller.ts`:

```ts
/** `identity.v1.TenantsService` (identity-service). Authorization: the handlers cross-check the principal. */
@GrpcController()
@TenantsServiceControllerMethods()
export class TenantsGrpcController implements TenantsServiceController {
  constructor(
    private readonly commandBus: CommandBus,
    private readonly queryBus: QueryBus,
  ) {}

  listMyTenants(
    @Payload(new ZodRpcValidationPipe(listMyTenantsRequestSchema)) r: ListMyTenantsRequest,
  ): Promise<MyTenantList> {
    return this.queryBus.execute(new ListMyTenantsQuery(r.userId));
  }

  createTenant(
    @Payload(new ZodRpcValidationPipe(createTenantRequestSchema)) r: CreateTenantRequest,
  ): Promise<MyTenant> {
    return this.commandBus.execute(new CreateTenantCommand(r.userId, r.name));
  }

  renameTenant(
    @Payload(new ZodRpcValidationPipe(renameTenantRequestSchema)) r: RenameTenantRequest,
  ): Promise<Tenant> {
    return this.commandBus.execute(new RenameTenantCommand(r.tenantId, r.actorId, r.name));
  }

  listMembers(
    @Payload(new ZodRpcValidationPipe(listMembersRequestSchema)) r: ListMembersRequest,
  ): Promise<MemberPage> {
    return this.queryBus.execute(new ListMembersQuery(r.tenantId, r.limit, r.cursor));
  }

  updateMemberRoles(
    @Payload(new ZodRpcValidationPipe(updateMemberRolesRequestSchema)) r: UpdateMemberRolesRequest,
  ): Promise<Member> {
    return this.commandBus.execute(
      new UpdateMemberRolesCommand(r.tenantId, r.actorId, r.userId, r.roles),
    );
  }

  /** Resolves `undefined`, which serialises as `google.protobuf.Empty`. */
  removeMember(
    @Payload(new ZodRpcValidationPipe(removeMemberRequestSchema)) r: RemoveMemberRequest,
  ): Promise<void> {
    return this.commandBus.execute(new RemoveMemberCommand(r.tenantId, r.actorId, r.userId));
  }

  leaveTenant(
    @Payload(new ZodRpcValidationPipe(leaveTenantRequestSchema)) r: LeaveTenantRequest,
  ): Promise<void> {
    return this.commandBus.execute(new LeaveTenantCommand(r.tenantId, r.userId));
  }

  createInvitation(
    @Payload(new ZodRpcValidationPipe(createInvitationRequestSchema)) r: CreateInvitationRequest,
  ): Promise<Invitation> {
    return this.commandBus.execute(
      new CreateInvitationCommand(r.tenantId, r.actorId, r.email, r.roles),
    );
  }

  acceptInvitation(
    @Payload(new ZodRpcValidationPipe(acceptInvitationRequestSchema)) r: AcceptInvitationRequest,
  ): Promise<MyTenant> {
    return this.commandBus.execute(new AcceptInvitationCommand(r.userId, r.token));
  }
}
```

      (imports as in `users-grpc.controller.ts`: the contract types and `TenantsServiceControllerMethods` from

      `Empty | Promise<Empty> | Observable<Empty>`, keep `Promise<void>` as `auth-grpc.controller.ts`'s `logout` does.)

- [ ] **Step 5: Gateway adapters.** Create `infrastructure/adapters/grpc/tenancy-grpc.normalizer.ts`:

```ts
import type {
  Invitation,
  Member,
  MemberPage,
  MyTenant,
  MyTenantList,
  Tenant,
} from '@domain/identity-contracts';
import { isEmpty } from 'lodash-es';

/** proto-loader decodes absent message fields as null; return the local adapters' exact shapes. */
type Wire<T> = { [K in keyof T]?: T[K] | null };

export const normalizeTenant = (t: Wire<Tenant>): Tenant => ({
  id: t.id ?? '',
  slug: t.slug ?? '',
  name: t.name ?? '',
  personal: t.personal ?? false,
  createdAt: t.createdAt ?? undefined,
});

export function normalizeMyTenant(m: Wire<MyTenant>): MyTenant {
  return {
    ...(m.tenant ? { tenant: normalizeTenant(m.tenant) } : {}),
    roles: m.roles ?? [],
    ...(m.lastActiveAt ? { lastActiveAt: m.lastActiveAt } : {}),
  };
}

export const normalizeMyTenantList = (l: Wire<MyTenantList>): MyTenantList => ({
  items: (l.items ?? []).map(normalizeMyTenant),
});

export const normalizeMember = (m: Wire<Member>): Member => ({
  userId: m.userId ?? '',
  email: m.email ?? '',
  displayName: m.displayName ?? '',
  roles: m.roles ?? [],
  joinedAt: m.joinedAt ?? undefined,
});

export function normalizeMemberPage(p: Wire<MemberPage>): MemberPage {
  return {
    items: (p.items ?? []).map(normalizeMember),
    ...(isEmpty(p.nextCursor) ? {} : { nextCursor: p.nextCursor ?? undefined }),
  };
}

export const normalizeInvitation = (i: Wire<Invitation>): Invitation => ({
  id: i.id ?? '',
  tenantId: i.tenantId ?? '',
  email: i.email ?? '',
  roles: i.roles ?? [],
  expiresAt: i.expiresAt ?? undefined,
  createdAt: i.createdAt ?? undefined,
});
```

      `tenants-grpc.adapter.ts`:

```ts
const operation = (method: string): string =>
  `${IDENTITY_GRPC_PACKAGE.package}.${TENANTS_SERVICE_NAME}/${method}`;

/** Gateway binding of `TenantsPort`: `identity.v1.TenantsService` over gRPC. */
@Injectable()
export class TenantsGrpcAdapter implements TenantsPort, OnModuleInit {
  private service: TenantsServiceClient;

  constructor(
    @Inject(IDENTITY_GRPC_PACKAGE.clientToken) private readonly client: ClientGrpc,
    private readonly caller: IdentityGrpcCaller,
  ) {}

  onModuleInit(): void {
    this.service = this.client.getService<TenantsServiceClient>(TENANTS_SERVICE_NAME);
  }

  async listMyTenants(input: ListMyTenantsRequest): Promise<MyTenantList> {
    return normalizeMyTenantList(
      await this.caller.call(operation('ListMyTenants'), (md) =>
        this.service.listMyTenants(input, md),
      ),
    );
  }

  async createTenant(input: CreateTenantRequest): Promise<MyTenant> {
    return normalizeMyTenant(
      await this.caller.call(operation('CreateTenant'), (md) =>
        this.service.createTenant(input, md),
      ),
    );
  }

  async renameTenant(input: RenameTenantRequest): Promise<Tenant> {
    return normalizeTenant(
      await this.caller.call(operation('RenameTenant'), (md) =>
        this.service.renameTenant(input, md),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

      `members-grpc.adapter.ts` and `invitations-grpc.adapter.ts`: the same constructor, `onModuleInit` and
      `operation()`, with `listMembers` → `normalizeMemberPage`, `updateMemberRoles` → `normalizeMember`,
      `removeMember` / `leaveTenant` → `await this.caller.call(…); ` (resolve `void`), `createInvitation` →
      `normalizeInvitation`, `acceptInvitation` → `normalizeMyTenant`. `identity-api.module.ts`: move
      `TenantsController`, `MembersController`, `InvitationsController` into the shared `CONTROLLERS` array and
      `TenancyResolver`, `MeTenantResolver` into `PRESENTATION`; `forLocal()` keeps the three local port bindings;
      `forRemote()` gains `{ provide: TenantsPort, useClass: TenantsGrpcAdapter }`, `{ provide: MembersPort, useClass:
      MembersGrpcAdapter }`, `{ provide: InvitationsPort, useClass: InvitationsGrpcAdapter }`; `PORT_EXPORTS` gains the
      three ports. `src/api/index.ts` and `API_VALUES` are unchanged (the ports were exported in Tasks 7-9; G-11
      exports no adapter); root `src/index.ts`: export `TenantsGrpcController` and the payload schemas.

```ts
export interface FakeInvitation { id: string; tenantId: string; email: string; roles: TenantRole[]; token: string; expiresAt: Date; acceptedAt: Date | null }
export class FakeTenancyState { constructor(readonly identity: FakeIdentityState); readonly invitations: FakeInvitation[]; /** The raw token of the last invitation to `email`. */ lastTokenFor(email: string): string | undefined }
export class FakeTenantsPort extends TenantsPort { constructor(state: FakeTenancyState) }
export class FakeMembersPort extends MembersPort { constructor(state: FakeTenancyState) }
export class FakeInvitationsPort extends InvitationsPort { constructor(state: FakeTenancyState) }
// createGatewayE2eApp() binds them and returns `tenancy: FakeTenancyState` as well.
```

The fakes apply the same rules as the real handlers (owner rank, last owner, email match, single use), reading the
actor's roles from `FakeIdentityState` memberships, so the gateway e2e journeys (Task 13) assert real behaviour.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test.** Create `domains/identity/src/testing/fake-tenancy-ports.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { createPrincipal, generateId, runWithPrincipal } from '@platform/common';
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { FakeIdentityState } from './fake-identity-ports.js';
import {
  FakeInvitationsPort,
  FakeMembersPort,
  FakeTenancyState,
  FakeTenantsPort,
} from './fake-tenancy-ports.js';

describe('gateway tenancy fakes', () => {
  let identity: FakeIdentityState;
  let state: FakeTenancyState;
  const ada = generateId();
  const bob = generateId();
  const tenantId = generateId();

  beforeEach(() => {
    identity = new FakeIdentityState();
    for (const [id, email] of [
      [ada, 'ada@example.com'],
      [bob, 'bob@example.com'],
    ] as const) {
      identity.users.set(id, {
        user: {
          id,
          email,
          displayName: email,
          roles: [],
          createdAt: new Date(),
          updatedAt: new Date(),
        },
        password: 'x',
      });
    }
    identity.tenants.set(tenantId, { id: tenantId, name: 'Acme', personal: false });
    identity.addMembership(tenantId, ada, ['owner']);
    state = new FakeTenancyState(identity);
  });

  it('create → list mine', async () => {
    const tenants = new FakeTenantsPort(state);
    const created = await tenants.createTenant({ userId: bob, name: 'Bob Co' });
    expect(created.roles).toEqual(['owner']);
    expect((await tenants.listMyTenants({ userId: bob })).items).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('invite → accept (email must match, single use) → member', async () => {
    const invitations = new FakeInvitationsPort(state);
    await invitations.createInvitation({
      tenantId,
      actorId: ada,
      email: 'bob@example.com',
      roles: ['member'],
    });
    const token = state.lastTokenFor('bob@example.com');
    expect(token).toEqual(expect.any(String));
    await expect(
      invitations.acceptInvitation({ userId: ada, token: token! }),
    ).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'INVITATION_EMAIL_MISMATCH',
    });
    await expect(
      invitations.acceptInvitation({ userId: bob, token: token! }),
    ).resolves.toMatchObject({ roles: ['member'] });
    expect(identity.membership(tenantId, bob)?.roles).toEqual(['member']);
    await expect(
      invitations.acceptInvitation({ userId: bob, token: token! }),
    ).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'INVITATION_INVALID',
    });
  });

  it('a staff switch-in principal acts with its token roles: members yes, owners never', async () => {
    const members = new FakeMembersPort(state);
    const staff = generateId();
    identity.addMembership(tenantId, bob, ['member']);
    await runWithPrincipal(
      createPrincipal({ userId: staff, tenantId, roles: ['admin'], staff: true }),
      async () => {
        await expect(
          members.updateMemberRoles({ tenantId, actorId: staff, userId: bob, roles: ['admin'] }),
        ).resolves.toMatchObject({ roles: ['admin'] });
        await expect(
          members.updateMemberRoles({ tenantId, actorId: staff, userId: ada, roles: ['member'] }),
        ).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'OWNER_RANK_REQUIRED' });
      },
    );
  });

  it('protects the last owner', async () => {
    const members = new FakeMembersPort(state);
    await expect(
      members.updateMemberRoles({ tenantId, actorId: ada, userId: ada, roles: ['admin'] }),
    ).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'LAST_OWNER' });
    await expect(members.leaveTenant({ tenantId, userId: ada })).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'LAST_OWNER',
    });
  });
});
```

```ts
import type {
  AcceptInvitationRequest,
  CreateInvitationRequest,
  CreateTenantRequest,
  Invitation,
  LeaveTenantRequest,
  ListMembersRequest,
  ListMyTenantsRequest,
  Member,
  MemberPage,
  MyTenant,
  MyTenantList,
  RemoveMemberRequest,
  RenameTenantRequest,
  Tenant,
  UpdateMemberRolesRequest,
} from '@domain/identity-contracts';
import {
  EntityNotFoundException,
  generateId,
  getPrincipal,
  PermissionDeniedException,
  satisfiesTenantRole,
  type TenantRole,
} from '@platform/common';
import {
  assertCanAssignRoles,
  assertCanRemove,
  generateInvitationToken,
  INVITATION_TTL_MS,
  InvitationEmailMismatchException,
  InvitationInvalidException,
  LastOwnerException,
  MembershipAlreadyExistsException,
  parseTenantRoles,
  TenantAccessDeniedException,
} from '../index.js';
import { InvitationsPort, MembersPort, TenantsPort } from '../api/index.js';
import type { FakeIdentityState } from './fake-identity-ports.js';

export interface FakeInvitation {
  id: string;
  tenantId: string;
  email: string;
  roles: TenantRole[];
  token: string;
  expiresAt: Date;
  acceptedAt: Date | null;
}

/** Tenancy state of the fake identity-service, on top of `FakeIdentityState` (users, tenants, memberships). */
export class FakeTenancyState {
  readonly invitations: FakeInvitation[] = [];

  constructor(readonly identity: FakeIdentityState) {}

  lastTokenFor(email: string): string | undefined {
    return this.invitations.filter((i) => i.email === email).at(-1)?.token;
  }

  tenant(id: string): Tenant {
    const tenant = this.identity.tenants.get(id);
    if (!tenant) throw new EntityNotFoundException('Tenant', id);
    return {
      id,
      slug: `${tenant.name.toLowerCase().replaceAll(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-')}-${id.slice(-8)}`,
      name: tenant.name,
      personal: tenant.personal,
    };
  }

  myTenant(tenantId: string, userId: string): MyTenant {
    const membership = this.identity.membership(tenantId, userId);
    if (!membership) throw new TenantAccessDeniedException();
    return { tenant: this.tenant(tenantId), roles: [...membership.roles] };
  }

  rolesOf(tenantId: string, userId: string): TenantRole[] {
    const membership = this.identity.membership(tenantId, userId);
    if (!membership) throw new TenantAccessDeniedException();
    return [...membership.roles];
  }

  /**
   * The acting user's roles, as `actorTenantRoles` computes them: the membership row, or the token
   * roles of a staff switch-in principal (no membership). The gateway's JwtAuthGuard put that
   * principal in CLS for this request.
   */
  actorRoles(tenantId: string, actorId: string): TenantRole[] {
    const principal = getPrincipal();
    if (
      principal?.staff === true &&
      principal.userId === actorId &&
      principal.tenantId === tenantId
    ) {
      return [...principal.roles];
    }
    return this.rolesOf(tenantId, actorId);
  }

  owners(tenantId: string): number {
    return this.identity.memberships.filter(
      (m) => m.tenantId === tenantId && m.roles.includes('owner'),
    ).length;
  }

  member(tenantId: string, userId: string): Member {
    const entry = this.identity.users.get(userId);
    const membership = this.identity.membership(tenantId, userId);
    if (!entry || !membership) throw new EntityNotFoundException('Member', userId);
    return {
      userId,
      email: entry.user.email,
      displayName: entry.user.displayName,
      roles: [...membership.roles],
    };
  }
}

export class FakeTenantsPort extends TenantsPort {
  constructor(private readonly state: FakeTenancyState) {
    super();
  }

  async listMyTenants({ userId }: ListMyTenantsRequest): Promise<MyTenantList> {
    return {
      items: this.state.identity
        .membershipsOf(userId)
        .map((m) => this.state.myTenant(m.tenantId, userId)),
    };
  }

  async createTenant({ userId, name }: CreateTenantRequest): Promise<MyTenant> {
    const id = generateId();
    this.state.identity.tenants.set(id, { id, name: name.trim(), personal: false });
    this.state.identity.addMembership(id, userId, ['owner']);
    return this.state.myTenant(id, userId);
  }

  async renameTenant({ tenantId, actorId, name }: RenameTenantRequest): Promise<Tenant> {
    if (!satisfiesTenantRole(this.state.actorRoles(tenantId, actorId), 'owner')) {
      // Same error as RenameTenantHandler.
      throw new PermissionDeniedException('Only an owner can rename the workspace', {
        details: { required: ['tenant:manage'] },
      });
    }
    const tenant = this.state.identity.tenants.get(tenantId);
    if (tenant) tenant.name = name.trim();
    return this.state.tenant(tenantId);
  }
}

export class FakeMembersPort extends MembersPort {
  constructor(private readonly state: FakeTenancyState) {
    super();
  }

  async listMembers({ tenantId }: ListMembersRequest): Promise<MemberPage> {
    const ids = this.state.identity.memberships
      .filter((m) => m.tenantId === tenantId)
      .map((m) => m.userId);
    return {
      items: ids
        .sort()
        .reverse()
        .map((userId) => this.state.member(tenantId, userId)),
    };
  }

  async updateMemberRoles({
    tenantId,
    actorId,
    userId,
    roles,
  }: UpdateMemberRolesRequest): Promise<Member> {
    const next = parseTenantRoles(roles);
    const current = this.state.rolesOf(tenantId, userId);
    assertCanAssignRoles(this.state.actorRoles(tenantId, actorId), current, next);
    if (current.includes('owner') && !next.includes('owner') && this.state.owners(tenantId) <= 1) {
      throw new LastOwnerException();
    }
    this.state.identity.setMembershipRoles(tenantId, userId, next);
    return this.state.member(tenantId, userId);
  }

  async removeMember({ tenantId, actorId, userId }: RemoveMemberRequest): Promise<void> {
    const target = this.state.rolesOf(tenantId, userId);
    assertCanRemove(this.state.actorRoles(tenantId, actorId), target);
    if (target.includes('owner') && this.state.owners(tenantId) <= 1)
      throw new LastOwnerException();
    this.state.identity.removeMembership(tenantId, userId);
  }

  async leaveTenant({ tenantId, userId }: LeaveTenantRequest): Promise<void> {
    if (
      this.state.rolesOf(tenantId, userId).includes('owner') &&
      this.state.owners(tenantId) <= 1
    ) {
      throw new LastOwnerException();
    }
    this.state.identity.removeMembership(tenantId, userId);
  }
}

export class FakeInvitationsPort extends InvitationsPort {
  constructor(private readonly state: FakeTenancyState) {
    super();
  }

  async createInvitation({
    tenantId,
    actorId,
    email,
    roles,
  }: CreateInvitationRequest): Promise<Invitation> {
    const invited = parseTenantRoles(roles);
    assertCanAssignRoles(this.state.actorRoles(tenantId, actorId), null, invited);
    const invitation: FakeInvitation = {
      id: generateId(),
      tenantId,
      email: email.trim().toLowerCase(),
      roles: invited,
      token: generateInvitationToken().token,
      expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + INVITATION_TTL_MS),
      acceptedAt: null,
    };
    this.state.invitations.push(invitation);
    const { token: _token, acceptedAt: _accepted, ...view } = invitation;
    return { ...view, roles: [...invited] };
  }

  async acceptInvitation({ userId, token }: AcceptInvitationRequest): Promise<MyTenant> {
    const invitation = this.state.invitations.find((i) => i.token === token);
    if (!invitation || invitation.acceptedAt !== null || invitation.expiresAt <= new Date()) {
      throw new InvitationInvalidException();
    }
    if (this.state.identity.users.get(userId)?.user.email !== invitation.email) {
      throw new InvitationEmailMismatchException();
    }
    if (this.state.identity.membership(invitation.tenantId, userId))
      throw new MembershipAlreadyExistsException();
    invitation.acceptedAt = new Date();
    this.state.identity.addMembership(invitation.tenantId, userId, invitation.roles);
    return this.state.myTenant(invitation.tenantId, userId);
  }
}
```

### Task 13: Tenancy e2e in both topologies (B2-28)

**Files:**

- Create: `apps/monolith/test/tenancy-journeys.e2e-spec.ts`, `apps/gateway/test/tenancy-journeys.e2e-spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: everything above plus core's `POST /v1/auth/switch-tenant` (Task 14), `POST
/v1/staff/tenants/:tenantId/switch-in` (Task 15), the monolith e2e app (`identity` in-memory persistence,
  `mailQueue`) and the gateway e2e app (`identity: FakeIdentityState`, `tenancy: FakeTenancyState`, Task 12).
- Produces: the four journeys of spec §13 pinned in both topologies: register → personal tenant; invite → accept →
  switch; staff switch-in with its audit trail; last-owner protection. The topology smoke journey (register → me)
  stays as E owns it.

**Wave:** Lane J, first task. Starts when Lanes B, P and I have merged into `feat/domains-tenancy`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the monolith journeys.** Create `apps/monolith/test/tenancy-journeys.e2e-spec.ts`:

```ts
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { createMonolithE2eApp, type MonolithE2e } from './support/monolith-e2e-app.js';

await vi.hoisted(async () => {
  const { MONOLITH_E2E_ENV } = await import('./support/e2e-env.js');
  Object.assign(process.env, MONOLITH_E2E_ENV);
});

interface Tokens {
  accessToken: string;
  refreshToken: string;
  tenantId: string;
  tenantRoles: string[];
  user: { id: string; email: string };
}
const PASSWORD = 'correct horse battery staple';

describe('tenancy journeys (monolith, B2-28)', () => {
  let e2e: MonolithE2e;
  const auth = (token: string) => ({ authorization: `Bearer ${token}` });
  const register = async (email: string): Promise<Tokens> => {
    const res = await e2e.app.inject({
      method: 'POST',
      url: '/v1/auth/register',
      payload: { email, password: PASSWORD, displayName: email.split('@')[0] },
    });
    expect(res.statusCode, res.body).toBe(201);
    return res.json<Tokens>();
  };
  /** The raw token of the last invitation mail queued for `email`. */
  const mailedToken = (email: string): string => {
    // The recording queue types job data as `unknown`.
    type InvitationJob = { to?: string; context?: { token?: unknown } };
    const job = [...e2e.mailQueue.jobs]
      .reverse()
      .find((j) => j.name === 'tenant-invitation' && (j.data as InvitationJob).to === email);
    return String((job?.data as InvitationJob | undefined)?.context?.token);
  };

  beforeAll(async () => {
    e2e = await createMonolithE2eApp();
  });

  afterAll(async () => {
    await e2e?.app.close();
  });

  it('register creates a personal workspace the token acts in', async () => {
    const ada = await register('ada.journey@example.com');
    expect(ada.tenantRoles).toEqual(['owner']);
    const tenants = await e2e.app.inject({
      method: 'GET',
      url: '/v1/tenants',
      headers: auth(ada.accessToken),
    });
    expect(tenants.json()).toEqual([
      expect.objectContaining({ id: ada.tenantId, personal: true, roles: ['owner'] }),
    ]);
  });

  it('invite → accept → switch: the invitee works in the inviting tenant', async () => {
    const ada = await register('ada.invite@example.com');
    const invite = await e2e.app.inject({
      method: 'POST',
      url: '/v1/tenant/invitations',
      headers: auth(ada.accessToken),
      payload: { email: 'bob.invite@example.com', roles: ['member'] },
    });
    expect(invite.statusCode, invite.body).toBe(201);

    const bob = await register('bob.invite@example.com');
    const accepted = await e2e.app.inject({
      method: 'POST',
      url: '/v1/invitations/accept',
      headers: auth(bob.accessToken),
      payload: { token: mailedToken('bob.invite@example.com') },
    });
    expect(accepted.statusCode, accepted.body).toBe(200);

    const switched = await e2e.app.inject({
      method: 'POST',
      url: '/v1/auth/switch-tenant',
      headers: auth(bob.accessToken),
      payload: { tenantId: ada.tenantId, refreshToken: bob.refreshToken },
    });
    expect(switched.statusCode, switched.body).toBe(200);
    const inAda = switched.json<Tokens>();
    expect(inAda).toMatchObject({ tenantId: ada.tenantId, tenantRoles: ['member'] });

    const members = await e2e.app.inject({
      method: 'GET',
      url: '/v1/tenant/members',
      headers: auth(inAda.accessToken),
    });
    expect(
      members
        .json<{ items: { email: string }[] }>()
        .items.map((m) => m.email)
        .sort(),
    ).toEqual(['ada.invite@example.com', 'bob.invite@example.com']);
    const forbidden = await e2e.app.inject({
      method: 'POST',
      url: '/v1/tenant/invitations',
      headers: auth(inAda.accessToken),
      payload: { email: 'eve@example.com', roles: ['member'] },
    });
    expect(forbidden.statusCode).toBe(403);
  });

  it('staff switch-in is audited in the target tenant and cannot touch owners', async () => {
    const ada = await register('ada.staff@example.com');
    const carol = await register('carol.staff@example.com');
    const stored = e2e.identity.store.users.get(carol.user.id);
    if (stored === undefined) throw new Error('carol is not stored');
    e2e.identity.store.users.set(carol.user.id, { ...stored, isPlatformStaff: true });

    const switchIn = await e2e.app.inject({
      method: 'POST',
      url: `/v1/staff/tenants/${ada.tenantId}/switch-in`,
      headers: auth(carol.accessToken),
      payload: { reason: 'Support ticket #42' },
    });
    expect(switchIn.statusCode, switchIn.body).toBe(201);
    const staff = switchIn.json<{ accessToken: string; auditEventId: string }>();
    expect(e2e.identity.store.auditEvents).toContainEqual(
      expect.objectContaining({
        id: staff.auditEventId,
        tenantId: ada.tenantId,
        type: 'staff.switch_in',
      }),
    );

    const list = await e2e.app.inject({
      method: 'GET',
      url: '/v1/tenant/members',
      headers: auth(staff.accessToken),
    });
    expect(list.statusCode).toBe(200);
    const demote = await e2e.app.inject({
      method: 'PATCH',
      url: `/v1/tenant/members/${ada.user.id}`,
      headers: auth(staff.accessToken),
      payload: { roles: ['member'] },
    });
    expect(demote.statusCode).toBe(403);
    expect(demote.json()).toMatchObject({ code: 'OWNER_RANK_REQUIRED' });
  });

  it('the last owner can neither leave nor step down', async () => {
    const ada = await register('ada.owner@example.com');
    const leave = await e2e.app.inject({
      method: 'POST',
      url: '/v1/tenant/leave',
      headers: auth(ada.accessToken),
    });
    expect(leave.statusCode).toBe(422);
    expect(leave.json()).toMatchObject({ code: 'LAST_OWNER' });
    const stepDown = await e2e.app.inject({
      method: 'PATCH',
      url: `/v1/tenant/members/${ada.user.id}`,
      headers: auth(ada.accessToken),
      payload: { roles: ['admin'] },
    });
    expect(stepDown.json()).toMatchObject({ code: 'LAST_OWNER' });
  });
});
```

```ts
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

const root = join(import.meta.dirname, '..');
const api = readFileSync(join(root, 'docs/API.md'), 'utf8');
const codesIn = (file: string, object: string): string[] => {
  const source = readFileSync(join(root, file), 'utf8');
  const block =
    new RegExp(`export const ${object} = \\{([\\s\\S]*?)\\} as const`).exec(source)?.[1] ?? '';
  return [...block.matchAll(/:\s*'([A-Z_]+)'/g)].map((m) => m[1] ?? '');
};

describe('docs/API.md error catalogue (F23)', () => {
  it.each([
    ['domains/identity/src/identity.constants.ts', 'IdentityErrorCode'],
    ['domains/billing/src/billing.constants.ts', 'BillingErrorCode'],
  ])('lists every code of %s', (file, object) => {
    const missing = codesIn(file, object).filter((code) => !api.includes(`\`${code}\``));
    expect(missing).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('lists the storage codes and the 408 mapping', () => {
    for (const code of ['STORAGE_ERROR', 'INVALID_STORAGE_KEY'])
      expect(api).toContain(`\`${code}\``);
    expect(api).toMatch(/408[^\n]*TIMEOUT|TIMEOUT[^\n]*408/);
  });
});
```

      envelopes carry `tenantId`/`actorId` and consumers act as the actor; a "Tenant scoping" subsection (billing rows
      and webhooks are scoped by the tenant signed into Stripe metadata; requests whose tenant differs from the
      principal's are `TENANT_MISMATCH`); an "Invitations" subsection (32 random bytes, sha256 at rest, 7-day TTL,
      single use, email must match, the raw token only in the mail job (deleted once sent or failed:
      `INVITATION_MAIL_RETENTION`) and the mail, never Kafka, logs or the database;
      `MAIL_APP_URL` must be https in production); the owner-rank and last-owner rules. `docs/CONFIGURATION.md`: the mail section notes that identity-service now loads the `mail` namespace
      (enqueue-only: production needs `SMTP_HOST`, `SMTP_PORT`, `MAIL_FROM` there too) and gains the mail
      table row `MAIL_APP_URL` (optional; public web app URL for mail links; https in production unless
      `SMTP_ALLOW_INSECURE`; compose default `http://localhost:3000`). `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`: the "Request principal"
      section lists the writers (I-2) including gRPC (B2-23), Kafka (B2-24), the Stripe webhook (signed metadata) and
      invitation acceptance; DataLoader batches keep the request context (B2-25 spec). `docs/TESTING.md`: toolbox rows
      `startBillingDatabase` (`domains/billing/test/billing-database.ts`), `FakeTenancyState` / `FakeTenantsPort` /
      `FakeMembersPort` / `FakeInvitationsPort` (`@domain/identity/testing`); int-spec rows `billing:int`
      (`billing.repositories.int-spec.ts`, local execution off) and the identity members/invitations blocks.
      `README.md`: the endpoint/permission matrix gains the tenancy rows (`tenant:manage`, `members:manage`) and the
      `:63` test count is updated from `bun run test` output ("<files> test files / <tests> tests at the time of
      writing"). `domains/identity/README.md`: a "Tenancy" section (tenants, members, invitations, ports and adapters,
      rules, error codes). `domains/files/README.md:23` (F29): `files.errors.ts (FilesErrorCode + 4 DomainExceptions)`
      → `files.errors.ts (FilesErrorCode + 5 DomainExceptions)` (`FileAccessDeniedException`, `FileNotFoundException`,
      `FileTooLargeException`, `UnsupportedFileTypeException`, `UploadCapacityExceededException`).

- [ ] **Step 5: Run.** `npx vitest run --project scripts` → PASS; `node scripts/check-env-example.mjs` → exit 0.

- [ ] **Step 6: Gates.** `npx biome check --write scripts/docs-catalogue.spec.ts`, `npx prettier --write docs README.md
domains/identity/README.md domains/files/README.md`, `bun run check` → exit 0.

- [ ] **Step 7: Commit.**

```bash
git add docs README.md domains/identity/README.md domains/files/README.md scripts/docs-catalogue.spec.ts
printf '%s\n' 'docs(repo): document B2 tenancy, propagation and the full error catalogue' '' \
  'API.md gains the tenancy endpoints and every domain code (F23, pinned by docs-catalogue.spec.ts);' \
  'SECURITY.md the gRPC principal trust, tenant scoping and invitation tokens; CONFIGURATION.md' \
  'MAIL_APP_URL; ARCHITECTURE.md and TESTING.md the new writers and helpers; files README count (F29).' \
  'B2-29.' > "$TMPDIR/b2e-msg.txt"
bunx commitlint < "$TMPDIR/b2e-msg.txt"
git commit -S -F "$TMPDIR/b2e-msg.txt"
```
