## Self-review

- **Scope.** Every inventory item of the scope (B2-6, 12, 13, 14, 16, 17, 23, 24, 25, 26, 27, 28, 29) and the four
  audit items (F23, F27, F29, F34) map to a task above.
- **Names and signatures across tasks.** `requireTenantActor(tenantId, actorId)` and `actorTenantRoles(principal,
memberships)` (Task 7) are used unchanged by Tasks 8-9; `parseTenantRoles`, `assertCanAssignRoles`,
  `assertCanRemove` (Task 8) by Tasks 9 and 12; the ports `TenantsPort`, `MembersPort`, `InvitationsPort` (Tasks 7-9)
  by Tasks 10-12; the contract messages `MyTenant`, `MyTenantList`, `Member`, `MemberPage`, `Invitation`, `Tenant`
  (Task 6) everywhere after; `principalPermissions(registry, principal)` (Task 3) in Task 2;
  `defineEventContract(…, { tenantScoped })` and `KafkaPublishOptions.tenantId/actorId` (Task 4) in Task 2;
  `stripeOwnerOf` (Task 1) in Task 2; `startBillingDatabase` (Task 1) only in billing int specs.
- **Contract alignment.** No core symbol is redefined. Core I-9's surfaces (switch, staff switch-in/out, login
  `tenantId`) are consumed, not re-added; new proto messages avoid core's names (`MyTenant`, not
  `TenantMembership`). Re-read of the core Interfaces on 2026-11-10 (after core review part 1): no `(changed …)`
  marker, "Interfaces I-1..I-12: unchanged"; core review part 2 (Tasks 10-19) had not run.
- **Decisions taken here** (see the structured result): pre-B2 billing rows move to the platform tenant; billing
  threads `tenant_id` explicitly and cross-checks the principal; invitation mail is enqueued with the template in
  `@domain/identity-contracts`; `MAIL_APP_URL` optional, https in production; actor roles come from the membership
  row (token roles only for staff); `me { activeTenant }` instead of a new `me` type; `PATCH /v1/tenant` uses
  `tenant:manage` (no `@Roles()` route in B2); `PaymentSucceededEvent` gains a trailing `tenantId` defaulting to the
  platform tenant (after L-7's optional parameter).
- **Known follow-ups owned elsewhere.** `stripeEvents.deleteProcessedBefore`'s LIMIT subquery is not pushdownable
  on a distributed table (SH-6 or B3-11 rewrites it); `GetUsersByIds` stays unscoped until B3's RLS (core Task 12;
  Task 5 proves the batch runs as the request principal); S-4's "count requests without an Idempotency-Key" is not
  in B2.

## Plan review (2026-11-10)

Adversarial review of this plan against the code on `feat/domains-tenancy` (f5e5c61: E Tasks 1-2, C wave 1),
lane/g11 (G-11 Tasks 1-3), lane/s0 (S0 Task 1), the ledger wave-0 plan and core's Interfaces I-1..I-12. Defects are
fixed in place above; each finding below names the task, the evidence and the fix.

### Executive summary (review)

- Verdict: executable after the fixes below; no blocker, no user decision. Core I-1..I-12 re-read after core review
  part 1 (no `(changed …)` marker, "Interfaces I-1..I-12: unchanged"); every use here matches them.
- Security fixes: (1) `callerContextFromCls` paired an override `userId` with the CLS principal's tenant, roles and
  staff flag, i.e. it could assert another user's principal; it now drops them for another user (Task 3, tests);
  (2) identity-service trusted the request `user_id` of `ListMyTenants`/`CreateTenant`: new `requireSelf(userId)`
  cross-check (Task 7, Task 11 gRPC test); (3) member handlers read the actor's roles before the per-tenant lock,
  so an owner demoted concurrently kept acting with owner rank: roles are now read after `countOwnersLocked`
  (Task 8); (4) `ListMembers` now requires a live membership (or staff), not just a token naming the tenant;
  (5) controller ruling: tenant-invitation jobs carry the raw token, so `MailService.enqueue` gains an optional
  `MailJobRetention` and the invitation mailer passes `{ removeOnComplete: true, removeOnFail: true }` (Task 9).
- Executability fixes (each would have failed a step): S0's `billing.migrations.int-spec.ts` pins the 4 enum labels,
  the old index list and a raw insert without `tenant_id` (Task 1 updates it); drizzle 0.45 wraps driver errors
  (`DrizzleQueryError`), so the NOT NULL assertion checks `cause.code === '23502'`; the existing webhook specs'
  sessions had no tenant metadata and would resolve to the platform tenant (Task 1 adds `signedMetadata()`);
  `drizzle-payments.repository.spec.ts` gets exact SQL/params; `billing.modules.spec.ts` and
  `notifications.modules.spec.ts` need `RbacModule.forRoot()` once the Grpc modules import `forFeature`, and a
  principal once listing is scoped (Tasks 1-3); `transport-context.spec.ts` and `grpc-roundtrip.spec.ts` send
  non-UUID `x-user-id` values that now fail closed (Task 3); `InMemoryUsersRepository.insert` takes a
  `UserAggregate`, not a plain object (Tasks 8-9); the recording mail queue types job data as `unknown` (Tasks 9,
  13); the gateway fakes ignored staff switch-in roles, so the gateway journey "staff cannot touch owners" would
  have got 403 `TENANT_ACCESS_DENIED` instead of `OWNER_RANK_REQUIRED` (Task 12 `actorRoles`); identity-service e2e
  specs import `@nestjs/bullmq` (devDependency added); the buf fallback `.git#branch=` cannot read a worktree
  (replaced by core's verified `git archive` form); three commit body lines were 101 characters; the relay spec asserts the
  envelope and the billing-service e2e uses `billing`/`call`/`USER_ID` (Tasks 1-2); the gateway journey uses core's
  `grantStaff`/`auditEvents`.
- Consistency fixes: `TenantMismatchException` follows billing's `super(msg, { code })` pattern; `activeTenant`
  roles come from the membership row (token roles only for staff); no resolver or gRPC adapter is added to
  `@domain/identity/api` (G-11 exports neither `UsersResolver` nor `AuthGrpcAdapter`); the fake `renameTenant`
  throws the real `FORBIDDEN`; compose `MAIL_APP_URL` goes in `x-env-base` (identity-service has no `*env-mail`);
  production identity-service now needs mail env (documented); doc line hints refreshed (`docs/API.md:725/768`).
- Verified unchanged: billing proto field numbers (8, 4, 11 are free); `drizzle-kit check` compares snapshot chains
  only, so the hand-edited `0001.sql` passes `db:check` while its snapshot stays generated; zod 4 `z.uuid()`
  accepts the nil UUID; `pg_advisory_xact_lock` + READ COMMITTED (the runner sets no isolation) serialises owner
  changes and survives PgBouncer transaction pooling; ledger L-8a's `PLATFORM_TENANT_ID` line matches the re-export
  step verbatim; L-7's handler edits do not collide with Steps 7-8; the vocabulary spec scans libs test files only.

### Contract check (core I-1..I-12)

- Names and signatures used here match I-1 (`createPrincipal`, `isPrincipal`, `getPrincipal`, `requirePrincipal`,
  `runWithPrincipal`, `CLS_PRINCIPAL`, `PLATFORM_TENANT_ID`, `isPlatformTenantId`, `TENANT_ROLES`,
  `isTenantRole`, `normalizeTenantRoles`, `satisfiesTenantRole`), I-3 (`AuthUser.tenantId/roles/staff`,
  `TEST_TENANT_ID`), I-4 (`RbacModule.forRoot/forFeature`, `RbacRegistry.resolve`, `hasPermissions`,
  `createTestRbacRegistry`), I-5 (`BillingPermission.Checkout/ReadAll`, `IdentityPermission.MembersManage/
TenantManage`; read-all re-granted to `admin` in Task 2 as I-5 requires), I-7 (repository methods; the edge
  additions `updateRoles`, `delete`, `countOwnersLocked`, `listMembers` keep I-7's signatures and add
  `findMember`), I-8 (`TenantAuditLog.record`), I-10 (codes) and I-11 (placement).
- Nothing of I-9 is re-added: `TenantsService` and its messages are new names (`MyTenant`, not `TenantMembership`);
  `SwitchTenant`, staff switch-in/out and login `tenant_id` are consumed only.
- Contract additions by this review (all edge-owned): `requireSelf(userId): Principal` (identity root);
  `type MailJobRetention` and `MailService.enqueue(mail, retention?)` (`@platform/mailer`);
  `INVITATION_MAIL_RETENTION` (identity root); `FakeTenancyState.actorRoles(tenantId, actorId)`
  (`@domain/identity/testing`); `callerContextFromCls` semantics (another user's override drops the principal).
- FakeIdentityState names this plan depends on (core Tasks 8, 10): `users` (`Map<id, { user, password }>`),
  `tenants` (`Map<id, { id, name, personal }>`), `memberships` (array), `membership()`, `membershipsOf()`,
  `addMembership()`, `removeMembership()`, `setMembershipRoles()`, and core Task 15's `grantStaff(userId)` and
  `auditEvents` (gateway journey). If core review part 2 renames any of them, Tasks 12-13 must follow.

### Notes for SH-6

- `billing.stripeEvents.deleteProcessedBefore` deletes with `DELETE … WHERE id IN (SELECT … LIMIT n)`. Once SH-6
  distributes `billing.stripe_events` by `tenant_id`, Citus cannot push a `LIMIT` subquery down to the shards
  (the subquery is not co-located on the distribution column), so SH-6 (or B3-11, when it moves the sweep into a
  system repository) must rewrite it, e.g. as a per-shard batched delete or a `tenant_id`-grouped loop.

### Residual risks

- Not executed here (Docker and the core lanes do not exist yet): the Citus behaviour of `ALTER TYPE … ADD VALUE`
  inside the migrator's transaction is expected to work (PG ≥ 12 allows it; single-node Citus has no worker to
  propagate to) and is exercised by `billing:int`. Splitting it into another migration would not help:
  `run-migrations.ts:160` runs a set's pending migrations in one transaction. If Citus refuses it, replace the three
  statements by hand (snapshot unchanged) with `ALTER TYPE "billing"."payment_status" RENAME TO
"payment_status_old"`, `CREATE TYPE "billing"."payment_status" AS ENUM(<the 7 values>)`, `ALTER TABLE
"billing"."payments" ALTER COLUMN "status" DROP DEFAULT`, `… ALTER COLUMN "status" TYPE "billing"."payment_status"
USING "status"::text::"billing"."payment_status"`, `… SET DEFAULT 'pending'`, `DROP TYPE
"billing"."payment_status_old"`, and adjust the unit spec's `ADD VALUE` assertion; never edit after commit.
- drizzle-kit's exact output for `0001` was not generated in this review; Step 4 already says to stop when the
  generated file has a statement the hand-edited list lacks.
- Owner removal outside the lock: deleting a user cascades its memberships (FK `on delete cascade`) without
  `countOwnersLocked`. No B2 path deletes users; whoever adds account deletion must take the lock per tenant.
- Gateway fakes do not model `requireSelf` or staff acceptance; those rules are pinned by the identity unit specs
  and the identity-service e2e, not by the gateway journeys.
